              _*WANI JINKIRI...*_

 *Written by* ✍🏻
 
         *Sa'adat Alkali D/tsafe*

*🌈KAINUWA WRITES ASSOCIATION*🤝🏻📚📚

      _*Da sunan Allah mai rahama mai jinkai*_

*```Dikkan godiya ta tabbata ga Allah (S. W. A) da yanuna man wannan rana, masoya littafe na gani nasake dawo muku da wani sabun littafin wanda bashi kuke tsimaye ba, ina mai baku haquri narashin ganin cigaban littafina da bansamu damar yin cigaban sa ba, wanda yake da suna KASAITACCE hakan yafaru ne sakamakon wasu 'yan dalilai amma karku damu zanci Gaba dashi insha Allah nagode*```


🍃🍃 _*Dedicated-*

  _*Nasadaukar da wannan page nawa gaduk wata Mace dake cikin duniyar nan, wacce take da sunan Aysha, ya Allah yakara daukaka wannan sunan aduniya, da dik masu sunan gaba daya ameen...*_😘 🌹🌹


            *(1)*

Kwance take kan dan wani qaramin gado tayi nisa sosai cikin tunanin kadarar ta da Allah ya qadarto mata cikin rayuwar ta, 

Yanzu Allah kadai yasan erin samarin da take samu suna tafiyar su wasu ma da zaran anfara musu zancin suturu magabatan su suke tafiyar su, wace erin rayuwace wannan? dika sa'annin ta sunyi aure wasu ma Harsun Haihu amma eta shiru kakeji.

tunawa tayi da wata rana har gore wata maqociyar su da sukayi fada da ita tamata wanda ranar kwana tayi tana kuka har kukan da tayi ya haddasar mata da ciyon kai, 

tadade tsaye a kanta tana karantar ta kokadan batasun ganin 'yar uwar ta cikin wannan hali na quncin da take ciki, 

jitayi ana share mata hawayin ta dake zubowa daga idanun ta, wanda ita kanta batasan iya adadin wanda ta zubar ba, ko ba'a fada mata ba tasan qanwar tace Dijah, lumshi idanun ta tayi a hankali kafin tayi qarfin halin miqewa  zaune tana duban 'yar uwar ta. 

cikin qarfin Hali ta qaqalo murmushin dole tace sister yau she kika shigo dakin nan har bansani ba?
 
tafada tana tsare Dijah da kyakyawan idanun ta tare da murmushi dauke a fuskarta wanda saida gefin kuncinta yalotsa. 


inako zakisan nashigo Adda?Dija tafada tana mai kallon ta,  kafin taci gaba da cewa "bayan kinyi nisa cikin tunanin da kika sabayi a kullum wanda kokadan bai da wani amfani agareki, sai kinje kinjefa kanki cikin wani hali da mu kanmu baki daya, Haba adda kiyi tawakali mana komai fa na duniya mai wucewa ne wllh wataran sai labari batun yau ba nake cemiki *WANI JINKIRI* fa Alkhaire ne amma kinqi yarda da hakan sai kice ke maganganun mutane ke damunki toh shi zaisa Allah ya canza miki jarabawar daya rubutu akanki kome?

kosu mutanin su zasu kawo miki mijin?

bari kiji ingaya miki adda indai kikace zaki biye tamutane  toh ko zaki kashe kanki a banza wllh,

yanzu wake ta mutane bakayi ba ma ance kayi bare to ka aikata, ay qiris kawai suke jira asaka abakin duniya, 

ni abinda nakeso gareki yanzu "kifidda komai aranki kidibi abunda kafishsheki, kidage da addu'a wanda nasan kinayi muma muna iyakar qoqarin mu gurin roqon Allah, fatanmu kawai dai Allah ya za'ba miki miji nagari shine kawai fatan mu.


shiru tayi tana nazarin dik maganganun 'yar uwar ta, tabbas tasan dik abunda tafada Gaskiya ne kuma tayi alkawarin yanzu zata cire komai aranta, zata kuma toshe kunnen ta daga saurarin dik zantukan mutane na banza, zata koma yin rayuwar ta tamkar ta da insha Allah. 


kallon Dija tayi cikin kulawa da so tace haqiqa dikkan maganganunki Gaskiya ne kuma insha Allah yau komai yazo qarshe bazan qara damar da kaina ba bare na zubda kwallah har nabata muka rai keda umma ba, tabbas dik abunda kaga Allah yayi akanka shine dai dai mai yuwa idan nayi aure da wure ba Alkairi bane gareni shiyasa hakan ta kasance, tohhh Allah dai yasa mudace. 

Ameen Adda *SAFFEAH* abunda nakeson kigane kenan"wllh namatuqar jin dadi da jin wa'annan kalaman da suka fito daga bakin ki, kuma nasan matuqar kikayi hakan Umman mu ba qaramin dadi zataji ba,

Yanzu bari natashi naje naqarasa aykin da nake kafin Umma tadawo.

Au..daman wai umma tafita ne? 

Safeena ta tambaya, ehh tafita tindazu Dija tabata amsa.

Allah yamai data lapiya, mekike dafa manane yau? 

Wllh Alalen gwangwani ce nake da mai da yaji kinsan fa yau juma'ah


sosai safeena tayi murmushi harsai da kyakyawan haqoranta masu matuqar haske suka bayyana cike da jin dadi tace kai amma wllh sister naji dadin wannan alalen da akayi yau ina matuqar son ta Allah, 

Itama Dijah cikin dariya tace sanin hakan da nariga da nayine, yasa nazage dantse nashirya ta, yanda zakiji dadin ta sosai. 

Rugume junasu sukayi cike da kaunar juna suna murmushi, kafin Dija tazare jikin ta tafita, 

Etama safeena meqewar tayi tafara cire kayan jikin ta dan shiga gurin wanka. 


Wannan Kenan...

           *(3)*

Wani tsaddadin guy ne nahango yana saukowa a hankali kan matattakala zuwa qasa, hannusa riqe da wani haddadin cup hannusa daya kuma yanaga kunnesa alamar waya yake amsawa sanye yake cikin wasu qananin kaya da sushi m matuqar karbar lallausar fatar jikinshi, 

Yayinda security's biyu ke dafama bayansa cike da aji yake takowa har allah yasa dashi da shiga cikin wani tangamemen parlor daya amsa sunansa, 

har yasamu guri ya zauna vai daina wayar da yake ba, qafarsa ya daura kan d'aya yana kur6an lemon dake riqe a hannusa, 


Manyan mutanene kusan su biyar suke zaman jiransa kowaninsa kallo daya zaka masa idanunka zai tabbatar maka naira ta zauna ajikin su. 

Shiru kakeji parlou yayi hatta da wanda ke wayar bakajin abunda yake fada. 

Saida yashare kusan 10mnt sannan can na iya jiyo kalmarsa ta qarshe da yake cewa me too my sweet mom😘 sannan naga ya dauke wayar a kunnesa

ahanali wani dayan  security yamatso kusa dashi tare da ranqufawa gurin dai-dai saitin kunnesa yayi masa mgn wanda koni banji me yake fada ba, 

Dai bayan daya naga wannnan security yaje gurin mutaninnan yana karba files dinsu kafin yakoma daya gefe yatsaya. 

can dai daga cikinsu yayi gyaran murya cikin girmamawa yace" muna neman alfarma ranka yadade dan Allah da ataimaka mana abamu kwangilar nan dan allah👏🏻

gefe daya yamaida kansa kafin yakoma jiyo kansa gurinsu cikin taushin murya yace musu insha allah karku damu, kuna iya tafiya, daga haka yamiqe yanufe sama cike da takonsa.



 *AིྀDིྀAིྀMིྀAིྀWིྀAིྀ*

Su safeena sun isa garin adamawa lafiya sun sauka gidan waning babansu mallam sule, mallan sule babban malamine danshi ke bada sallah anan cikin unguwarsu yanada mata biyu kuma kowaccin ta tanada yara uwar gida fadima tanada yara takwas biyar maza hudu mata, lubabatu kuwa tanada yara shida biyu maza mata hudu, gidane babban gida wanda dika iyalin gidan nacikin gidan annan suka ajiye iyalinsu, 

Gidan gwanin shawa da birgewa zamane wanda babu kissa acikinsa daga uwayin har 'ya'yan koda su safeena suka sauka gidan baqaramar tarba aka musu ba, saboda sosai akaji dadin xuwansu, dan kwananan ma aka yanki shawarar zuwa kaimusu ziyara sai gasu sunzo da kansu. 


Su jidda kam da zalifa da ikram da najwa. ba wanda yakaisu jin dadin zuwan su safeena

Saboda jidda da zalifa sune tsaran safeena, ikram kuma da najwa sune tsaran Dija shiyasa kusan kansu yaxo daya, wannan kenan... 



*WྂAྂNྂEྂNྂEྂ SྂAྂIྂFྂ...?*
     ***********

Saif dane ga shahararin dan kwangilar nan kuma d'an kasuwa Alhaji  Ahamad Baseera,

Alhaji Ahamad Nazeer yayi suna matuqa aduniy fiye da yanda ake tunani, Alhaji Ahmad yanada mata daya Hajiya Asma'u macce mai gadara da nuna isa tana matuqar son jin dadi a rayuwar ta, Saif shine danta na ukku aduniya kafinshi akwai mata biyu aunty sadiya da aunty meena dik sunyi aure harda ma yaransu kuma dik manyan jigajigan masu kudin Nigeria ne suke aure,  sannan shi SAIF bayanshi sai qanisa mai binsa uzaif bayan uzaif akwai mata biyu nadiya da nafisat" nafeeaat itace auta dik cikinsu ba wanda ya gado halin mamy su sai Nadiya, nadeeya sosai halinsu daya da mamy,  na qyamar talaka kokadan basasun yara6esu akwai son nuna qarya da nuna cewa su wasu ne. 

Saif tsaddadin yaro matashi mai aji yanada kyau naban mamaki baya da hayaniya kokadan kuma baya da buqatar amasa,  miskiline shi sosai wanda saikaji jiki kafin kasamu irinsa


baya da ra'ayin ko wace mace a yanzu,  yafi maida hankalinsa gurin harkokinsa dana mahifinsa kwatakwata shi bama zaunin  qasarmu bane Nigeria, yafi buqatar kullum yana waje rashin son hayaniya kumq bayason takura da yawan surutu mutunne shi d'an hutu


Ko yanzu mahaifiyarshi ce takirash suke magana akan lallai yadawo dan zasuyi tafiya adamawa ita dashi dan tanason yaje yaga 'yar governor adawama 'yar qawarta dansu dai dai ta, bayanda zaiyi ne kawai shi yasa yakar6a mata saboda shi yarone mai matuqar biyayya ga iyayin sa... 



```Yanzu labarin zai fara... ```

SAKO GAREKI MARUBUCIYR WANI JINKIRIN


*Naga saqonki na kumaji amma dauka bai zaman man dole ba, nasani dik abunda kake sai kaga wani qalubale aciki abinda kika fada dubunki sunfada akan marubuta, toh kisani bazan canja sunan book dina akanki ba kuma bazan daina yinsa ba, wannan tatsuniyar munsha jinta amma kisani ninasan kaina dan haka banda wannan time yanzu inada abubuwanyi agabana kema kiji danaki*
🤝🏻😊



             *(4)*


Tafiya suke cike da nishadi suna raha kai daga ganin su suna cikin farin ciki su shida ne dukan su, tafiyarsu suke cikin wani layi na masu kudi kasancewar unguwar shiru shiru ne yasasu tafiya yanda sukaga dama  dik wannan rakiyar ta Zalifa ce tazone gurin wata qawarta da sukayi scol daya suka kawo mata ziyara yau gaba dayan su, 

a kullum cikin yiwa zalifa qorafin qin zuwa gidansu summy take, ita kuma zalifa bawai batason zuwa bane a.. a sai dan batason shige matane dan gudun wulaqanci dikda tasan summy ba haka take ba, 


sallama sukeyi da su summy wacce tarako su tare da musu godiya da nuna jin dadin ta, ta wannan ziyarar da suka kawo mata. 

Can suka fara jiyo jiniya tashigo cikin layin🚔🚔🚔

Tsayawa sukayi dan ganin masu wucewa ikram ce tace cikin saure munbani koxo mutafi kada 'yan sanda nan sutafi damu, dik da kowace hankalinta nakan kwana da motocin zasu bullo bai hanasu darawa ba, 

summy ce tace lallai sis ikram a kwaiki da abun dariya tohh kuma aka cemiki shikenan da 'yan sanda sunga mutun sai suhau kame ko?😅

Kwantar da hankalinki wannan abun da kikeji bai wuce 'yar gwamna garin nan ce tazo kawo ziyara gidan qanwar mahaifiyar ta daman tana zuwa,  saboda 'yan uwanta,  amma wllh abunda nakeso daku karku kuskura ku kusance layin kubari tafara wucewa ko motoncin kada kukallah dik da ido ba'a masu shamaki, amma ku daure kudaukesu daga motocin da zasu shigowanan dan wlh mamallakiyar su batada mutunci kokadan tanada gadara da isa da nuna ubanta governor ne, 

Najwa ce tace ay wllh aunty summy ba abunda bamu sani gameda marar kunyar....... Mgnr ta talaqene sakamakon danniwar motocin cikin layin da suke tsaye, adai dai lokacin da sunka kusanto su, akuma dai dai lokacin da wata mage tafito daga cikin wani gida tabiyo kan titi zata wuce, 

Safeeena batayi wata wata ba ganin za'a taka wannan magen ana gani tayi saurin zuwa ceton ranta. 


Qoooo..... Take motocin sukaja wani erin Brake. Qutttttttttt.. 


Sai da kan Hanifa yayi gaba yayi baya gaba daya drink din da take riqe dashi  ya6ata mata wankan da tayi,


Kutt tashin hankali wanda ba'asa masa rana🤢

Gaba daya securities din dake cikin mota suka fito kankace me sunke waye Safeena suna huci ko wannin su fuskarsa tamkar baqin kumurci, rasa tayi yazatayi gata riqe da mage kawai sai tasake mage jikin safeena yahau rawa muryar ta narawa tace dan Allah kuyi haquri wllh bada gangan nayi ba. 

Wace 'yar iska jaka ce taso kasheni kwanana basu qare ba, safeena ta tsinkayo muryar Hanifa wacce qanqanin lokaci suka buda mata hanya ta bayyana gaba gareta 

ke dabbace koke sakarya ce da kina ganin motoci zasu wuce zaki karawa mutane a hanya kesan kowacece ni agarin nan da har kika za6e ki halaka rayuwata akan ki cece ta mace wllh natsaneki banza sakarya wacce batasan menene muhimmanci rai ba. 

cikin qanqanin lokaci idanun safeena suka qanqanci jin erin zagin da ake surfa mata nafitar arziki, wani wulaqantaccin kallo ne tafara jifan Haneefa dashi kafin tace... 


       **********

Wani mahaukacin gidane naga 'yan wangale get dinsa nan take wasu shahararrun motoci suka fara dannawa cikin gidan, gidane wanda bayada ko lissafi sunyi tafiya mai dan nisa kafin su shiga get din dazai sadasu da harabar filin gidan wanda cike yake da shuke shuke na alfarma abin ba'a cewa komai qaryane na iya lissafa muku abunda ke cikin gidan amma ku kwatanta da kanku zaifi. 😇

Mamy ce nafara hangowa tadosu motocin bayan sunsame guri sunyi parking sai faman murmushi take tamkar anmata bushara da Aljanna, suma su huzaif bayanta sukabi suna mai farin cikin dawowar dan uwan su. 


tinkafin yafito mamy ta sanya kanta cikin mota tare da riqo hannun sa suka fito tare, nan take wani sansanyar qamshi yamamayi gurin mai taba zuciya😜

A hankali suke tafiya shida mamyn sa har suka isa cikin wani babban parlou 

Numfasawa Nafeesat tayi kafin tace a gaskiya sis nadeeya brother Saif yanada matuqar kyau kinga fatarsa har wani yellow tayi gawani sihirtaccin kyau da allah yazu bamasa, uwa uba gashi dan gayu komai nasa mai kyaune wllh dik maccen da tasamu tsadaddin mutun kamar bro a gaskiya ita kam tagama sa'ar miji anan duniya☺

Cikin jin dadi nadeeya tayi wata dariya kafin tace gaskiya ne kam sis masha Allah,  yaya saif kam Allah yabashi komai shi yasa qawaye na ke damuna da zancin sa, 

Allah nima haka nafisat tace. 

Wai gulmar me kuke kunbar mutane da yunwa Uzaif ne ke wannan mgnr yana daga bakin qofar parlou. 

Cikin sauri suka qarasa suna dariya qasa qasa.

           *(2)*

     *WACECE SAFEENAH?*


Safeena 'yace ga malam Lirwanu, itace 'yarsu ta farko a duniya sannan Deja, tsakanin ta da Deja shekara biyu ne ba wata tsera atsakanin su dan kusan ma tsayinsu daya saboda Deja nada dan tsayi sosai kasancewar safeena gajiruwace yasa nan take Deja takai tsayin safeena, 

sai qannin su tagwaye mace da namiji saleem da saleemat baza su wuce 10 years ba,

dik wannan maganganun da mutane suke yawanyi akan safeena ba wani girmane sosai tayi a gida ba dan duka duka bata wuce 22 years ba, Kawai tsabar sa ido ne da rashin aykin yi saboda sunga Allah yayiwa mahaifinsu rasuwa, shekara biyar da suka wuce sakamakon hatsarin mota da yasamu daga Kano zuwa Kaduna, 

ba wani qrfine malam lirwanu yake       dashi ba, 'yar sana'ar sai da gwanjo ce yake da itace Allah yarufa masa asiri yake daukar dawainiyar iyalinsa, cinsu shansu tufarsu har zuwa kan kudin makarantar su har suka kammala secondary scol dinsu, yana fafutukar sama musu kudin cigaba da karatunsu zuwa Jami'a Allah yayi mishi rasuwa. 

Haka rayuwa ta canzawa su safeena yau dadi gobe akasin haka abubuwa suka taru suka musu yawa, gashi bamai taimaka musu kowa   takansa yake, abinda zasuci ma wuya yake musu, sauqin ta dai gidan da suke ciki mallakin su ne, 

a haka har Allah yakawo musu mafita suka sai da wata tinkiya da umman su ta siya, da dadewa suka dauke ta da 'ya'yan ta Duka aka sai da. 

tohh fa da kudin ne suke 'yar sana'arsu ta hannu suke samun abunda sukaci. 


Cikin taimakon ubangiji Allah yasama abunda suke sai dawa Albarka har umma taga bari takama sana'ar mijin ta ko Allah zai sa adace, haka kuwa akayi takama saro kayan gwanjo masu kyau tana sai dawa da haka suke rufawa junasu asiri ba wanda yasan halin da suke ciki... 

*CIGABAN LABARI*


shirye shirye kawai su safeena suke ita da Deja da Alama tafiya zasuyi edan ka kalle fuskokin su qunshi suke da farin ciki, sungama shirya komai Deja ta shiga gurin wanka safeena na fitar musu kayan da zasu saka, umma ce tayi sallama tashigo cikin dakin gefen gado tasamu ta zauna kafin tacewa safeena harkun shirya Kenan? 

cikin murmushi safeena tacewa umman su ehh wllh umma har mun shirya tintini ni harma nayi wankan Deja ce tashiga yanzu, wllh umma kokadan banaso muyi tafiyar dare ne shiyasa gara mutafi da wuri Adamawa akwai nisa sosai.


Hakane umma tafada ahankali kafin tace tohh ni har yanzu baku sanar dani ko yaushe Zaku dawo ba, ko ba rana?

wata kyakyawar dariya safeena tayi cikin 'yar tsokana tace tohh umma mutun yaje garin mahaifinsa kam aysai yaga dama zai dawo gaskiya ba rana dan so muke mugana da 'yanuwan mu sosai suganmu,mugansu kedai kawai sai kin gammu.

baki umma tariqe cikin mamaki tace ehhhh lallai sannuku masu dangi yo ay naga da mahaifin naku yarasu ba wanda yataimaka mana dako tsinke, kuma sunsan muna buqatar taimakonsu shine zaki wani cikani da zancin 'yanwanku banaso cika baki, umma takai qarshin maganr ta murmushi dauke a fuskarta dan ko ita cikin tsokana tayi wannan maganar, 

tabbb ayko wllh umma kiyi shirunki kawai dan koni gaskiya banji dadin abunda sukai mana ba, kawai dai dan kada muyada zumunci ne da bazanma koma taka qafata garinnan ba, 

Deja ke wannan maganar wadda shigowarta daki kenan taji ana wannan chapter shine tayi saurin karbewa.

Ke waya sakaki kinsan fa banason haka ko? safeena tafada cikin dan shan mur" Allah yabaki haquri sarkin son dangi yi haquri tuba nake.

haka dai suka gama dik abunda suke suka fito tare da yiwa umman su sallama, tana mai musu fatan isa lapiya har suka bar layin gidansu suka same mai napep ya daukar musu kayansu sai tasha, 
Acan tashar kuwa basu wani dadi ba dan bayan su mutun biyune kawai yarage ba kuma dade ba, motar tacika, suka dauke hanya sai dai muce su safeena Allah yasa a sauka lapiya..

            *(5)*


Bayan safeena ta qarewa Haneefa kallon wulaqanci, tare da ta6e baki,  tace dik nikikewa wannan zagin saboda nacece ran mage? Ke kina ganin rayuwar ta abar banza ce, takice mai amfani,? toh bari nafada miki rai dik rai ne babu rayuwar da tafi ta wani, sai rayuwar da takasance mai tsananin bautar ubangijin, toh kekuma banga alamun hakan atattare dake ba, kinga mai yuwa ma rayuwar magen tafi taki rayuwar daraja, saboda ita mai yuwa tasan darajar 'yan uwan ta dabobi, kekuma kokadan bakisan darajar 'yan uwanki 'yan Adam amatsayinki ta 'yar adam ba. 


Hmmm sai ki tantance min tsakanina dake wacece jaka,  sakarya, idan baki da amsa ni inada ita, wllh babu jaka sakarya wawuya wacce batasan darajar dan adam ba, da tawuce ki, ni bandamu da insan ke ko wacece ba,  saboda mai yuwa ma nafiki matsayi, ko agorin allah dan banako tantama hakan, dan inaji ajikina nafiki jin tsoronsa, kisani ba'ayi wata mace aduniya da zata gayaman mgnba sannan tatafi bayatare da na bata amsa ba, wllh qaryane ba ayinta ba, bare ke wacce ban dauka a bakin komai ba, 


Wayyo hoho zo kaga tsantsar mamaki da al'ajabi gameda takaici da sukayiwa Haneef dirar maqiya a qahon zuci.

Cikin wani jin haushi daya tinrniqe Haneefa azuciye tace nikike gayawa haka? wacece ke? Ke 'yar gidan ubanwa ce? da kike neman sanya  rayuwarki a halak.. 

cikin daga murya safeena tace wllh karkusa kiqarasa zancinki, umm  kedin banza wllh ke kinyi kadan kiman abunda Allah na  bai man ba, ni ba 'yar kowa bace sannan kuma ubana ba kowa bane sai  dai har yabar duniya, yanada daraja da qima a idon duniya har lokacin da yabarta mutane basu daina kallonsa da wannan daraja ba, kinga inada matsayin da baki da shi, asaboda ninasan kaina, 

Ke narantse da Allah darajar Allah da manzonsa kikace wllh da saina koya miki hankali,  inada abubwanyi agabana idan harke baki da shi. 

daga wannnan maganar safeena ta ratsa ta gabanta tabar gurin koda ta isa gurinsu Ikram gaba dayansu  sunyi mutuwar tsaye dan tsaf sukaji dik maganganun da sukayi itada haneefa. 

Kuma sam basuyi mamaki hakan da yafaru ba tsakanin su ba, dan sunsan ko wacece safeena ba tada wasa kokadan

Tsinkayo murayar ta sukayi tana cewa idan kuntashi tafiya kozo mutafi ko nakama gaba na. 


Haka suka tafi sukabar summy da faduwar gaba 

Duniya idan ran haneef yayi dubu toh yabace saboda tinda take aduniya ba'a ta6a gaya mata magana erin haka ba, kumama arasa wacce zata mata haka sai wata kucaka 'yar talakawa wllh tajawa kanta da dik dangin ta, 

Nune tamusu da summy da take tafiya cikin sauri da tsoro akan suzo mata da ita susata amota suqarasa gidan da zataje..

A hanakali suka dinga jin tashin muryoyi da misalin qarfe  8:09 am, na safe, bayan sunyi sallah assuba sunkoma bacci kasancewar jiya zaune sukayi sunata firar abunda yafaru jiya tsakanin Safeena da  Haneefa

Kusan ma abin dariya yadinga basu suka dinga dariya cikin dare har sai da inna fadima ta tsawatar musu sannan suka sassauta, kusan Haneefa kwana tayi tana juyayin abun dan tanajin firarsu ikram taqi tankasu har bacci yayi nasarar saceta cike da takaicin haneefa a ranta. 

Toh saifa gashi tinda safe hayaniya ta kaure cikin gida, ay sunajin haka gaba dayansu suka fito, nanfa sukayi arba da wasu securities mata sukusan hudu tare da summy a tsaye da alama summy tasha maruruka kafin tafadi gidansu Ikram, cikin mamaki suke tambayar meya faru?

Mama lubabatuce tafara korowa su ikram da bayani tana tambayarsu shin tabbas d'ai daga cikinku ta zage 'yar governor garin nan kodai qagine, ba gaskiya bane?

Shiru sukayi kowaccinsu nararrabun ido tamkar marar gaskiya,  wai ba magana ake muku ba kukayi banza da mutane?

Yaya sulaiman ne ke wannan maganar cike da jin zafin wannan taruwar da aka musu cikin gida da wajin gidama tinda safe haka

sauqinta ma malan bayanan yayi tafiya tin jiya.. 


kamar daga sama suka jiyo muryar Safeena wacce fitowarta daga cikin daki kenan, taci  tabbas nice nan nayi abunda akace nayi kuma ina sane da ita din kowacece nayi hakan, kuma har a zuciyana vanji ban kyauta ba, dik wani abu da suke tunanin zasuman wllh ashirye nake nakar6esa dan nasan dik abunda zasuma ubangijina yariga da yarubuto shi acikin qadarata, sannan kuma na ratse da har gobe taneme tacimin mutunci wllh sai naci nata fiye da yanda taman... 

Ke kinada hankali kuwa kinko san abunda kike fada?,

Safeena kinyi kuskure babba meya kaiki aykata wannan abun daga zuwanki eyee?

haba safeena ya kikejin mahaifiyarki zataji idan taji labarin abunda kikayi kina ganin kin kyauta kenan?,

kuma ma shin yanzu idan malam yadawo yakike son muce masa idan yanzu yatarar da bakyanan suntafi dake.

kamar yanda aka basu umurnin yin hakan akan suzo su tafi dake, kuma mu yanzu ina muke da qarfin ja dasu na hanasu tafiy dake. 

Dik inna Fadima ce ke wannan maganar fuskarta kad'ai zaka kallah kasan tana cikin tashin hankali marar misaltuwa,

hakama mama luba, da sauran mazan gidan da matansu kowa nacikin tashin hankali, bama Deja dasu najwa wa'anda tini suka fara kuka. 

Cikin qarfin hali safeena tace inna karki damu wllh allah yafisu,

Nidai kawai abunda nakeso daku shine inna dan Allah karkuga laifina

wallahi akan gaskiyata nake, su ikram zasu muku bayanin komai..



Haka family mallam  lirwan sunaji suna gani aka tafi da safeena ba yanda zasuyi, Deja kuka take kamar me suma su zalifa haka, gaba daya gidan shiru kakeji kowa naciki da alhinin wannan baqon lamari.. 

etama summy kuka take nan tashiga basu labarin irin wuyar da suka bata dan tafadi sunan gidansu Safeena taqi, saida tasha azaba sannan tayi magna, taci gaba da cewa wllh yanzu haka nasan gida suna cikin tashin hankali jina shiru da sukayi, dan allah zalifa ayiwa su yaya magana muje tare dansu wankeni gurin iyayina kuma dan allah kuyi haquri wllh bada sun raina nafadi nan gidan ba sunfi qarfinane taqarashe magna tare da fashewa da kuka.. 

Rungumeta sukayi gaba dayansu suna kuka Deja ce tace cikin kuka wllh babu komai sis karki damu kokadan bamuga laifinki ba, Allah dai ya tseratar da sis daga sharrin wa'annan mutane. 

atare sukace amin sukaci gaba  da kukan su.. Lol... 😜

*Via Wattpad Saadatalkali*


              *(7)*


         AིDིAིMིAིWིAི 

Wani kallon wulaqanci ne Haneefa ke jifan Safeena dashi kafin ta tattaro yawun bakin ta, ta tofama safeena su a fuska. 


Kafin Haneefa taja wani kafirin tsaki tace" kinyi qarya ki zageni kigayaman magana kuma kitafi kawai, dubeki ma matsiya ciya dake har kike da kalan da zakice zaki gayaman magna wllh ko ubanki daya kawoki duniya yayi kadan... 

Banza dake 'yar talaka wa'anda basu aje ba bare suba wasu ajiya. Kanta tadago tadube securities nan mata fuska a tamke tace" banace kuzoman da matsiyata iyayin nata bane zaku kawoman ita kadai kawai.. 

Am sorry madam dayar tace munje daniyar hakan amma kuma fada mana da akayi su baqine nan gidan qanin yayan mahaifin ta ne tazo, shiyasa bamu samu damar cika umarni ki ba, Allah yahuce zuciyar ki,

Nufasawa tayi sannan tace sunci arziki amma basu tsira ba dik inda suke sai ankawoma su harnan kinyi kuskure da kikayi qoqarin ja da wacce tafi qarfinki, inaso kufita da ita haraba bakin get afarmta hukunci kafin nafito dan inada babba baqo inason dik wanda zai shigo gidan nan yaga erin kalan wulaqanci da ake mata kuna jina?


Cikin sauri sukace angama madan za'ayi yanda kikace, 
Saida ta haye sama sannan suka tasa qeyar safeena agaba suka fita da ita, wacce tsabar baqin ciki yasama takasa cewa komai bancin da zuciyar ta tariga da tabushe da tini tafashe da kuka tsabar baqin ciki da yamata yawa azuciya. 


           *************


Yanzu shikenan munaji muna gani bazamuyi komai ba kenan, inajiye mana fushin malan idan yatarar da abinda ake ciki agidan nan, 

Su yaya sulaiman ne da yaya Sagir ne keta wannan tattaunawar atsakanin su, kuma sunrasa mafita, tohh wace ko mafitace suke da ita inasu inaja da gvrnrty..



Motoci ne bila adadin keta faman shigowa cikin harabar gidan governor abin ba'ama cewa komai,  baya ga motocin su Saif. Akwai motocin da akaje tararrsu na musamman tare da manyan abokanshi dake cikin wannan garin na Adamawa. 


Cikin ji da kudi da mulki kowa kefitowa erin babu sauqin nan👌🏻

Mamy kam 'yan canchara ado sai wanda yagani kallo daya zaka mata kasan cewa babbar macice hutu da jin dadi sungama mamaye dikkan jikinta dan alamu sun nuna. 


Dai-bayan daya suka dinga fitowa amma shiru kakeji Saif bai fito ba, 

Leqawa mamy tayi tace a.. a Saif ya haka? Kafito mana haka kabar mutane a tsaye.. 

Sosai suka rungume junasu itada qawarta Hajiya kubra cike da jin dadin wannan waduwar da kullum suke fatan wannan rana tazo musu saboda 'ya'yan su, kowaccin su bakin ta har kunne. 

Mamy ce tace a..a nikuwa ina 'yata ban ganta ba?

Ay kafin kafin Hajiya kubra tayi magana mamy ta tsinkayo Haneefa taci wani uban ado cikin wani tsadaddin less wanda kudinsa ma kadai idan kaji sai ka girgiza, fuskar nan ko tasha Make-up abin har yaso yayi yawa, da sauri tazo tayi Hugging din mamy ba kunya ba girmamawa adole wayeya, 

Fitowar Saif yayi dai dai da kwarawa safeena ruwan sanyi da akayi tindaga kanta har wata eriyar ajiyar zuciyace Tayi wanda saida yajiya ajikinshi. 

Koma kallon gurin yayi sosai yaga lallai idanunshi ba qarya suke masa ba, naganin wannan wulaqanci da akeyiwa matashiyar budurwa haka, Fitowa yayi gaba daya daga cikin motar yanufe gurin cikin takunsa.


Atake securities dinsa suka dafa masa baya, ganin haka ya Ankara dasu mamy Kallon gurinda Saif yanufa. 


Hajiya kubra ce tadube Haneefa tace waccan kuma wacece?

Cikin shagwa6a tace momy,'yar iskar yarinyar nan fa ce danake baki labarinta jiya wacce tazageni taman rashin kunya shiyasa yau tinda safe nasa aje gidansu aka dauko Man ita saboda nasan idan daddy yana gari ba yanda zan rama shiyasa tinkafin yadawo nasa ashirya wulaqantaman yarinyar nan. tagane shayi ba ruwa bane. 

Kinmin dai-dai daughter Mamy tafada cike da nunajin haushin yarinya qarara a fuskar ta, taci gaba da cewa wllh natsane talaka arayuwata saboda suncika girman kai gashi basuda komai  shiyasa nayi alqawarin matuqar ina numfashi aduniya bazan ta6a hada jini da talaka, ba.

Numfasawa tayi sannan tace tohshi wancan da yanufe gurin uban me zai yi.

Kuzo muje nasan halinshi da tausayi tinda wuri nada katar dashi nasanshi da shiga abunda by shafesa ba,


K'amshin sa kadai ya isar da saqon zuwansa gurin nan take masu bada horon suka dago dan har ankoma antayo wasu ruwan za'a watsawa Safeena sai dayar ta dakatar dasu da musu inkaya da mai zuwa. 

Nan take suka kai qasa dan bada gaisuwa baiko tankasu ba cikin daure fuska yace meye haka, waya sakaku metayi, kuna tsoron Allah Kuwa?

Lokaci daya dik yajero musu wa'annan tambayoyin suka rasa wacce zasu ansa, daya daga cikin suce tayi qarfin halin cewa ko ita bakin ta har rawa yake tsabar tsoro dan kallo daya suka masa sukasan cewa wannan babbane kuma ba wasa tattare da kyakyawar fuskar sa.


Am....ranka yadade... madan tawa rashin kunya kuma ta zageta.. tushine madam tasa aka daukota daga gidansu dan a hukunta ta, tou.. yanzu ma haka damun gama cikin wani daki za'a sanyata Wanda tini sanyin AC da fanka suka gama sanyaya dake zata zauna ciki zuwan wani lokaci kafin nan afiddata ta fuskance wani hukunci na gab.....


Is ok yafada da dan tsawa yace lallai daku da wacce tasakaku duka baku tsoron Allah kasheta zakuyi? 


Tohh saime idan ankasheta din ba ita ta janyo ba.....?
Yatsinkayo muryar mamy bayansa, kafin taci gaba da cewa"ninarsa wannan shishigi erin naka ina ruwanka waya sakaka, kawai daga zuwan mu ko ciki bamu shigaba bare mu futa, kana neman 6ataman rai wllh Saif kafita daga Idona, nasha gaya maka banason erin haka fa, shige mutafe👉🏻 tafada cike da umarni,


yanzu mamy da bakinki kike fadan haka 'yar adam ce fa kamar kowa kuma shikenan ana neman kashe yarinya bazanyi magana ba, itafa rayuwa ba haka take ba, idan mune yau, gobe bamu bane ba, wllh ko dabba ka wulaqanta sai Allah yanuna maka kuskurin ka bare dan Adam dake da girma da daraja, 

Inaso nasan wacce tasa ayi wannan kisan kai?

Nice nan, Haba Sweetheart yada zuwanka ko futawa bakayi ba kakeson ka tadama kanka hankali akan wannan Banz......... 

d'auuuuuuuuuu kaji ya dauke Haneefa da wani gigitaccin mari da saida yagirgiza dik wanda ke gurinnan Harma wa'anda ke can nesa saida hankalinsu yadawo, 

Tashin hankali zokaga fuskar mamy data Hajiya kubra dataji marin har cikin jikin ta,

            *(8)*


Ido cikin edo suke kallon juna su, Saif da Haneefah yayin da yake mata kallo mai tattare da tsana, ita kuma tana mishi kallon lallai ma wannan guy, yacika namiji da har yake da zarrar yimata wulaqanci har cikin gidan su, kuma har yadauke hannu sa, ya mare ta agaban mahaifiyar ta, kuma bataga alama ko ajikin shi wannan abun da yayi bai kyauta ba. 

kokadan bai bata haushi hakan da yayi mata ba, 

Illah ma qara burgeta da taji yayi, ya kuma qara shiga ranta, amma babu abunda yamata tsaye a zuciya erin yanda ya mare ta akan wannan shegiyar yarinya, toh amma kuma idan tayi la'akari da labarin sa da taji erin yanda kokadan ba'a raina talaka a gabansa ne yasa ta kauda komai aranta. 

Sai yanzu mamy  tayi qarfin cewa amma wllh lallai Saif banta6a tunanin rashin mutuncin ka da rashin ganin da rajata da girmana yakai haka agurin ka ba, yanzu a gabana nizaka ciwa mutunci? ka tozartani kanunaman ba kowa nake agunka ba,? 

Akan wannan wulaqantar tar yarinyar zaka wulaqantani a gaban aminiyata ka dauke hannu ka wankawa 'yarta mari a gabanta, kuma agabana, saboda rashin mutunci erin naka  ko? 

Tohh kasani ba ita ka wulaqanta ba nice nan kawulaqanta na kuma gode abinda kayi ka kyauta.

Rintse edon sa yayi da qarfi saboda kokadan bayasun abinda zai 6atawa mamyn sa rai, uwa uba kansa daya ke masa ciyo saboda wannan surutun kokadan bai saba yinsa ba, ko yanzu dole ce tasa dan yaga ana qoqarin kisan kai. 

Ciki dauriya da taushin murya yace "kiyi haquri mamy na, kokadan banayi hakan dan na wulaqantaki kamar yanda kikace ba, nayi hakan ne saboda bana iya jure ganin ana wulaqanta dan Adam musamman ma mace.

Sannan kudaina cewa 'yar mutane wulaqantata saboda Allah bai wulaqantata ba,

tinda tana dashi kuma tanada iyaye sannan agidansu akaje aka daukota, shin ita yanzu da ake son kasheta bata da gatane? ko kuma iyayinta basa sonta ne? 

wllh babu wulaqantacce sai wanda ya wulaqanta kansa, ay kuma talauci da mulki ba hauka bane da akeson kassara rayuwar 'yar mutane, shikenan kuma basa'ayi magana ba, wllh bazai yuba,


Kai kudauke ta kusaman ita amota, mutafi asibity Saif yafad'a cike da bada umarni ay babu 6ata lokaci matannan suka dauke Safeena da tune numfashin ta ke barazana daukewa, suka sakata a cikin mota d'aya, 


Juyowa yayi kan Haneefa fusakar nan ko dik taqara wani yellow saboda 6acin rai, cikin kakkausar murya yadubeta taré da nunata da d'an yatsa yace" ke kuma bari kiji, wllh dik ranar da kika koma gigin sa adauko 'yar mutane daga gidansu dan kawai kici zarafin ta, wllh sai namiki abinda baki ta6a tinani ba, a rayuwarki Saif yafada cike da gargadi kafim yanufe gurin wata hadaddiyar mota nan take aka bude masa da saura yashiga sannan aka rufe,  a 360 suke fita gidan.
 


Inason muje asibitin da tafi ta kowace asibitin garinnan tsada da qwararrin likitoci saboda inason aduba lafiyar ta sosai, 

Angama ranka yadade dayan security yace, nan take suka dauke hanyar zuwa babban asibitin dake cikin garin Adamawa, motocin kadai zaka gani kasan cewa wani babbane aciki,

Mamaki taré da al'ajabi sungama yiwa mamy yawa rasama tayi abinda ke damun ta tayi, kunya ce taré da takaici suka rufe ta, duniya tarasa ma wani hukunci zata yiwa Saif, kwata kwata shi rayuwarshi daban take dik abinda yatashi yi sai yayi baya shakka cikin ma yana gudun 6acin ranta bai son fushin ta, da batasan yanda zata kasance ba, 

Haneefah ce ta isa gurin ta, tare da riqo hannun ta ahankali tace karki damu mamy akan abunda sweethrt yaman wllh kokadan ban dauke wannan komai ba, mamy Haneefa takira sunan ta kafin tace "kinyi matuqar sa'a aduniya da har kika haifo jajirtaccin namiji kamar Saif, ki godewa allah, haqiqa Saif yashiga rayuwata fiye da komai na duniya wllh ina matuqar sonshi saboda ina sonshi haka kuma ina sonshi saboda ya cancance asoshi tabbas Saif yayi kama da kansa, wllh mamy ina masifar sonsa dan allah kitaimaka man nasamesa plssssss. 

rungume mamy Haneefa tayi taré da fashewa da kuka, 

Haba my daugther dan allah kidaina banaso wllh Saif nakine har abada in allah ya yarda, kuma ina raye karkiji komai kinji


Kanta ta daga a hankali alamar taji


Itadai momyn Haneefa murmushi kawai tayi amma akwai wani abu aqasan ranta wanda nima bansan ko menene shi ba, 

da haka suka rankaya suka shiga cikin gida mamy naci gaba da masifa tana fadar ay zaizo yasameta.


       *************



Dik wani taimako na gaggawa anbawa safeena shi cikin qanqanin lokaci tohh harakce ta manya dan tindaga lokacin da labari ya eso cewa Saif ne a wannan asibitin Doctors din dake cikin asibitin suka rude dan Maihaifin Saif ne yayiwa mai asibitin hanya dan samun cikar burinsa kuma gashi yanzu takai babu asibitin da takaita daukaka da qwarewa gurin ayki anan dai cikin garin Adamawa.

shiyasa komai akayishi lokaci kad'an kuma cikin ikon Allah Safeena tadawo normal sai dai baccin da tasamu sakamakon qarin ruwa da aka mata kuma ansanya alluran sa bacci a ciki, 


Dik yanda likitoci sukayi akan Saif yatafiyar sa akwai masu kula da eta na mussaman da zasu kula da eta harta tashi, kuma Ida tatashi zasu kirasa amma yaqi, abunda kawai ya iya cewa"shine kuyi aykinku kawai dan ku tabbatar da samun lafiyar ta, saboda wannan amana ce, haka dole suka haqura suka qyakesa, 


Safeena ita a batatashi daga vaccin da take ba sai bayan isha.. ga lokacin su Saif sunje masallaci dan yin sallah. 

ko ina taci gaba da bi da kallo har sai da tagama tina dik abunda yafaru da eta kafin tace a hankali amma wannan shegiyar yarinyar tacika tsinana wllh saina koya mata hanakali.... 


Motsi taji alamar za'a bude qofa maida edonta tayi kan qofa tana jiran mai shigowa. 


Edo hudu sukayi da junasu nan take Safeena ta miqe da sauri har roba ruwan na batun cirewa tare da fara salati aranta tana mai karanto dik wata addu'a da tazo kanta, kasan cewar koda Saif yazo agazonta dazu tini hankalin ta yagushe bata gansa ba.

Mamaki ne yagama cikashi ganin yanda Safeena ta tsorata dan tagansa bayan da shi tinda yake bai ta6a sanya ta" a idonsa ba, koda kuwa amafarki, tinanin yayi ko dai tasamu ta6uwar hankali ne?


mamakin shi bai gama tsinkewa ba, jiyayi tana karanta dik addu'ar da tazo bakin ta da qarfi tana qoqarin cire ruwan da akasa mata tamkar wata mai ta6uwar hankali... 


Zuciyarshi ce yaji tayi wani erin bugawa sakamakon ganin yanda jini ke fita daga hannuta saboda allura data fizge iya qarfin ta, 


Saif bai san sanda yabude murya da qarfi ba wacce koshi bai san yana da itaba,  yakama kiran "Doctor🗣🗣🗣🗣 da iya qarfinsa ........

            *(9)*


Kafin qiftawar ido Doctors tare da nurses' sun cika dakin hatta da securyts ba'a barsu abaya ba, kuka Safeena keyi da gasken gaske tana fadin dan allah kufitar man da wannan wllh aljanine Idan kuma bakwa ganinshi dan allah kubarni nafita,


wata nurse ce taje gurin ta tare da, da fata tace kikwantar da hankalin ki ba aljani anan gurin wannan da kike gani Shine yasa aka kawoki nan mutunne shi kamarke dan haka karki damu kanki godiya yadace kimasa ba tsoronsa yadace kiyi ba Kinji ? 

kanta tadaga a hankali alamar taji, nan kuwa gaba daya kunya tagama rufeta, yanda lokaci daya kyan wannan hadaddin mutun ya razanata haka haryaso yabada ita, tohm allah dai yasa bai gane abinda yasa taji tsoronsa ba, dan karya raina ta.

gefinsa takalla cikin kallon nan na qasa-qasa taga yama maida hankalin sa gaba daya kan waya, ajiyar zuciya tayi tace am...tana susar kai tamkar marar gaskiya tace"ni ba wai wani abu bane yasa nayi hakan kawai dai nadauka mafarki ne nake, 

gaba dayansu ba wanda bai murmusa ba saboda dik singane abunda yasa tayi hakan sune take son rainawa da hankali, 

bayan doctor yagama murmushin sa sanann yace toh yanzu zauna namiki wasu tambayoyi dan mutabbatar da samun lafiyar ki kamar yanda yallabai yace'

dago kanta tayi takalle doctor sannan tamaida kanta qasa tare da isa bakin gado ta zauna a hankali, cikin qanqanin lokaci doctor dik yagama mata tambayoyin da yake son mata kuma alhamdulillah yaga cewa tasamu sauqi dan haka yadube Saif tare da cewa Yallabai yanzu zamu iya baku sallama saboda sanyin bai bugar da ita sosai ba, gadai maganin ta nan da akasiyo insha allah indai zataci gaba da shanshu zata qara samun sauqi, 

tamkar badashi ake magana ba azahiri, amma abadi ni dik abunda doctor yace yaji shirune kawai yamasa. 

dik wani cike-ciken takardu anriga anyi anbasu sallama nurses din dake gurin su suka taimaka mata suka kaita har bakin mota tare da taimaka mata tashiga ta zauna kafin su mata fatan qara samun sauqi suyi ciki abinsu. 


tsaf take qare masa kallo ta cikin mota yayin da suke tsaye da Doctor yana qara mishi bayani. ba qaramin burgeta yayi ba da taga yanda yake tafiya sécurités na biyar sa, abaya har gurin motar da take gaban ta ta, 

a tare suka fito daga cikin asibitin yayin da motocin su Saif suka dauke titin hannun dama, ita kuma driver tare da wani security tambayar ta sukayi tana iya gane gidansu jin tayi gabata ya wani erin faduwa saboda itada take baquwa acikin wannan garin taya zata iya kwatancin gidansu, bare wannan unguwar dake da manyan benaye, 

ko bazaki iya gane gidanku bane taji wannan security yafad'a. 


gaskiya kam danni baquwace anan garin amma ko kunsan wani wanda ake cewa Malam sule mai da'awa ? 


shiru sukayi suna tinani kafin driver yace Eh kamar nata6a jin sunan amma bari mufita cikin gari sai mu tambaya..

haka suka dauke hanyar hannun hagu suka nufe cikin gari ko allah zaisa asan wanda yasan gidan...

edanun mamy sun
rufe gabad'aya sai surfawa Saif ruwan masifa take, inda take shiga ba tanan take fita ba, kansa naqasa yayin da yake durqushe agaban ta, duniya yarasa abunda ke masa dadi kokadan bayason yaga wani ya6atawa Mamy rai bare toh ace shi da kansa, sosai yake jin haushin hakan, yarasa meyasa mamayn sa bata karba gaskiya sai taga laifinsa dan yana son ya lurassa da ita, 

Sai da yaji tayi shiru dan kanta sannan yace cikin taushin murya" dan allah mamy kiyi haquri idan hakan da nayi ya 6ata miki allah yahuce zuciyarki. 


Ba zanyi haqurin ba, nace bazanyi haqurin ba, saida kagama kunyatani gaban dik wanda ya dace ka mutuntani agabansa kadauke hannuka kamare 'yar aminiya ta kuma matar da zaka aura, agabana agaban mahaifiyar ta, baya ga wannan dik bai esheka ba shine kadauke shegiyar yarinyar nan kakaita asibiti wanda banta6a gani kayi hakan ba sai dai kasa akai mutun amma yanzu da kanka, kaida Kake fad'ar lokacinka atsare yake baka shiga lokacin da zaka wani abu kayi wani, sai gashi ni lokacina kaje kabawa munafukar yarinyar nan, wllh sai ka tozartani yau fiye da tunanin mai tunani kuma nagode. 


Jiyake tamkar yafasa kuka yakeji wai yazai fahimtar da mahaifiyar sa, har tafahimce sa ne? 

Jiyake zuciyar sa ke masa matuqar quna idan tana zagin yarinyar nan da bataji ba, bata gani ba, 

Tattaro yawun bakinsa yayi yacewa mamy" naji nayi laifi Mamy kiyi haquri duniya komai kikeso zanmiki shi matuqar baifi qarfina ba, zankasance mai miki biyayya koda ta bayan ranki ne insha allah, dan allah kidaina 6ata ranki akaina wllh hakan na matuqar sanyani cikin tashin hankali. 

Numfaswa mamy tayi kafin tace "shikenan nayi hanquri amma da sharadi... 


shiru yayi kadan sannan yace menene komeye namiki alqawarin yinsa matuqar ba kaucewa hukuncin allah ba. 


baima kauceba sarkin iya tsari, son nake ka amince man yanzu ba anjima ba cewar ka amince da auren ka kai da Haneefah, idan har kaman haka tom kawanke dikkan laifinka agurina sannan kuma zankasance cikin farin ciki tare da sanya maka albarka har qarshin rayuwarsa. 


Tirqashi" wata zuface tafara keto masa tako ina dik da sanyin AC dake cikin parlour hakan bai hanashi jin wani zafi yakama dikkan jikinshi ba, kansa ke sara masa tamkar zai tsage ya keji, amma yazaiyi dole yaqi farin cikin sa yaso na mahaifiyar sa, dan shine samun kwanciyar hankalin sa. 


muryasa batako fita tsabar baqin ciki dake cin zuciyar sa yace" idan hakan shine zai sa kisamu farin ciki Mamy azuciyar ki wllh na amince da abinda kike SO. 

Saura kadan Haneefa tafasa iho sai da momyn ta tayi saurin toshe mata baki, dan tindazu suke la6e suna saurarin abinda suke tattaunawa, daki sukayi yayin da haneefa tashiga cikin toilet aguje ta dauro alwala wai ita adole sallah godiya ga allah zatayi, bayan ko sallah magarib da ishsha'e bata samu damar gabatarwa ba, zatace zatayi sallah Nafila saboda kawai tsabar baqin cikin da take ciki Saif yafita da 'yar iskar yarinya nan. 


Mamy albarka take ta sanyawa Saif wanda shiko zuciyarsa ke masa suya, cikin qarfin hali yamiqe tare da mata sallama zaije hotel ya kwana, saboda Sam baya iya kwana wannan gida. 

Kokadan mamy bata hanashi ba tsabar murna da take ciki yahanata tambayar yaci abinci ko zaici ?  bare harta lura da erin baqin cikin da d'anta yake ciki. 


Haka yamata sallama yafita bayako ganin gabasa, mota aka bude masa yashiga suka rufe suka bar gidan.


ciki Mamy tayi da hanzari dan tawa aminiyar ta babban albishir sufara shirye shiryen biki, batan san su wannan zancin tsohone à gurin su ba... 


gabad'aya gidan ankewaye safeena sai faman tambayoyi suke mata, fuskokinsu kawai zaka kallah kasan suna cikin farin ciki. agajiye take ga yunwar da tamata yawa, gashi sunsha wuya kafin sugane gidan da qyar suka samu wanda yasan gidan yakawosu bayan sun sauke tane suka bata magungunnan ta da damin kudi wanda bansan ko nawa bane sukace yallabai ne yace abata, tare da sanar da ita lallai yace tasha magani.

Fadar farin cikin da jama'ar gidan sukayi sai kagani haddai da tagama basu labarin komai sun tausaya mata sosai sannan kuma sun mata dariya akan labarin ta6arar da tayi asibiti. 


Ruwa aka samata tayi wanka sanan taci abinci tayi dukkan sallolin dake kanta sanan fa su Ikram fira tasoma dan sunso matuqa suga wannan guy da Safeena tarikece akansa dik da ko erin kawaicin ta da kauda kai akan abu...

           *(10)*


Firace suke ta faman zubawa har dare yaraba basuyi bacci ba, sai faman debewa Haneefa Albarka suke tare da allah ya Isa, bancin haka babu abunda zasuyi dan bazasu iya ja da ita ba, sai dai allah yafi qarfin ta, 

a haka dai har bacci yasace su dikan su, gaba dayan su, har safeena wacce take jin ta tamkar anmata shegen duka tsabar yanda take jin jikin ta yamata nauyi. 

Abin mamaki bacci tayi mai cike da mafarkan wannan had'ad'in guy wanda ko sunan sa bata sani ba. 


     ***************


Zaune yake bakin gadon dakin sa dake cikin wani hadaddin Hotel dafe yake da kansa wanda yake ji tamkar yafado tsabar buga masa da yake, 


Maganar da sukayi da mamyn sa ne take ta faman masa yawo a qwaqwalwa, tabbas uwa uwace kuma Allah yafifita ta fiye da uba amma yasan cewa yarinyar da mamyn sa keso ya aura hankalin sa kwata-kwata bai kwanta da ita ba, kuma yasan mahaifin sa sam ba erin wannan yake mishi sha'awa da ya aura ba, amma tohh ya zaiyi dole ya danne zuciyar sa yabi umarnin mahaifiyar sa, 

Tsarinsa kokadan bai tashi yin aure a yanzu ba, har sai yasamu wacce yaji yanaso a ransa wanda yasan cewa da wuya idan zatazo nan kusa saboda bai faraji ajikin sa lokacin zuwa ta yayi ba. 



yayi nisa sosai cikin karatun wasiqar jaki. wanda kokadan baya da wani amfani yinsa. wani qayattacin murmushine yasu6uce masa wanda koshi baisan sanda yazo masa ba, tinawa yayi da yarinyar dazu wacce suka kai asibiti yanda ta dinga faman zuba addu'a wai shi zata kallah tacewa aljani. Kurjciya da dadi take, yafad'a aransa kafin yayi qarfin halin miqewa tsaye yanufe hanyar toilet dan watsa ruwa, sosai yake ganin juwa saboda ga yunwa sannan kuma ga rashin isashin hutu da bacci da bai samu ba, wanda yaune tazo mishi da hakan... 


Ba qaramin murna da nuna farin ciki momy tayi ba, sai sanyawa Saif albarka suke, ita kuwa Haneefa tana gefe sai faman rufe fuska take adole ita wai kunya, (uhmm... daga baya kenan) 


maganar tsare tsare biki ne su Mamy suka fara tattaunawa, dan basasun adauke wani lokaci. 


zuciyar Haneefa yau jinta take fes tamkar babu abinda yata6a 6atamata rai duniya, sam har taman ta yanzu da wata wai Safeena da kuma abunda tamata, 

Yanzune hankalin ta ya kwanta zata same namiji mai tsada wanda gwargwado Allah ya mallaka masa komai, zata kece raine gurin qawayin ta, Saboda tana matuqar Son Saif a rayuwar ta. a gaskiya nayi sa'ar miji kam heee wllh naqaro wulaqanci tafada da shewa dik da ita kadai take dakin ta, amma su Mamy sunso sujiyo Ehun ta..



 *Washe gari* 


Bayan su safeena angama karyawa anyi wanka kowa ninsu yayi kwalliyar sa tamkar zasu fita unguwa, zaune suke cikin daki sai faman surutu ake, innace ta cikin dakin tare da cemusu kunsan wannan surutun ajiyeshi zakuyi kutaso zuwa aykin gida dan fita zamuyi tare da matan yayyunku zamuje qauyin mahaifin ku gurin biki dan haka yau kuke da aykin dik wannan gidan, wasu suyi abinci wasu sukama wani ayki, 

wanke wanke dai Almajirai sunriga sunyi da shara yanzu yarage naku, idan kuntsaya surutu wannan cikin ku, nikunga tafiya ta zanje nashirya, 

Ikram ce tace tare da turo baki kuma shine inna tinda safe baku daura kayanku ba kafin yanzu kungama, inamu ina aykin dik wannan gidan gsky inna saiku. 

tsabar mamaki magana ma kasayi inna tayi illa gyad'a kanta da tayi kawai tace toh kada allah yasa kuyi tawucewar ta abin ta. 

Safeena ce tace kinganki ikram da mugun son jiki kike yanzu meye laifi tinda munada dan yawanmu nan danan zakuga mungama cikin lokaci indai ba shiriri ta muka tsayawa yi ba, 


Sunaji suna gani su inna suka shirya tare da surukan su suka tafiyar su, dole su Najwa aka mike alanqosa kokadan basasun ayki ay dagangan su inna suka musu haka, saboda malam bayanan bare dole su suyi tinda bazasu fara bashi abincin yara ba. 


Tohhfa ayki yayi ayki sai faman zufa ake manyan su safeena suke abinci qananan kuma suke yanke yanke abubuwa, 

Kwatsam suka fara jin dirar motoci bakin gidan su,
Deja ce tafara cewa" kunkoji  abinda nake ji, 

Najwa tace qwarai kuwa kamar dirar manyan motoc... Bare muga ikram da Deja sukace 

Kafin su zalifa su hanasu tin sun fita waje. 

Habaaa aguje suka shigo gidan suna haki suna cewa wllh sundawo.

Ido su zalifa suka turo sannan sukace waton daman wannan tsinana matar bata hanqura ba kena...


Basuko Ida abinda zasuce ba Safeena tafige gyalen ta dake sarkafe da qofar kiching tayi waje dan dama damar take son koma samu na yaga Haneefa gaban ta da bayan ta,

Kallon kallon suka shiga yiwa junansu kowa na dibin nakusa dashi, ganin sukayi Safeena takoma cikin gida da d'an gudun ta, da hanzarin su suma suka rufamata baya, 

dai dai shi kuma lokacin ya ajeye wayar sa, tare da mai da kansa yana duban gidan gyaran murya yayi kawai driver yaja mota suka fara tafiya, sauran motocin na take masu baya. 


koda suka shiga suka tarar da Safeena jingine da bangon kiching dafe da qirjin ta, tana faman maida numfashi, Zuciyar ta keta faman bugawa da sauri da sauri tamkar zata faso qirjin ta fito.


Mamaki dik yagama cika su Nazifa ga tarin tambayoyi dake cin zuciyar ko wacin su,  sosai suke kallon ta yanda tawani rintse idon ta, tohh menene ?

kwashewa su Najwa sukayi da Dariya gabad'ayan su banda Jidda da tayi wannan maganar dan bakin gaskiyar ta kenan


sai da sukayi dariyar su mai isarsu sannan suka tsagaita Safeena ce tace"haba sis yi haquri daman ay ko bakuso kuji ba zanfada muku.  dan gaskiya nayi mamakin ganinsa a qofar gidan nan wllh  har yanzu ganin nake tamkar mafarki nake ba gaske bane,

gaskiya kam, sukace tare kamar had'in baki. 

Hmm m Tom kenan yanzu me yazo yi ? 
gurin wa yazo ? 

Oho Safeena tabawa jidda amsa, kafin taci gaba da cewa wllh nima abunda nake son sani kenan.

Deja ce tace tohh sis mai yuwa gurinki yazo yaduba lafiyarki ko kinji sauqi? shiyasa qila yazo yaganki, kuma kinwani sheqo karfa yarainaki,

wayam"sai takon sawayen tayoyin motoci kadai tagani har aranta bataji dadin hakan ba, amma yazatayi tinda yariga yarafi, cikin gida takoma tare da gayawa 'yan uwanta cewa bata tarar dashi ba, 


toh tayama za'ayi yajiraki tinda ba aykjnyi ne baya da va, jiddace ke wannan maganar 

safeena dai komai batace ba dan tagaa alama jiddah ranta kamar a6ace yake, fita isakn ta tayi tanufe cikin kiching dan duba abincin ko yayi ? 

tabarsu sunaci gaba da tattauna maganganunsu na zuwan Saif da kuma tafiyar sa....


   *************

tabbas yana matuqar tausayin yarinyar yanaji ajikinshi tamakar wani gefene nashi najikin shi to menene shi ? 

baida amsa baiwa kansa har dai suka Isa gida. 

Dik yanda mamy taso Saif suke be da Haneefa dansu dai-dai ta, hakan bai samu yuwa ba, lalla6asa take yanzu dan tasan halinsa baudadin mutune shi, gara abishi a hankali harsu same cikar burinsu,

            *(12)*


Kayan ciye-ciye ne birjik kamar hauka anbaje su tsakiyar wani tsararrin parlour sai faman shewa su Haneefa sukeyi suna qarawa tamkar anmata albishir da Aljanna, su kusan hudu ne cikin parlou dik qawayin ta ne, kuma 'yan uwanta.


Kalisat, Afna, Sara, kasaitattun 'yan matane masu ji da kansu, kokadan basu da mutunci, dika halinsu daya da qawarsu Haneefa shi yasama qawancin yake matuqar musu dadi dan suna tsula tsiyar su yanda suke SO. 

bayan sunci sun qoshine ko waccen su takama tsadadda wayar ta tana dannawa tamkar wasu kurame suka koma tamkar ba yanzune aka gama shewa da ihu saboda Albishir din da Haneefa tamusu zata aure tsadaddin matashin saurayi kamar Saif. 


Dikansu ba wacce bata sanshi ba, dan ba boyayye bane a duniya fittace ne wanda kowa yasanshi. 


can Haneefa" tace dan Allah inaso kutashi muje ku rakani zanje Saloon.

umm gaskiya nima kaina yayi datti muje kawai.

Afna tace itama tanason awanke mata qafafuwan ta, haka dai suka tashi suka bar gurin da dik suka 6ata saboda sunsan masu ayki su kawai suke jira su gyara gurin. 


cikin mota daya suka shiga gaba dayansu hatta da securitys ko daya basu fita dashi ba, momy kawai suka gayawa zasuje saloon tamusu fatar dawowa lapiya suka fita daga part din ta suka shiga motar kaleesat sukabar gidan.

driver sai faman zabga uban gudu yake kan titi saboda yanda yallabai yau da kansa yace suyi gudu saboda bayasun suyi dare basu dawo ba, bayason su kwana ko ina yau yake so sujiyo sudawo bauci.

Kefa muke jira Safeena kina 6ata mana lokaci. 

Kallon zalifa Safeena tayi kafin tace kunsan idan kuka matsaman zanyi zamana dan daman matsuwarku ce tasa zanje dole badan inaso ba, gurin fa bidi'a ne zamuje ba gurin wani gagaruma nasiha zamuje ba, kunwani dame ni. 

Allah yabaki hanquri jidda tafad'a, tare da cewa Kamun yana da muhimmaci sosai agurina. 

Ta6e bakin ta kawai Safeena tayi ba tare da tace komai ba... 


Haka suka shirya suka fita, yayin da su Deja suke musu tsiya suna cewa dik wannan erin wanka haka, tamkar zasuje gurin gasar za6ar kyawawa, sudai Allah yasa daga can asamomusu yayyu. 

basu kulasu ba suka fita iskan su sukayi gaba, 


       ************

Wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya ce ya sauke, alokacin da suka shigo cikin garin Adamawa, toh meyasa yau yake jin kansa daban da jikin sa, bayan bayau bane yasaba shigowa cikin Adamawa ba. 

(tohhh nima dai bansani ba, mai yuwa ko ku masu karatu kunsani kubawa Saif ansa.) 


tsaye suke abakin titi dan jiran abun hawa yau da dukkan alama ana wuyar masu Napep, haka dai sukaci gaba da tsayuwa har tsawun wani d'an lokaci.

Can"sai ga wata dalleliyar wata mota baqa qatuwa, tawuce ta gaban su yayin da ruwan da akayi jiya da daddare sun kwanta gurin, sukuma su Safeena na gefin ruwa, da qarfi motar ta danna cikin ruwa, ay gaba daya ruwan sukawa su Safeena wanka tufafin jikin su, duk suka lalace, zo kaga fusakokin su tamakar zasuyi kuka, bama kamar Safeena da take da qanqani,

motar har tatafi kuma sai tadawo ta parka ta gaban su. 
kokadan baka ganin wanda ke cikin motar saboda gaba daya glass din motar baqi ne, bude motar akayi aka fito.

wani matashin saurayi ne daka gansa kasan yana cikin jin dadi ba laifi ya had'u kallo daya zaka masa kasan wannan mai sanyi ne sosai.

Alokacin da yaga erin abunda yawa su Safeena sai da ya dafe kansa tare da cewa Oh"my god dan Allah da darajar annabi kuyi haquri dan Allah.

Ko waccen su rasama tayi me zatace tayi sai Safeena ce ta numfasa tace a gaskiya malam idan mukace kamana wulaqanci matuqa bamuyi qarya ba, sannan kuma bamuji dadi ba, amma tinda kabamu haquri ya wuce amma gskiya ka kiyaye ka dinga dubin gaban ka. 


tinda Safeena take magana guy nan ya tsareta da edo. jin saukar muryar ta yake har cikin qwaqwalwar shi. gaba daya Safeena tagama tafiya da tunanin sa.

cikin qarfin hali yace ina matuqar godiya kuma insha allah zamkiyaye yanzu toh ina kukayi ? 


inako mukayi banda gida tinda ya za'ayi mutafi haka gurin da zamuje dole mukoma gida tinda kayi mana wannan, jidda ce ke magana cike dajin haushin abunda yamusu..

Hannuwan sa ya game guri daya👏🏻 tare da cewa amin afuwa plsss. yanzu idan bazaku damu ba kuma idan kunyar da dani bazan cutar daku ba, mai zai hana kushigo nakaiku gida tinda kunga za'a dinga kallon ku Ida kuka wuce amma yanzu asiri rufe kushigo na'aje ku, gida ko zan dan goge kad'an daga cikin laifina dan Allah.👏🏻👏🏻

basu da yanda zasuyi dole suyi yanda yace tinda da gida da bakin titin tafiya ce mai nisa sosai, gashi abun hawa yayi wuya bare su tara yamai dasu, kuma sunsan samarin unguwarsu ba mutunci ne dasu ba, tsaf zasu dinga masu Dariya.

Haka kuwa suka shiga yayin da suka cilastawa Safeena shiga gaban mota, ba dan taso ba, shiko gogan hakan yamasa dadi har a zuciyar sa...

tohh kuma ba wacce tasan gida acikin ku taman kwatance ?

sufa su Deja basu san Saif bane ke cikin mota saboda ba'a ganin wanda ke ciki amma shi tsaf yake tsinkayosu. kuma abun da yasa tunanin su bazai basu shi bane saboda mota daya yazo da eta, daga shi sai driver, haka yake idan yanason yayi fitar sirri.

tohh gaskiya basa nan sai edan tafiya zakayi kadawo anjima. 


tohh yayi ba matsala amma inason kusanar da su zuwa baqonsu kafin nadawo. 

cikin mamaki sukace wai baka ma san gurin wacce kazo bane ? 

à.. a nasani idan nagansu sun dawo sunan ta dai ne ban sani ba, 

tohh shikenan zamu sanar dasu sai anjima.. sukace tare da shigewa cikin gida, shikuma driver ya koma gurin motar dan yasanar da Saif cewa manyan basu gidan sai qannesu,

               *(13)*

quri yawa motar da edo yana jiran yaga mai fitowa cikin ta, su zaleefa ne suka fara fitowa gidan baya, kayansu har sunfara bushewa sai dai idan ka tsirawa kayan edo sosai zaka fahimce sunyi datti.

har yanzu bai kauda kansa daga kallon motar ba, haka kuma gaban sa bai daina fad'uwa ba,  a hankali yaga wani matashin saurayi yafito daga cikin motar yayin da ya zagayo gefen mota da saurin sa yabude gaban motar yayi tsaye agurin. 

kyawawan kafafuwan ta tafara sauka suwa kafin tafito gaba d'aya.... 


wani bugun da zuciyar sa tayi ne yasa saida kansa yasara, dafe qirjinsa yayi da sauri yayin da kala edanunsa ya canza addu'ace yayi qoqarin fara karantowa a zuciyar sa har yaji bugun qirjin sa ya ragu. 

Innalillahi wa'inna elaihir raji'un yafara furtawa afili wanda yasa driver sa yafara tsorata, afili yake ta nanata wannan kalmar gabad'aya Saif baya cikin hayyacin sa. 


Sukuma su Safeena sai yanzu edonsu yakai ga motor dake gefin gidan su wacce akayi parking dinta kuma basuga kowa yafito ba, 


Shi wannan saurayin sai faman zuba yake shi adole subashi no Safeena, bayan sun rantse masa akan dikan su basu da waya saboda gidansu ba'a basu damar riqata ba, 

badan yayar da ba ya qyalesu amma da sharadin zai dawo dan haka yake gayawa safeena shi dai yana son ta, so bana wasa ba, dan haka yanason tafada gida dan abashi ezini fara zuwa gurin ta kafin yaturo. 

amsa masa sukayi akan yabasu kwana biyu zasuyi shawara, basu jira cewar sa ba sukayi shigewar su cikin gida, dan basasun su yaya ko malam sutara dasu agurin.

dole da yaga sun shigewar su gida, shima yashiga motar sa yaja yatafi. 


cikin qarfin hali Sai yacewa driver sa kadaukar man number motar sa da sauri kafin ya 6acewa ganinka. 

Kafin kace me har yacika aykin Oga sa, dan daman driver mai elimine matashine kuma, bakomai ne zai mishi wuyar dauka ba..


Sai da sunka shafe kusan 10mnt da shiga cikin gida, dan har jidda taje dauko musu abinci su kicking sai ga wata yarinya 'yar maqotan su mai suna siyama tashigo gidan tare da yin sallama tace inna ina Aunty zalifa,?

a.. a yau siyama gidan nan ina mamanki? Inna ta tambaye ta.

tana gida yanzu ma gurin kitso zantafi aykoni akayi gurin su aunty.

Allah sarki shiga suna dakin su, cikin hanzari kuwa tanufe dakin su jidda da sallamar ta, 

Zalifa ce tace eye siyama baby ina zuwa haha? 

murmushi yarinyar tayi dan jinin su yahadu da zalifa sosai.

wllh aunty wai wanine ke kiranki mai mota. 

Edo suka zaro tare da cewa kardai laqaqin can bai tafi ba, 

Dafe kai safeena tayi tare da cewa ammako da yacika sarkin naci. dan allah zalifa kije kisan yanda zakiyi dashi kar malam ya iskoshi a qofar gidan nan rayukanmu su 6ace kawai a banza..


Bayan zaleefa tafita itada siyama ta nuna mata wanda ke kiranta ta, daga nan tawuce war ta gurin kitson ta. 

Cikin fuska ta nuna tashin sani zalifa ke duban mutumin da tagani bakin mota a staye. a hankali tamasa sallama suka gaisa sannan tace"malam lafiya kuwa Kake Nemana? 

murmshi driver Saif yayi mai kayatarwa kafin yace" lpy qlw 'yan mata, qanneku kenan basu isar da saqon da nabasu tin dazuba kenan?

Munjima gurinnan atsaye yalla6ai ne yazo ganin marar lafiya da tayi jinya kwanakin baya. 

Tinani zalifa ta tsaya yi sanan tace wa Keenan?  sannan kuma kai kaikadai naganka banga wani yalla6ai ba? 

Hakane gurin 'yar uwarki wacce tayi rashin lafiya har wani yataimake ta, ko kwana baya munzo nan kumashi wanda yazo ganin ta yana cikin Mota.. 


Habawa ay zalifa najin haka taruga aguje cikin gida dan yanzu kam tagane wanda yaxo gurin safeena ranar mai kyau dinnan 😉

Babu ko sallama tafada musu cikin daki, dikan su sai da suka tsorata, kafin jidda tahau masifa, amma ko saurarin ta zalifa batayi ba tace wllh yadawo tafada tana saukar da numfashi. 

atare sukace wanene? 

kallon safeena zalifa tayi tace Handsome dinnan da yataimakeke shine yanzu haka a qofar gida.

da sauri tafige gyalen ta tafita dan tasan data tsaya zaya iya barin qofar gidan, tana kusan fita gidan, ta dai dai ta kanta saboda tanason taja aji sosai dan karya rainata.....

*N͎a͎ s͎a͎d͎a͎u͎k͎a͎r͎ d͎a͎ w͎a͎n͎n͎a͎n͎ p͎a͎g͎e͎ d͎i͎n͎ g͎a͎b͎a͎ d͎a͎y͎a͎n͎ s͎a͎ g͎a͎d͎u͎k͎ w͎a͎n͎i͎ m͎a͎s͎o͎y͎i͎n͎a͎ k͎u͎m͎a͎ m͎a͎s͎o͎y͎i͎n͎ W͎A͎N͎I͎ J͎I͎N͎K͎I͎R͎I͎,  w͎l͎l͎h͎ ina q͎a͎u͎n͎a͎r͎k͎u͎ n͎i͎m͎a͎ a͎z͎u͎c͎i͎y͎a͎ t͎a,͎ A͎L͎L͎A͎H͎ dai y͎a͎͎b͎a͎r͎m͎u͎ t͎a͎r͎e͎ a͎m͎e͎e͎n͎* 


              *(14)*


waje tafita gaba daya tana mai dibi-dibi tamkar wacce ke neman wani abu, bayan daka fito daga gidan motar na facing din gidan,  sarai ta kula da motar amma tayi tamkar bata ganta ba, 

dik abinda take yana kallon ta daga cikin mota, yana mamakin kansa yanda akayi yadamu da yarinyar nan amma shi yasan dai kawai yana tausayin ta ne baya ga hakan ba komai aransa. 

(Aྂnྂaྂsྂhྂiྂ tྂiྂnྂaྂnྂiྂ kྂeྂnྂaྂnྂ)

toh dik wannan lokacin naka daka 6atar a qofar gidan su, bayan komai naka atsare yake, sannan kai baka jira sai dai ajiraka, shi dik wannan na mene?

d'ayar zuciyar ta tambaye sa, girgiza kansa kawai yayi a hankali bai ce komai ba, saboda shi kansa baida wannan amsar. 


driver ne yatako har gurin da safeeena take a tsaye sai da taga yakawo dab da eta, sai tayi kamar zata juya ta shiga gida dan tasan dole ya kirata dik wannan tana yinshi ne saboda gudun raini, amma bahar azuciyar ta ba, sosai take da buqatar ta sanyashi a edon ta, batasan meyasa hakan ba. 


da sauri Ck driver ya tsaidata tare da cewa am.... 'yan mata pls 2mnt, jiyowa tayi tare da shan mur"tace lafiya?

washe baki ck yayi tare da cewa lafiya qlw ranki ya dadi, daman tin dazu ne mukazo tare da yalla6ai domin ya duba lafiyarki,  wanda ya taimaka miki dinnan, 

ciki da gamsuwa tace ok nagane toh yana enane?

Yana mota ck yabata amsa. muqarasa madan ku gaisa. 

yamutsa fuskar ta tayi tare da kallon ck tace"inace kace zuwa yayi ya duba ni, shine kuma ni zanje gurin sa, oh'nice ma zanje gaidashi bashi bane zaizo ba kenan?

Ummm tohm baxanje ba ni zan shiga gida saboda enada aykin yi... 

Zaka rantse da Allah har azuciyar Safeena wannan zancin da take gaskiya ne, nan kuwa dik fargaba tacika ta, Allah Allah take ck ya tsaidata ya lalla6ata tare da bata haqurin kafin taje, saboda zuciyar ta azabtuwa take da son ganin sa, batasan meyasa ba,  uwa uba muryar sa da take son taji shin ya take dan kokadan bakin sa bayako motsi bare yayi magana. 

Addu'ar da take aranta takuwa kar6u ck yashiga magiya da ban haquri sannan fa ta yarda zataji... 


He he he dadi kawai safeena takeji aranta, wani gefe kuma gavan ta ne keta faman fauwa tohh ko meye dalili?

OHO....🙅🏻‍♀


Esarsu kenan gurin motar sai c.k yayi knocking din glass din motar a hankali ya jaye daga gurin yakoma can gefe ya tsaya. 

yadan dauke tsawun lokaci kafin ya saukar da glass din motar sa. amma kansa nakan jarida da yake karatu, ganin haka da safeena tayi ne yasa etama tajuya masa bayan ta tabashi qeya,

Koda ya dago yaga yanda tamasa mamki abun yabashi toh me yarinyar nan take nufi shi zata bawa qeya dan ma tataka babbar sa'a yazo duba lafiyar ta, shine ko gaidashi kenan bazata iyayi ba, toh me take tinani ko ta dauka wani abune yakawo sa gurin ta, wani siririn tsakine yaja, wanda sai da safeena tajishi har cikin ranta, saboda ta tsane tsaki a rayuwar ta, umm kenan eta zaiwa tsaki dan ma yasamu tafito?


koda duniyar nan zata taru akansa batama taso yafarawa koda yayar sa da suka fito ciki daya da etaba bare wannan, dan haka koda zai rasa sukuni edan yakoma gida sai da yarasa amma dik wata mace komai takeji dashi tayi kadan ya saurare ta bare ma har yamata magana.


zuciyar ta keta azalzala ta akan ta daure tamasa magana ko babu komai fa gurin ta yazo kuma lafiyar ta, yazo dubawa, toh in banda ma abin ta kamar wannan yazo gurin ta har zata iya tsarawa akan dole sai yafara mata magana, hanquri tabawa zuciyar ta akan tamasa magana dan tasan yanzun nan zasu iya wucewa abarta atsaye ita kuma kokadan bazata so hakan ba... 


A hankali ta juyo fuskar ta na gefe cikin wata eriyar murya wacce ita kanta batasan tanada eta ba, 
tace INA WUNI?

yi yayi duniya tamkar ba dashi take magana ba ko baiji ba, amma sarai yajita, amsawar ke masa wuyar fad'a, 

a zuciyar safeena kuwa bataji dadin yanda yamata ba sam"kuma tasan babu wai yama jita. 

kamar daga sama taji ya amsa cikin sansayan muryar sa, amma fa kansa nakan jarida dake riqe a hannun sa, jintayi yace" LPY. 

kuma sai sukayi shiru na tsawun wani lokaci kafin yakoma motsa bakin sa da qyar yace" koda yake basai ma na tsaya tambayar ki jikinki ba, alamu sunnuna kinji sauqi,  tinda gashi har anfara fita yawo ana samun manema suna kawo mutun da mota har a qofar gidan su, 

abin da nake son mutun yagane shine kokadan wannan ba darajar sa bane kuma ba darajar gidan su bane, akwai hadari aciki shiga motar saurayi ko ta wanda baka sani ba, domin hakan naragewa mata daraja da qima, a edon duniya, ni koda mutun baiji dadin abunda nafad'a masa ba, ni wannan ba damuwa ta bace  gaskiya ce, mutun dai yakula yatsare mutuncin sa kansa shine yafiye mishi komai a rayuwar sa, bangaya wa mutun magana dan yadaina abunda yake ba, na dai fad'ane kawai saboda korewar ganin haqqin IDo, nabarki lapiya... 

Dik magangaun da Saif keyi kokadan bai dago kansa bare ya kalleta edasa yanzu nakan wayar sa, yana danna. 

daga haka yaja bakinsa yatsuke tare da mai da glass din motar sa yarufe,
 

tsananin mamaki da al-ajabi ya hanawa safeena koda qiftawar edo bare takai da motse, shin da mamakin sautin muryar sa dake fita mai matuqar ratsa sassan jiki zataji, koda maganganun da yake fad'a mata, tohh kenan me yake nufi? kenan yaga lokacin da jabir ya saukesu amota,? kuma yana nufin daman aykinta kenan shiga motar saurayi,


uwa uba abunda ya tsaya mata arai shine wai yanda ya dinga magana yana anbatar mata mutun, kenan ita yake nuf, saboda tsabar wulaqanci yana nufin eta bata ko kai ya kirata macce ba,

driver ne yakatse mata tinanin da take na aje mata wasu jibga jiga ledodi kusan hudu shaqe da kaya baice mata komai ba ya ajeye tare da zagayawa yashege mota yajasu sukabar gurin.

tamkar tafasa ihu take ji shi wannan mutumin wani erin kala ne,  miskilancin shi da ezzarsa sunyi masifar yiwa kansa rawani, 

Kallon kayan tayi jinge a gaban ta rasa yanda zatayi dasu, tasan dai ko hauka take bazata fara shiga da kayan nan cikin gida ba, edonta ne yakai kan qofar gidan su siyama daman suna shiga gidan sosai suyi fira saboda anty fati batada matsala erin maccen nace maison mutane shi yasa suke matuqar d'asawa, 

Aykuwa saiga wasu yara sundawo daga makaranta su biyu sunata fira abin su, saurin tsaidasu tayi taroqesu akan su taimaka mata su daukar mata kayan nan sushigar mata dasu gidan can tafada tare da nuna gidan anty Fatima, bako musu suka dauke kayan nan etama ta dauke wasu suka shiga cikin gidan dasu. 


Garin yakeji gaba dayan sa yaqone masa, da bari zaiyi sai gobe sannan ya koma bauci, sam" wannan tafowar tashi bata masa dadi ba, saboda yayi edo biyu da abunda yau kokadan bazai barsa ya runtsa ba,  


      ************


Ehu kawai kake ji cikin dakin anty fatee bayan anbaje kaya safeena taje gida takira su jidda, tabbas dole ne abun ya daure maka kai yakuma baka tsoro erin yanda aka jibgowa safeena 'yar banzar siyaya tamkar za'a bude shago wai kayan ganin marar lafiyane haka, abin baigama basu tsoro ba sai da sukaga wata leda qule da wani abu aciki, saurin budewa su zalifa akayi jiki na rawa sai ga Rafar 1000 guda tare da wani abunda sai da yasa suka zauna bashiri... 


Kallon kallon suka shiga yiwa junan su wanda gaba dayan su, abun yamatuqar firgitasu feye da tuninin mai karatu, saboda ko wacce su tinda Allah ya halicceta bata ta6a toh zali da shi ba, a edon ta...... 



*Wannan Kenan😘*

                   *(15)*


Anty Fatee ce tafara magana bayan ta 6oyar da mamakin ta, cikin sanyin murya tace tabbas wannan wayace mai matuqar tsada iPhone 5 ce sabuwa hull cikin kwalin ta, 

su jidda tinda suke basu ta6a ganin ta koda da edon su ba, sai dai sunajin ana fadar wayar da sunan ta, dan ko yayyunsu basu ta6a gani da eta ba, 

Kai harma aunty Fatee ita kanta bata ta6a sanya wayar a edon ta ba, dik dako ma'aikaciyace amma sai dai suyi ta firar wayar tare da qawayin ta,


Ko waccen su zuciyar ta sai faman saqe-saqe take mata, bama kamar aunty fati, saboda taji su jidda nayawan labarin wannan guy shi yasa koda taso taji labarin suka sanar da ita komai, toh ita kanta mamakin take wannan me yake nufi kuma wani erin kudi ne keda shi da har zai danqarowa 'ya mace wannan tsadadiiyar wayar bayan kuma ba sonta yake ba,  saboda kawai taimako,

kai gaskiya wannan akwai abunda yake nufi ko wane da nashi zancin zucin da yake har dai nazeefa ta numfasa tace"gaskiya wannan mutumin ninasan ba abanza ba, tabbas akwai wani abu a zuciyar shi... Nima haka nake tinani Jidda tafada cikin mutuwar jiki..


etadai uwar gayyar bata samu damar cewa komai ba, dan itadai tasan wannan kyautar babu wani dalili illah kawai yana matuqar tausayin ta ne, yama za'ayi wannan hadaddin mutun maiji da aji da ezza ga kudi hade da kasaita zai mata wani abu dan kawai wani dalili nashi, gaskiya ba haka bane. 

domin kuwa wannan ko d'arin ta yana eya siya yararrabawa masu so dan ba komai bane agurinsa, tohh dan haka eta tasan ba komai aransa, kuma zatayi qoqarrin kauda komai a ciki zuciyoyin 'yanuwanta game da hasashin da zuciyoyin su suka fara musu na qarya,  dan wannan kam yafi qarfin ta nesa ma ba kusa ba...



Haka dai suka gama dik wasu shawarwarin su, suka tashi tare da yanke shawarar abar kayan gurin aunty Fatee harsu same mafita yanda zasuyi dasu, wasu kayan chocolate 🍫  kadai suka diba suka yiwa anty sallama tare da dibar mata nata, suka nufe gida dan kada anemesu...


*BAYAN SATI DAYA*

tsaye suke dukkansu dauke da kayayyake suna neman abin hawan da zaikasu tasha, yayin da su jidda sukayo musu rakiya ji suke tamkar kada su, tafi d'an uwa da dadi yake, haka gefen Safeena da Dija yake ji suke tamkar suyi zamansu, amma ina umma tamatsa musu akan sudawo gida hakanan, saboda taga abin nasu bana qarewa bane, 


da qyar suka same mai napep sukayi jinga dashi aka sanya musu kaya ciki sannan sukayi sallama dasu mazefa harda 'yar qwallasu ta rabuwa😥


        ***********


Ayaune iyayin Saif sukazo Adamawa dan nemawa Saif auren Haneefa, kasancewar ansan junane yasa ba'a wani tsayawa binceke ba, aka bada Haneefa tare da sa ranar biki, wata biyu masu zuwa... 

Musamman wannan rana sai da Haneefa tahad'a wani dan qaramin party akaci akasha aka chashe dan tayata farin cikin wannan rana, da yawansu sukaji tamkar sune dan duk cikinsu ba wacce batasan Saif ba. 


Mamy takasa zama sai tura saqo da mutane qasashin waje take akan ahado mata Lefe naji da fadi wanda sai duniya ta shaida hakan, amma wanda akeyi domin sa kwata-kwata baya cikin natsuwar sa, yarinyar ta matuqar tsaya masa arai yarasa yanda zaiyi yaji dadi aransa.


yayi ta faman gwada layin ta akashe mai yuwa batayi amfani da wayar ba, dan saida yasa aka mata komay sannan yabata eta

gashi yasa anemomasa bayani akanta antabbatar mishi nan gidan da tazo gidan qanin mahaifinta ne kenan ita ba 'yar gari bace, tohh kenan ita 'yar inace??  zuciyar sa ce tabashi amsa da cewa saika bincika kaji tinda kadamu da sanin Hakan....



Lov u All my fans😘🌹

          _*WANI JINKIRI...*_

 *Written by* ✍🏻
 
         *Sa'adat Alkali D/tsafe*

*🌈KAINUWA WRITES ASSOCIATION*🤝🏻📚📚


*Kin zamo ta musamman daga cikin mutane na musamman da na ta6a had’uwa da su a rayuwata, kin shiga zuciyata kin zauna ta yadda ba zan iya mantawa da ke ba har abada. Ina fata da burin kasancewarmu tare a daula mafi tsari da kyawun burgewa wato Aljanna. Cikin nishad’i da yalwatuwar murmushin fuska nake mai furta miki cewa INA TSANANIN SONKI AISHA😘 💕Mrs MaKama*💕




                      *(16)*



Fira suke tafaman zubawa ummansu tana tsakiyar su, a zaune sai faman murmushi take musamman da taga yaranta cikin qoshin lafiya tare da nishadi a tattare dasu. 

sai ga su Salim qanninsu sundawo daga makaranta ay aguje suka rungume su safeena suna musu oyoyo da qyar suka 6an6are su, daga jikinsu suna dariya dukansu.

saleem ne yafara cewa"Adda Safeena yaushe kuka dawone?  shine baku fada mana ba muzomuku da 'yan tsaraba daga makaranta. 

shafa kansa safeena tayi sannan tace ayya Salem tuba muke kaga ko umma batasan zamu dawo ba ganin mu kawai tayi,  kubar tsarabar ku mungode kunji, 

toh tunda kungode adda, mu ina tamu tsarabar? 


dariya suka komayi wannan karon Deja ce tayi saurin kar6a zancin da cewa" me kuke ci nabaka nazuba yanzun nan zaku ga tsarabar ku mai yawa. 

Aye tsalle suka hau famanyi dadi dik yacikasu. Ummace tace toh kuje kucere kayan makarantar sai kuzo kuci abinci sannan abaku tsarabar dan yanzu idan anbaku ba abincin zakuci ba.. 

Yau kam" ba'ayiwa umma musu ba, sakamakon kullum sai tayi da gaske sannan suke cin abinci..

*********


tashi sukayi suka dake kayan da sukazo dasu suka barwa umma saqonta da su Inna da mallam sukace akawo mata suka nufe dakinsu dansu watsa ruwa agajiye suke matuqa, gashi dole sai sunyi gyaran dakinsu tinda basu sanar da umma cewa yau zasu dawo ba bare ta share musu.


*Bauci*


tindazu nake fama dakai akan kayi Brkfast amma katisa abinci a gaba kaqi kaci, wai menene matsalarka ne? 

kodan batun auren ka ne daya taso dik kabi kawani canzawa kasawa ranka Damuwa


Hmmmm.. numfasawa mamy tayi tare da tsaresa da edo tace Saif idan dan wannan shine damuwar ka tohh kayi gaggawar cireshi aranka matiqar kuwa kanason ganin dai dai arayuwarka,  tinkafin daddy ku yalura da halin da kake ciki afasa wannan aure. dako ka fuskance fushi mai tsanani daga gurina.


Maganafa nake da kai kaman shiru, Saif kasan fa natsane wannan halin naka Ko? 

jiyake tamkar kansa zai buga tsabar ciyon da kansa ke masa, kuma dik hakan yafarune sakamakon rashin esashin bacci da da rashin natsuwa dik yakawo masa wannan,

cikin yanayi muryar sa yafara magana wanda kayi kadan kaji me yake fad'a, ko mamy dan tasabane da yanda yanayi maganarsa yake ne yasa take fahimtar sa. 

No mamy kokadan wannan bayako cikin jerin list din matsalan dake Damuna dama wannan ne da dik yafiye min sauqi akan abunda Ke damun gangar jikina da ruhina, plss mamy kitayi da  addu'a wllh ina cikin tsananin damuwa da rashin natsuwar zuciya. mamy dafa qirjina kiji yanda nakeje ko zaki fahimce kadan daga cikin abunda nake ji, gaba daya magana yake cikin wani azababbin yanayi da shi kansa kadai yasan yanda yake ji. 


Janye hannun ta mamy tayi yayin da take jin qirjinsa nabugawa da sauri, tsoro ne yashigeta sosai, zuciyarta ce tafara saqa mata kodai danta yafada tarkon sone, da sauri ta kauda wannan aranta tana mai nanata ba zaimayuba dan gara komai zai faru yafaro amma bazata ta6a barin hakan yakasance ba.. 

cikin tsananin tausayawa take kallon sa, sai yanzu ta lura da yanda Saif ya rame yaqara wani erin haske dik yawani komawa wani eri tohmeke damunsa, tasan halin danta da matuqar zurfin ciki yanzu koda ta tambayesa abinda ke damunsa ba fad'a zaiye ba koda ko zata kwana d'ari tare dashi tana tambayar sa, abunda bashi bane zai fad'a Mata..


dafa kansa tayi cikin tsananin so da kulawa tace, kayi qoqare ka daurewa zuciyar ka akan dik wani abu dake damunka kuma kaci gaba da addu'a nima zantayaka allah yashige maka gaba yakuma maka maganin dikkan matsalolinka amma kadaure yanzu kaci abinci dan ko rashin cinsa babban ciyone,


amsa mata kawai yayi da tohm zaici a ransa kuwa fad'ar yake bazaki gane bane mamy, 

a zucyar mamy kuwa itama cewa take, yaro Man kaza na dik san damuwarka bai wuce wannan auren kuma sai anyishi yaro saikace marar lafiya ace kwata-kwata shi mace bata bashi sha'awa a rayuwarsa yafison ya zauna hakan muna kallon sa, yana kallon mu tohh banzai yuba. 


*1 Month later* 


Takama kenan saura wata daya a bikin Saif, har ankai kayan lefe naji da fad'i komai anyishi cikin tsari da tsantsar riya, anyita surutun kayan har angaji anyi shiru. 

da yanzu zuwa wannan lokaci labari ya esowa Saif yarinyar da yasa amasa bincike akanta takoma gidansu tin bayan wata daya da suka wuce, sannan kuma ankasa samun sunan garin da take, gashi har yanzu wayar da yabata yakira taqi shiga har yagaji ya daina. 


Ayau jirginsa ya daga ezuwa qasa mai tsarki dan tafiya yowar umara. ko zai rage jin abunda yakeji.


*******

Kwance take kan dan madaidaicin gadunsu da suke Kwana, dik zafi yadameta, ga tinanin mutunmin nan yatsaya mata arai dashi take kwana dashi take tasahi tarasa yanda zatayi tacireshi aranta, tadai san ko amatsayin 'yar aykin gidan sa bazata shiga layi ba bare har takai matakin da tinanin ta a kullum yake yaudarata Akansa, 

Amma kuma ay SO ba qarya bane, haqiqa tayi matuqar zurfi cikin son wanda yafi qarfin qarfinta, wanda zuciyar ta kulum cikin azalzala mata sonsa take, Ya Allah gani garka tafad'a afili yayin da hawaye suka kwaranyo akan kumcin ta masu matuqar quna...
