          _*WANI JINKIRI...*_

 *Written by* ✍🏻
 
         *Sa'adat Alkali D/tsafe*

*🌈KAINUWA WRITES ASSOCIATION*🤝🏻📚📚


           *(10)*


Firace suke ta faman zubawa har dare yaraba basuyi bacci ba, sai faman debewa Haneefa Albarka suke tare da allah ya Isa, bancin haka babu abunda zasuyi dan bazasu iya ja da ita ba, sai dai allah yafi qarfin ta, 

a haka dai har bacci yasace su dikan su, gaba dayan su, har safeena wacce take jin ta tamkar anmata shegen duka tsabar yanda take jin jikin ta yamata nauyi. 

Abin mamaki bacci tayi mai cike da mafarkan wannan had'ad'in guy wanda ko sunan sa bata sani ba. 


     ***************


Zaune yake bakin gadon dakin sa dake cikin wani hadaddin Hotel dafe yake da kansa wanda yake ji tamkar yafado tsabar buga masa da yake, 


Maganar da sukayi da mamyn sa ne take ta faman masa yawo a qwaqwalwa, tabbas uwa uwace kuma Allah yafifita ta fiye da uba amma yasan cewa yarinyar da mamyn sa keso ya aura hankalin sa kwata-kwata bai kwanta da ita ba, kuma yasan mahaifin sa sam ba erin wannan yake mishi sha'awa da ya aura ba, amma tohh ya zaiyi dole ya danne zuciyar sa yabi umarnin mahaifiyar sa, 

Tsarinsa kokadan bai tashi yin aure a yanzu ba, har sai yasamu wacce yaji yanaso a ransa wanda yasan cewa da wuya idan zatazo nan kusa saboda bai faraji ajikin sa lokacin zuwa ta yayi ba. 



yayi nisa sosai cikin karatun wasiqar jaki. wanda kokadan baya da wani amfani yinsa. wani qayattacin murmushine yasu6uce masa wanda koshi baisan sanda yazo masa ba, tinawa yayi da yarinyar dazu wacce suka kai asibiti yanda ta dinga faman zuba addu'a wai shi zata kallah tacewa aljani. Kurjciya da dadi take, yafad'a aransa kafin yayi qarfin halin miqewa tsaye yanufe hanyar toilet dan watsa ruwa, sosai yake ganin juwa saboda ga yunwa sannan kuma ga rashin isashin hutu da bacci da bai samu ba, wanda yaune tazo mishi da hakan... 


Ba qaramin murna da nuna farin ciki momy tayi ba, sai sanyawa Saif albarka suke, ita kuwa Haneefa tana gefe sai faman rufe fuska take adole ita wai kunya, (uhmm... daga baya kenan) 


maganar tsare tsare biki ne su Mamy suka fara tattaunawa, dan basasun adauke wani lokaci. 


zuciyar Haneefa yau jinta take fes tamkar babu abinda yata6a 6atamata rai duniya, sam har taman ta yanzu da wata wai Safeena da kuma abunda tamata, 

Yanzune hankalin ta ya kwanta zata same namiji mai tsada wanda gwargwado Allah ya mallaka masa komai, zata kece raine gurin qawayin ta, Saboda tana matuqar Son Saif a rayuwar ta. a gaskiya nayi sa'ar miji kam heee wllh naqaro wulaqanci tafada da shewa dik da ita kadai take dakin ta, amma su Mamy sunso sujiyo Ehun ta..



 *Washe gari* 


Bayan su safeena angama karyawa anyi wanka kowa ninsu yayi kwalliyar sa tamkar zasu fita unguwa, zaune suke cikin daki sai faman surutu ake, innace ta cikin dakin tare da cemusu kunsan wannan surutun ajiyeshi zakuyi kutaso zuwa aykin gida dan fita zamuyi tare da matan yayyunku zamuje qauyin mahaifin ku gurin biki dan haka yau kuke da aykin dik wannan gidan, wasu suyi abinci wasu sukama wani ayki, 

wanke wanke dai Almajirai sunriga sunyi da shara yanzu yarage naku, idan kuntsaya surutu wannan cikin ku, nikunga tafiya ta zanje nashirya, 

Ikram ce tace tare da turo baki kuma shine inna tinda safe baku daura kayanku ba kafin yanzu kungama, inamu ina aykin dik wannan gidan gsky inna saiku. 

tsabar mamaki magana ma kasayi inna tayi illa gyad'a kanta da tayi kawai tace toh kada allah yasa kuyi tawucewar ta abin ta. 

Safeena ce tace kinganki ikram da mugun son jiki kike yanzu meye laifi tinda munada dan yawanmu nan danan zakuga mungama cikin lokaci indai ba shiriri ta muka tsayawa yi ba, 


Sunaji suna gani su inna suka shirya tare da surukan su suka tafiyar su, dole su Najwa aka mike alanqosa kokadan basasun ayki ay dagangan su inna suka musu haka, saboda malam bayanan bare dole su suyi tinda bazasu fara bashi abincin yara ba. 


Tohhfa ayki yayi ayki sai faman zufa ake manyan su safeena suke abinci qananan kuma suke yanke yanke abubuwa, 

Kwatsam suka fara jin dirar motoci bakin gidan su,
Deja ce tafara cewa" kunkoji  abinda nake ji, 

Najwa tace qwarai kuwa kamar dirar manyan motoc... Bare muga ikram da Deja sukace 

Kafin su zalifa su hanasu tin sun fita waje. 

Habaaa aguje suka shigo gidan suna haki suna cewa wllh sundawo.

Suwa su jidda suka tambaya banda safeena dake tsaye tana kallon ikon Allah 

Wallah wasu mahaukatan motoci ne muka gani masu shegin kyau sunkai 8 gasunan aqofar gidannan,

Ido su zalifa suka turo sannan sukace waton daman wannan tsinana matar bata hanqura ba kena...


Basuko Ida abinda zasuce ba Safeena tafige gyalen ta dake sarkafe da qofar kiching tayi waje dan dama damar take son koma samu na yaga Haneefa gaban ta da bayan ta, 

Aguje suka bita suna kira amma ina tatafi. 

Turus" sukayi dukan su sakamakon wani hadaddin balarabe da suka gani yasha uwar wata shadda fara qal" yana sanye da gelss fari a edonsa, sai wayar dake gefen kunnesa qirar iPhone 8+ yayi kyau fiye da tunanin mai tunani da alama waya yake.

Yana daga cikin mota gelss din mota kadai aka saukar.

Suman tsaye sukayi dukkansu dan ganin wannan kyakyawan halitta da sukayi. 

Uwar tafiyar wani yawune yawuce mata da qarfi bada shiri ba sakamakon wanda idon ta yayi tozali da shi.

                     *(17)*



Tintafiyar Saif qasa mai tsarki a kullum yana cikin masallaci yana kaiwa Allah kukan sa, da yaye masa damuwar sa matuqar abinnan da yasanya wa ransa ba Alkhairi bane, Allah kayi gaggawar fitar masa shi a Ransa.


amma sam kwata-kwata jiyake tamkar kullum qara masa abunda ke cin zuciyarsa qarfi ake kuma har yanzu yarasa dalili da yasa yakejin haka game da yarinyar da sam ba Ajinsa bane,kuma shi har yanzu yakasama yarda da abinda yake tinani fadawar sa cikin wannan Halin. 

da haka har yashafe kusan 2weeks yana abu daya waton addu a har yanzu dai jiya wayau, har lokacin komawarsa gida yayi yafara shirye-shiryen dawowa gida....


      *********


Safeena ce a tsakiyar gida tana wanke-wanke yayinda Dija ke wanke musu tufafin su, Umma ko nafaman daura girki gashi lokacin da manane wuta da wuyar kamawa take..


sallama sukaji anyi tare da shigowa cikin gidan atare suka daga kansu tare da amsawa dan suga mai sallama. 

cikin iho Safeena tasake kwanaka da take wankewa ta ruga aguje ta rungume mai shigowa, dukansu mamaki da murmushi yaqi barin fuskokinsu, Deja da umma sukazan 'yan kallo tare da yima wacce safeena ta rungume kallon Sani, dija ce tace shin kodai idanuna ne keman qarya, Aunty Sakna ce kuwa.?



yarinyar da aka kira Sakna cikin murmushi tace nice kuwa Sis Dija, janye jikinta tayi daga na Safeena tare da cewa"ke ni dan Allah malama sakeni naje na gaida umma kinwani riqeni sai kace zangudu..

dungere mata kai Safeena tayi tace dan Allah ja can malama dan kinsamu nama taryoki, kada Allah yasa ki taryoni din😏


Deja ce tace"ayfa Sadaa, idan bayi kukayi ba bakwa jin dadi dariya sukayi gaba dayansu kafin Sakna ta gaida umma, suka nufe cikin daki, dole Dija ce taqarasa wanke wanken sannan taci gaba da wankin da take,


*Wacece Sakna?*


sakna 'yace ga wani mashahurin mai kudi.kuma yaya ga Alhaji Nazeer Dan kwangila mahaifin Saife, uwa daya uba daya suna matuqar ganin mutuncin junasu komai nasu kusan daya ne ba wanda zaiyi wani abu, batare da ya shawarce dan uwansa ba, SANAM shine babba yaron Alhaji Mukhtar, sai Sakna ce mai binsa kasancewar akwai tazara mai yawa atsakininsu, sannan baffa, yaro dan shekara goma, Sakna kawar safeena ce sosai sakamakon haduwa da sukayi a makarantar islamiya ta hadda, lokacin suna nan kaduna kafin sukoma bauci, 


ba wanda baisan zaman taren su safeena da sakna ba, jinisu ya hadu Sosai zakayi sha'awarsu lokacin da suke zuwa makaranta ko idan zasu dawo, yanda wani lokacin atafi ana nishadi ana fira amma idan za'adawo zaka jisu sunata faman fada ko tsokanar juna, haka suke tamkar wasu 'yan uwa najini, har qawancinsu yakai ga iyayinsu mata suna mutunci tsakanin umma da Mama, saboda Mama ba ruwanta da qyamar talaka, wataran ma gidansu safeena sakna ke kwana suyita faman rikici da tsokana har umma tagaji tasa musu ido...


sai gashi rana tsaka su Sakna suka tattara suka koma bauci lokacin suna junior scndry, rana ansha kuka da qyar aka raba sakna da safeena, suna kuka kamar me.


bayan tafiyarsu sakna sai gashi mahaifin su safeena Allah yamishi hanya yasiya gidansa na kansa dan damar gidan hayane suke, kuma tsakanin unguwar da suka taso da unguwar da suka dawo akawai nisa sosai,

shiyasa ko lokacin da mahaifan sakna suka dinga neman inda su safeena suka koma basu same wanda yasan gurin da suke ba kasanacewar ba kowane suke huld'a dashi ba,

sai yanzu da Allah yasa mai gadin gidansu sakna yasan mahaifin sakna kuma yasan mahaifin safeena asalima yasan tsakanin safeena da sakna shi yasa yanayi edo da sakna ya shaidata, aykuwa tin likacin da tasan baba isah mai gadi yasan inda su safeena suka kuma tamatsa akan akainata,  amma ina lokacin komawarta makaranta yayi yasa dole ta hanqura,

tohfa yanzu tana dawowa ta tadda rigima sai tazo, tom yau dai ga safeena ga sakna.. wannan shine labarin sakna Mukhtar..

                    *(18)*



Kai gaskiya idan muka gama cin abincin nan mudan fita muza gaya wllh zafi nakeji sosai,

harara wasa safeena ta jefe Sakna dashi tare da cewa toh shanshani har yanzu dai kinanan da d'an banzan yawon nan naki, ko? tohh shima ay zafi dole kijishi kinyo zama qasar turawa kuma kuma muhallinki badai yake danan ba nepa ma yanzu tsiya suke mana sai sunga dama suke bayar da wuta, mai yuwa hadarin nan ne dake haduwa ne yasa zafin yayi yawa,


tsaf sakna ke qarewa safeena kallo ad'age sai da tabari takai aya sannan tace "kewai ina wasa dake, nasha fada miki nifa banida muhallin da yawuce nan gidan zaki wani Hard cewa kinyo zaman turai, ba turai ba turu😉

toh! toh, anfara ko umma tafada tana shigowa cikin dakin, wllh kuwa umma ay tindazu suke inajisu shiru kawai namusu da baka shiga tsakanin su, deja ce mai magana da take faman ninke kayan da suka bushe kafin akawo nepa tayi guga. 


Ay tindaga waje nima nake jiyosu umma tafada tana mai zama a bakin gado, gaisuwa suka qarayi da sakna ta tambayeta mamanta, lafiya qlw sakna tabata amsa tare da cewa wllh umma nayi masifar kewarku, har mamana,.

Cikin jin dadi umma tace Allah sarki sakna muma haka munyi kewarku sosai bakima bazai iya fad'a ba,  haka kuma nayi matuqar jin dadi da har kika kawo mana wannan ziyara da baki manta damu ba, ace tsawon shekarunnan muna ranki, Allah yayi miki albarka, kuma ina fatan zaki dan jimanmana anan ki kwana biyu ko kafin kitafi. 


ameen ya rabbi umma, e.hh toh nima naso haka umma amma kwana daya kawai zanyi saboda uzirurukan da suka taso mana amma insha allah tinda naga guri zan diga zuwa akai akai,


toh Masha Allah, naji kince uzururuka sun taso muku kamar namefa Allah dai yasa lafiya? 


murmushi sakna tayi kafin tace a.. a lafiya qlw umma daman lalura dan uwanane tataso d'an qanin Abbana zaiyi aure satin nan mai zuwa tohh hidimace babba ke gaban mu, saboda ba'a ta6a wanke yaro namiji ba a family mu sai akansa, kinga kuwa umma akwai gagarumin biki ko? 


ahhhhh... Sosai kuwa bama kadan ba, toh Allah yasa ayi lafiya aqare lafiya shikuma bada zaman lafiya..

Ameen umma, sai kuma sakna tayi shiru can dai tace, agaskiya umma zanji dadi kuma nasan mama zataji dadi sosai idan kinji bikinnan,


dariya umma tayi har aranta taji dadi sosai irin yanda sakna tadamu dasu, toh ita ina ita ina shiga harka manya gaskiya bazaiyu ba. dik da bata haufin komai akan Mama amma tana gudun wulaqanci ga qawayin ta...


hakane sakna nasani amma kiyi haquri kinga qanninku suna zuwa makaranta kuma kinga bazaiyu natafi nabar su safeena sukadai a gida ba,  amma  ba komai safeena zata shirya taje a madadina kinga tamkar ninaje nasan kuma kema kinfison haka ko? 


tsabbd'i ay kuwa ba inda zanje kawai intashi inje gayyar sod'i ba'a gayyaceni ba sai dai dija taje amma bani ba,

toh daman wayace kije kinji nakira sunan ki kada Allah yasa din kije😏


Ehhh anqi din nace bazanje ba,

toh kinqe dan Allah malama kimana shiru🤫


ekon Allah Dija tafada yayin da idan ta kalle waccen ta kalle wannan.


sai kallo umma ta Ida, ni narasa wannan dalili Kenan haka zakuje kuna yiwa hajiya sai da Hali cikin mutane ko?


dariya sukayi sannan sakna tace a..a hakan bazaima faru ba insha Allah 

wllh sai yafaru cewar Deja tana gintse dariya

ke waye sa'anki anan kinji anyi maganar yara anan? toh kikama kanki, safeena ce mai wannan maganar..

Kingako umma daman nace miki bamai shiga tsakanin su kinji abinda adda ke cewa, gefe daya kuma duba yanda adda sakna keta faman hararana,,


ay gaba dayansu dariya tabasu, bayan sun tsagaitane sai sakna kecewa amma umma gobe zamu wuce kenan har Safeena tinda kinga lokaci ya qarato kuma inason idan munje muyi saurin kai dinke dinkin mu cikin lokaci kuma zata rageman wasu abubuwa dake kaina.

tohh yanzu dai bani aciki wannan tsakanin ku ne keda mutuniyarki kunfi kusa, yanzu nace wani abu naji kunya, a.. a ni dai abinda kawai ne nawa shine addu a ay safeena kece ita, itace ke, ni kunga tashina kuyi kugama kufito waje kusha isaka, dakin nan zafi.. 


bayan fitar ummane Sakna ta dubi safeena zata mata magana sai suka hada ido, ke miye na kallona haka?

Ankalla din ya akayi kikaga na kalleki ke idan ba kallona kikayi ba, idan fa kikaman wllh Allah ba inda zanje. safeena tace. 


Kar Allah yasa kije din sai me?

Kinkace ko?

mamaki dik yagama cika dija ga sha'awarsu da taji har cikin ranata, azuciyarta take cewa"baga tanan ba jifa yanda suke tadi kafin ajima kuma arkice.

                  *(19)*


Har gurin mota suka rako su Safeena, umma da Deja suna musu fatan isa lapiya da kuma fatan gama biki lapiya, safeena rungume Dija tayi tanajin wani abu daban wanda bata ta6a jin erinsa ba dan ko sau daya basu ta6a kwana daya basu tare ba sai yau.

Etama Deja hakane gurinta batajin dadi kokadan, haka dai suka haqura sukayi sallama bayan umma tayiwa Sakna shatara na arxiki dikda ba wani abune suke da shi ba, amma tayi qoqari, dan Sakna saida tayi ta nuna rashin jin dadin ta, amma umma tace idan har bata kar6a ba, tohh itama tadauke dik abinda tazo musu dashi ta koma dashi sun gode, saida Sakna taga kamar ran umma ya6ace da hakan, dole tabata haquri kuma ta kar6e kayan sannan fa aka zauna lapiya.


saida su umma sukaga driver yaja mota sun bar layinsu sannan suka koma cikin gida,


*wai ya labarin Amarya*?


Kokadan masu kudi basajin qyashin 6arnan da kudi saboda ganin su, suna dasu, da zakuga yanda ake almubazzaranci da dukiya daku kanku zaku jinjina lallai wannan lamari mai girmane, gyarane akewa amarya nakece raini, yanzu haka kwanan ta biyu daga dawowa daga qasar Sudan gurin gyaran jiki, yanzu kuma aka dauko wata shuwa, da 'yar kanuri kawai dan suci gaba da gyara amarya, fanin gyaran kayan mata kuwa, wata shahararriya ce aka samo tindaga garin sokoto dan tazo tayiwa amarya tsimi na musamman dan anga alama kan angon na rawa, gara akasheshi ta wannan hanyar.


Gyara kam Haneefa tashashi ba ita kadai ba har 'yan matan ta, gyarane suke erin nakece raine koba komai za'a hadu da manyan yara,

wani kyaune na mussaman ke fita daga jikin Haneefa tayi kyau harta gaji, fatar nan kuwa taqara sheqi da walqiya dik inda ta wulga wani sihirtaccin qamshine ke bayyana guri, kai abin dai ba'acewa komai,

murmushi mamy tayi kafin tace banda abinkda daddy su, me saif yake a gida har yanzu, tohh saif ma yanzu baya gidan kasan halinsa da rashin so hayaniya, wai Yakoma,  gidan Alhaji Babba, dazunnan suka tafi shida Sanam,

dariya daddy yayi erin tasu ta manya, yace" saif saishi abun kenan harda gudu daman tambayarsa nakesun yi, cikin manyan gidajinsa da kenan da kuma abuja ciki waye zai zauna aciki kinga asan inda za'a fada mawa can gidan amarya susan gurinda zasu kai kayansu..

amma bari yanzu idan nafita sai nabiya can gidan muyi magana da shi.. 


Hakanma yayi dai dai daddyn su dan kada aqure lokaci,

toh shikenan nina fita sai nadawo.

Allah yadawo dakai lafiya, etama nafeesa addu'a tamasa ta dawowa lapiya, 

Allah yasa daddy ya amsa tare da barin cikin parlour..

wani wahaltaccin kallo ne yake jifan ta dashi yayin da zuciyar sa, ke masifaffin bugawa da sauri da sauri, gaba daya yaman ta da wayar da yake yakasa koda motsa kyakyawan lips dinsa, yashiga kogin tinani ta yanda akayi wannan yarinyar tazo nan.


gefin safeena kuwa tini numfashin ta yayi qoqarin daukewa tsantsar yanda lokaci guda abubuwa da yawa suka dake zuciyar ta, da sauri ta aje basket din da tazo dashi tafita daga cikin dakin tana tafiya tana tinani.

kardai ace wannan shine yaya Saif wanda Sakna ke masifar tsoro, cak"taja tatsaya sakamakon wani mumuna faduwar gaba da yazo mata lokaci daya, a hankali ta furta cewa kardai ace shine angon da za'a daurawa aure kwanannan?

wani masifaffin sarane kanta yamata tare da dafe qirjin ta tana karanta dik addu'ar da tazo bakin ta, Allah yasa bashi bane da nashiga ukku nan take hawaye suka fara zubar mata a fuska, wata zuciyar ta ce tayi saurin katseta da cewa tohh idan MA shine ko bashi bane meye matsalar ki tinda kinsan wahalar banza ne kike koba auren sa bane meye ribar ki idan ba nasa bane kinsan dai ko amafarki kada mafarkin ki yayomiki abinda yafi qarfin ki. 

dafe kai safeena tayi a wani ginin dake gefen gidan tare da sakin wani wahaltaccin kuka mai matuqar zafi tarasa ya zatayi da rayuwar ta taji dadi wannan wace erin masifa ce ta sanya kanta da zuciyar ta aciki ne? 

Jitayi andafa ta tabayan ta ana qoqarin jiyo da fuskar ta, yayin da gaba ta ya qire yafadi sakamakon razana da tayi da hakan da taji....


sosai Sanam ya tsare Saif da edo yanda yaga gaba daya ya canza lokaci daya daga ganin wannan yariyar, toh make faruwa ne? 

sau ba adadi yake ta faman ajiyar zuciya dalilin qarfi bugunta da yaninku sau ba adadi juyawa yayi yakoma cikin daki dik jikinsa asake.

yayinda yabar Sanam cikin ko kwanto wannan baqon lamari da yafaru yanzu, toh kodai ya santa ne? 

amma bare yayi qoqari yaje yaji mekefaruwa dik da ba lallai bane yasan yasamu amsar sa da yake sonji ba.. 


Subuhanalillah me akayi miki meke damunki so yaya ne suka miki wani abu Ko? 

daman nasani hakan saita faru wa'annan da kike gani mai iya musu sai Allah mai yuwa sunfada miki wata magana da bata miki dadi ba ko? 

dan Allah kiyi haquri tawan wllh kadan ma kika gani su ba ruwansu da baqo ko dan gida idan suka tashi dizga mutun ba ruwansu ba subar kowa ba... 

ni dan Allah ki qyaleni da wannan surutu naki da hasashin da kike na banza haka kawai kiyanke hukunci bakisan meyake faruwa ba kin hau wani zance na da ban kin zauna akai, tohhni ba abunda suka man, kawai dai natuna da mahaifinane ina matuqar ke warsa a rayuwata dole nayi kuka dik sa'adda natina shi😥

Allah sarki kiyi haquri tawan kinji addu'a yake buqata kawai gurin m, ba kuka ba, dan kuka bayada wani amfani a gurinsa addu'a etace komai a rayuwa Allah yashige mana gaba kiyi haquri nima kada kisani kukan nan muje ciki muci abinci kinga hadari nahaduwa sai faman walqiya ake,,

batayi musu ba Sakna tariqe hannuta suka shiga cikin gida Sakna naci gaba da lallashi Safeena.

yayinda gefen safeena kuwa mamakin tayi erin yanda qarya kokadan bata gaban ta amma jiyanzu lokaci daya tayiwa aminiyarta qarya tare da 6oyemata matsalar ta, bayan ba wacce yadace tagayawa damuwar ta edan ba eta ba, amma banda wannan matsalar dayar zuciyar ta ta bata amsa, 


.......................................


Lafiya kuwa naga gaba daya ka chanza lokaci daya meke faruwane? 

Sanam ne ke wannan maganar bayan ya riske Saif adaki cikin wani dogon tinani 

shiru Saif yamasa wanda daman Sanam yasan za'ayi Hakan, bai matsa masa ba illa miqewa da yayi tare da cewa tohni zanje naci abinci dan yunwa nake matuqar ji edan kagama ekon ka kafito kaci idan kuma nan za'a kawoma za6i yardage naka kaga tafiya ta.


 *WASHE GARI*


Bayan su safeena sungama dukkan ayyukansu da yakamata suyi, sungoge ko ina sunshiga wanka..

suna cikin shiryawane dan suna son da zaran sun kammala Brkfast suje zuwa kai dinkuna su, dan tym yana qurewa, mamace tayo sallama cikin dakin tashigo tare da cemusu Masha Allah harkun shirya kenan tohh kufito ku karya sai kuje kukaiwa su yayanku brkfast nasu daga can ku wuce,

wannan karon safeena tafi sakna shiga cikin rud'u da qin zuwa gurin da aka aykinsu, mama batako tsaya ta lura da yanayi su ba tafita tana qara nanata musu akan suyi sauri su fito. 


Kallo kallo suka dinga kallon Junasu kafin sukwashe da dariya safeena ce tafara magana da cewa"kinga idanunki yanda suka wani fitowa K
kuwa? 


ay dole sufito saboda natsane kai abincin nan banason zuwa part dinnan nasu kokadan wllh...

Tohh yazamuyi tinda yazama dole koni da bansan halinsu ba kinsani jin shakkunsu Allah..

dariya suka tare da tafawa suka dauke kayan da zasu kai dinki tare da mayafansu suka fita zuwa parlour...

Tamkar wasu kurame suke zaune acikin parlour su, yayinda Sanam ke faman motsa jikinshi, shi kuma gogan nafaman danna laptop dinshi gaba daya hankalinshi ya karkata ezuwa abunda yake...


sundade a tsaye bakin qofa suna shawara shiga dauke da kayayyaki a hannusu ko waccin su sai faman bugu zuciyar ta kemata, sadaqarwa sukayi yayinda Sakna ce ke kan gaba taturo qofa tare da yin sallama a hankali tamkar marassa gaskiya,

shiru-shiru safeena bata biyo bayan sakna ba, sai da sakna takusan gama fidda kayan dake cikin basket sannan safeena tayi sallama tashigo..

Cak"yatsaya dayin dikkan wani abinda yake sakamakon wani bugawa da zuciyarsa tayi, rintse edonsa yayi da qarfi yana maici gaba da saurarin zuciyar sa, 

etama gefen safeena ba qaramin qarfin hali tayi ba da kayan da take dauke dasu basu su6uce mata ba, sakamakon yanda Jikinta keta faman rawa saida ta sauke nannauyar wata ajiyar zuciya lokacin da taji wani qamshi ya ziyarce ta, 

bayan sun ajiyene zasu fita sai Sanam yace Ke kuje akawai kayan jiya da kuka kawo suna kicking ku wuce dasu cikin gida..


cikin qarfi hali Sakna tace am.. a... Yaya yanzu munriga munfito fita zamuyi kai dinki idan muka dawo zamuje mudauka mushiga dasu cikin gida please.. 


Kutt"ke dan ubanki ni zaki gayawa fita zakuyi lallai yarinyar nan kin raina ni, ina wasa dake ne? 

cikin wata sansanyar murya safeena tace dan Allah kayi haquri yayanmu zamu shiga dasu inane hanyar kiching?


Kamar daga sama suka jiyo muryar Saif yace ka qyalesu kawai daman ay ba inda mutun zaije karna kuskura naji ance qafar mutun tafita anan gida..

daga haka yaja bakinshi yayi shiru yana cigaba da abinda yake, nan kuwa zuciyar sa ke faman azalzalasa akan yadaure yamata kallo ko sau daya ne, amma tsabar jin kai da miskilancin sa ya hanasa, yin hakan...

Sakna na gaba safeena na bayan ta sukaje suka dauko kayan daga kicking suka fita...


Kinga abinda nake gaya miki ko? guy nan yacika matsama mutun tohh yanzu yazamuyi kenan?

Hmmm karki damu ni zanje nakai kawai zanje gidansu Afna tinda kince guri daya kukai dinki kikirata kawai sai mutafi tare, nan da kika ganni bana gudun komai wllh 

girgiza kanta sakna tafarayi cikin rashin amincewa da wannan maganar ta safeena, amma ina safeena tanace harda rantsuwar ta akan sai taje komai zai faru sai dai yafaru dan ita bata daukar mutun yasata abunda dik bata tashi yinsa ba... 


badon Sakna taso ba takira Afna suka koyi sa'a tana gida dan jiya datazo gurinsu race tanason tafita tohm fitar batayi ba rabon  haka zata faru. 


haka safeena tasa qafarta tafita daga cikin gidan aranta ko d'ar bataji amma sakna kuwa addu'a take allah yasa kar saif yaji fitar safeena Harata dawo, gaba daya ita a tsorace take... 


Harta bar gidan gaba daya akan idonsa hakan yafaru yana sama yana kallonta, 

baiye niyar fita ba yau sam, har meeting yau keda shi yana da abubuwa da yawa da yake sonyi ayau amma dik yakauda su saboda bayason fitar..

amma yarinyar nan tasashi dole yau sai ya fita...

*wannan page din nakune kyauta sai yanda kukayi dashi, 🌹🍃WANI JINKIRI FANS 1🍃🌹*
*inajin dadin addu'ar ku haka kuma inajin dadin yanda kuke bibiyar littafina hadin kanku na matuqar burgeni Allah yabar so da qauna, tabbas ina matuqar jin dadin zama cikn ku wollah, erin sosai soasi dinnan 🤝🏻👌🏻💋*


Tambayar kafa nake gurin da zakaje kaman shiru kodai amarya ne tayi kira bansani ba shine yasa za'ayi tafiya bako neman d'an rakiya,

tamkar Sanam na magana ne da dutse uffan baice mishi ba dan yaga abin na sanam yafara zama rainin hankali...

dik wani abu da yake buqata yagama daukar sa cikin takonsa had'e da qamshin sa yaratsa ta gaban sanam yawuce wanda ko gefin da yake bai kallah ba 

Fitarsa keda wuya driver tini ya eso cikin wata hadaddiyar mota mai masifar kyau da daukar edo, nan take aka bude mishi bayan mota ya shiga ya hakimce tamakar wani basarake, (koda yake kusan haka ne) 

a hankali driver ke driving din motar ciki salon qwarewa,

anzo dai-dai Saif na fitowa titi sukuma su safeena suna shiga Napep etada Afna.

cikin salon muryar sa wanda sai maiji sosai yace kabi dik inda mai Napep dinnan yabi idan kayarda ya6ace maka wllh toh abakin aykinka,

edo drivar yazaro da qarfi tsiya wanda saida cikina yamotsa dajin erin kalamin da yafito daga oga sa, yau kusan shekara 22 yana musu ayki gidansu wanda tsantsar amincewar da akamasa ne yasa Alhaji yayar da yazama driver Saif kuma yau gashi shekara kusan 6 kenan yana jan saif basu ta6a samun matsala da juna ba, ace sai yau matuqar edan mai Napep ya yarda ya6ace masa abinkin aykinsa kuma yasan wannan maganr tabbatacciya ce,

ay babu wani sauqi driver ke aykinsa har lokacin da Allah yasa yaga mai Napep dinnan yayi parking abakin wani babba shago na dinki, wasu 'yan matane su biyu driver yaga sunfito daga cikin napep din suna yiwa mai napep magana wanda bai zama lallai suji ba, suka dauke kayansu suka shege cikin shagon sam basu lura da motar da take bayansu ba..


Kaje kacewa Mai shagon dinkin nan idan har ya kuskura ya yarda ya kar6e dinkinnan wllh saina daure shi,

jiki na rawa driver yaje ya esar da saqon mai gidansa saiga su safeena waje mai shago naroqonsu akan su gaggauta fita kafin suyi sanadin yarasa hanyar cin abincin sa, yana samu suka fita yajawo shagonsa yarufe jikinsa na rawa yaje yakai gaisuwa sunan Saif kadai idan aka fada a fadin garin bauci da sauran garuruwa ma harda wasu qasashe ba wanda baisan saif da matsayinsa ba, Saidai kowa yasan ba mugu bane amma ana gudun 6acin ransa dan hakan zai iya haifarwa da mutun damuwa...


lokacin da glass din motor sa ke sauqa ne edon safeena yakai kansa wanda ya haifar mata da wani mumuna faduwar gaba, amma saboda jarumta erin tata tadake saima tayi tamkar bata gansa ba suka nufe gurin mai Napep dinsu daman ashe cewane sukayi yajirasu, sufito suna shiga yaja suka tafi...

.komai da yake idonsa nakan jarida dake riqe a hannusa kokadan baiko kalle qofar shagon ba bare yasan da mutun aciki har mai dinkin yazo yayi gaisuwar da yake yabar gurin bai dago da kansa ba har sadda yasa aka saukar da glass din motar dan safeena tagansa bai fasa abunda yake ba, shi kwata-kwata bayada lokacin komai idan yana abunda ke gaban sa, baya ga ibadah... 


Yalla6ai suntashi mukoma bin bayan su ne? 

idan har kana son aykinka ba kenan, Sai yabashi amsa kai tsaye baiko dago ba..

ay driver jinjina lamarin yayi lallai wannan abun mai girmane yau koma yalla6ai abunda yatsiro dashi kenan, Allah-allah yake akan Allah yasa surabu lafiya kada yau qarshin aykinsa ne yazo...

(tohhh nidai nace ameen😜)


koda suka koma tsayawa wani shagon dinki inda Afna ke kaiwa aunty ta dinki hakance tafaru mai shago yarufe shagonsa yayi tafiyarsa a sanda saqo yazo masa, 

ayanzu safeena tafara quluwa da wannan abun, gefin afna kuma tafara jin tsoro dik da ba wani sani tayiwa wanda ke wannan abun ba dan sadda safeena ta gansa lokacin idonta baya gurin shiyasa bataga wanda ke cikin motar ba, 


a haka suka je shago kusan hudu amma da zaran sunje kashedi su safeena ke bayyana a waje tamkar wasu tsamaye😜😅


Tohfa nan ake yinta suna fitowa suka neme mai Napep dinsu suka rasa gashi ruwa yafara sauka ga daya lokaci daya mutane suka dauke kowa na sauri ya isa gurinda zaije kafin ruwan yagama qarfi,

tsorofa dikansu yagama kamasu ga unguwar da sukazo tayi tsit titin da suke tsaye ma akansa ababuwan hawa suntsya cak"tamakar anyi ruwa an dauke,

dik abinda suke yana kallonsu kafin yacewa driver muje..

haka motar Sai tabi gefen su safeena tawuce su sunaji suna gani..


da qarfin tsiya suka qara qarfi kukan da suke suna fadin sunshiga ukku sun lalace😍


ana haka saiga wata rantsatsiyar mota taja tatsaya mamallakin motar yafito da saurinsa yace" bayin Allah me kuke a tsaye cikin ruwan nan bayan kunga unguwar ba kowa,

dan Allah kataimaka mana kakaimu gida wllh tsautsayine yasa muka fito dan allah ka tausaya mana,

babu matsala kushigo kawai, haka su safeena suka rankaya suka shiga cikin mota bako tinanin wanda ya daukesu na qwarai ne ko mugu, sudai kawai akaisu gida...


sunkoyi sa'ar na qwaranna yakawosu har gida suka mishi godiya Afna tanufe gida itama safeena tanufo get din da zai sadata da cikin gida...


shiru kowa babu a harabar gidan hatta da ma'aikatan gidan sun same guri sunfake kafin a ida ruwa.. 


riqe yake da wayarsa a kunne naji yana cewa ba komai aykinka yayi kyau tama shigo gatanan tana zuwa 


dariya wanda yadauko su safeena yayi tare da cewa gaskiya yalla6ai yaran nan ba qaramar tsorata sukayi ba har cikin mota sai faman kuka suke suna da sunsani basu fito ba..


Ummm qarshin mai taurin kai kenan nalura har yanzu batasan waye Saif ba, amma da taso ta sani yanzu sai tafara nazari akansa kafin ta idar da saninsa matuqar zatace bazataji magana ta ba..

*Bayan kwana biyu*


Kota ina sai faman shiri ake tsakanin gidaje biyu harma da gidan Alhaji babba mutane nanesa dana kusa dik anfara zuwa ana bajewa ana kwasar gara kai harma da 'yan gayyar sod'i erinsu Haula dasu murja😜 an tattaro anzo cin arziki tom adaiyi a hankali kar akoma gida ana gudawa🤭

Ayfa su Hameed ankasa zama ankasa tsaye Huzaif kuwa tamkar shine ma ango saboda yawan zirga-zirga har wata rama yayi, toh bare kuma Sanam da komai shine tindaga kama hotel Hotel inda abokanasu zasu sauqa na qasashe dana nan Nigeria wa'anda ke nesa, ezuwa Hall din dik da ba'ayi wani ivnt shine kai abinma sai kungani...

ango kuwa tamakar ba aurinsa ba kullum cikin aykin dake gabansa yake kala Sanam yana fad'a haryayi yagaji yabari dole dan wanda yakewa ko kallo bai ishesa ba. 

        ********

A gaskiya Haneefa kam ba dama duminku wa kyau kam tayishi har tagaji matsala kawai angon ta, tinda take arayuwa bata ta6a ganin hakan ba, abinda dai dai wannan lokacin dik wani ango safe da rana dare yananan maqale da amaryar sa koda ta wayane yana fada mata dinbin farinciki dayake ciki kusantowar wannan lokaci da zumudin dayake idan Allah ya gwada mishi akai masa amaryar sa gidan sa, amma banda Saif....

abun na masifar damunta idan take6e da mamanta tayi ta mata kuka da qorafin halin data ke ciki, momyn ta tadinga lallashin ta kenan tana fada mata wuyar ta dai kishiga gidan idan har kika shiga toh ina mai tabbatar miki da cewa komai zaizo qarshe.. da hakan momy kesamu ta lallashi haneefa hartayi shiru. 

wasu daga cikin qawayin ta kuwa wa'anda ke hassadar wannan auren dadi dik yagama cikasu, kuma sunqara tabbatar wa kansu Saif yakai dik inda yakai tinda ace kamar Haneefa cikakkiyar mace wacce Allah yagama mallakawa komai amma sam bata gabansa, hakan yasa suka qara jinjina masa yacika cikakakin namiji maiji da kudi da ezza, uwa uba aji da tsantsar kyau da yake da shi.

bai ankara ba yana danna wayoyin sa koda yad'ago kansa yaga tacika misha  plt dam da abinci, wani yamutsa fuskar sa ne yayi kafin yad'ago kansa yadube ta.

sadda kanta qasa tayi tana mai jiran me zaice mata kuma.

kansa yakoma maidawa kan wayoyinsa yaci gaba da abinda yake saida yaga dama dan kansa batare da yadago kansa ba yace"daman yunwa kikeji kenan? tom ay sayki zauna...


a.. am.. a.. a wllh ni aqoshi nake kayi haquri zanrage idan yama yawa,

wani kallon da ya watsa matane yasa tayi shiru da bakin ta tare da zama bata shirya ba dik tawani marairaice fuska tamkar zatayi kuka😟

Shiru tamasa tana jinsa amma batace masa komai ba, tsaf yatsare ta da edo yana kallon ta, shi tinda yake aduniya yake kuma yawun qasashe bai ta6a ganin halitta erin ta wannan yarinyar ba,  komai nata eta da banne dana kowa yarasa menene yakeji akanta ne, yana matuqar, matuqar rasa tinaninsa , a duk sanda ya ganta.

yi tayi yanda yace ta zauna tare da duqar da kanta tana wasa da awarwaron hannu ta. 

kamar daga sama taji yace ina wayar dana baki meyasa bakiyi amfani da ita ba?


shiru tayi can dai ta daure bakin ta na rawa tace "am..aaaa ee,h daman ban iya amfani da ita bane, kuma daman ni ban ta6a riqa waya ba tinda nake a rayuwata, ko gurin da za'a kunnata ni ban sani ba, shiyasa kawai nina kaina na ajiye... 


dagowa yayi daga danna wayarsa da yake, yakai kallonsa gare ta yaga iyakar gaskiyar ta dik take wannan maganar, kuma tabbas yaji dadi har a ransa da jin wannan maganar tata, haqiqa iyayinta sunyi matuqar kyautawa kuma sun taimaka da har suka hana cikakkiyar budurwa erin wannan riqa waya, haka itama kanta ta kyautawa iyayinta, sakamakon 'yan matan yanzu dako iyayinsu sun hanasu riqa waya suke yinta a6oye. 


Jinyayi gabad'aya iyayinta da ita kanta sun masifan birgeshi, kansa kawai ya daga alamar gamsuwa azahiri. 


shiru-shiru dakin yayi safeena nason tashi tana kuma gudun tatashi tayi laife haka dai tayi ta zama, shikuma yana aykinsa saida yagama sannan yajanyo ragowar abincin nan da taci yajanyo yafara ci, tamkar wani cikkakin basarake yake cin abinci cikin kwanciyar hankali da natsuwa.. 


mamakine da al,ajab suka taru suka yiwa Safeena yawa, abincin da taci tarage har kingin na bakin ta na fitowa ace shi wannan haddadin guy kece ko ajiknsa, kodai ya aza wanine tazuba masa, kai kasa jurewa tayi sai da tayi qarfin halin cewa a..... wannan fa wanda nacine narage.. 


tamkar baiji me tace ba candai kuma tamkar mai ciyon baki taji yace NASANI...


ware idonta tayi tare da jin wani abu ya daketa azuciya saboda dadin da taji inama ace wanda ke sontane taji wannan Kalmar atattare dashi inama yanda take jinsa a zuciyar ta ace haka yake jinta, 


Allah yasani tana matuqar sonsa feye da tinanin mai tinani.. 


jin sukayi anbude qofa tare da shigowa lokaci daya  Sanam ne yashigo dauke da kaya niqi-niqi securities din Saif na biye dashi da wasu kayan a hannusu har cikin tsakiyar parlour

                       *(27)*


Wani kallo ne Sanam yafara jifan Saif dashi na erin bangane ba dinnan, amma kwata-kwata wanda ma yakewa wannan kallon baisanma yanayi ba.


kallon gefen da Safeena take tsaye Sanam yayi cikin daure fuska yace mata barnan, ay jikin Safeena narawa tanufe hanyar fita tamkar wacce tayi sata zaaka kammata haka take ta faman sauri har tafita.

duban sa yamaida kan Saif sannan yace "wai kai me kake nufine da wannan yarinyar har kabata damar zaman mana kan dinning kuma ace kaima kana zaune a gurin, ninafa kasa gane manufarka tin sanda naga kayi edo hudu da yarinyar nan naga alamun wani abu atattare dakai.

toh koma menene wllh kayi kadan kamayi gaggawar fitar dashi a ranka, waiko kamanta kaiko waye ne natina ma,?

Kansa yago a hankali tare dayiwa Sanam wani kallo wanda baya da ko fassara tashi yayi batare da yace masa komai ba yamiqe yanufe cikin bedroom dinsu cikin tafiyarsa ta isa..


takaici ne yaqara kama Sanam a zuciyarsa, yaji kamar yarufe Saif da duka ya yake neman zubda musa da ajinsu wanda dubban mutane ke ganinsu dashi bayan yasa yadauke dik wani  halinsa na daurawa kaina kuma ace yanzu yana neman bada mu, bazai tuba, shin waina me yake nufi da wannan yarinyar ne?

yanda yasan saif tin tashinsu baita6a ganin yayi wa mace koda kallo daya na kirki ba, Kai" bama wannan fuskar tawanan gefen, amma dan yana dan rainin hankali shine zai wani sanya wannan kucakar yarinyar agaba yana aykinsa, dik da bai tarar dasu suna magana ba, haka kuma bai tarar da Saif yana koda kallon ta ne ba, amma yaji matuqar zafin hakan da yagani,

wata zuciyar tashi ce yaji tayi saurin katseshi da cewa tom kai wai meye ruwanka ne a ciki,?

rinse idonsa yayi cikin jin zafi kasancewar bashi da wannan amsa yasa yabude daki yafita, yanufe part din Mama...


         ~____________~


koda safeena tashigo dakin su, ta tarar da sakna har tadawo, kallo daya tayiwa safeena taga  gaba dayan ta kamar arude take, tambayar ta tashiga yi tana cewa yau kuma me yafaru ne? 
dan nadawo na tambaye Mama kina ina tace kinje gurin kai abincin su yaya Saif, Hope ba dai wani abu suka miki ba ko? 


murmushin dole safeena ta qirqiro tare da cewa "ba komai wllh ba abinda suka man kawai dai kinsan nace miki yau banajin dadin jikinane shi yasa kika ganni haka.


Oh naman ta kinko fadaman dazu, tohm allah yakawo sauqi,

ameen safeena tace, har yanzu tana mamakin yanda akayi take 6oyewa sakna damuwar ta, naba tason tasan. matsalar ta,


kinga kayanmu can na kar6o mana saimu fara gyaran jikin kafin abubwa su mana yawa, infada miki kedai nafa fad'awa Mama dik abin yafaru na game da kayan mu da yaya saif ya kar6a, kuma naroqe ta akan tasaya mana wasu yanzu dai koda na tambaye ta "tace har ankar6o mana ke harda dinki tasa ankai mana, tace ta dauke smpl din kayana da naki ta hada dika aka kai, kinga yanzu dai alhamdulillah komai yazo mana da sauqi, 


magana sakna keyewa safeena amma sam hankalin safeena baya jikin ta, maganar ta, ta taqarshe kawai taji, amma dik bataji sauran maganganun da tayi ba. 

dadai sakna taga yau safeena sai a slow batasun firar dole ta qyaleta da zantukan ta, 

hakan yama safeena matuqar dadi shine yabata damar tinanin abinda yafaru eta da saif.

Har dakin Mama Sanam yashiga tare da yin sallama koda yashiga ya taddata  tagama sallah kenan tana addu'a, jira yayi sai da tashafa tukunna sannan yafara maganar da takawo shi. 


Mama dan Allah inason daga yau kar a koma bawa yaran nan abinci kai "komai ma, kar aqara basu sukai mana abari zan dinga zuwa ina dauka da kaina tinda shi wancan baya buqatar kowa yadauko abincin da zaici dole sai dan gida.

tsaresa da edo Mama tayi kafin tace "lafiya kuwa? 

lafiya qlw Mama kawai dai sun cika shirmene da shiririta tamkar wasu qananin yara.

shikenan Mama tace, kafin takoma cewa "wllh kama futar dasu dan kosu ba son zuwan suke ba. 

batare da Sanam yace komai ba yatashi yafita yanufe part dinsu..



Yau takama saura kwana biyu asa amarya a lalle duk wasu shirye-shirye da yakamata ayi na zuwan wannan rana anriga da ankammala su sai zuwan wannan rana kawai, tohm sai dai muce Allah yakaimuna rai da lapiya ameen,

wata hadaddiyar mota ce naga tayi parking a dai-dai gurinda nake a tsaye ganin nayi anbude mota anfito a raina nake cewa ashe ma macce ce ke driving ganin da nayi qafar maci ce tasauka a mazaunin driver, fitowar ta keda wuya, wazan gani? Aysha tace tafito cikin wata kyakyawa shiga ta alfarma tayi kyau harta gaji, yayinda kyawawan haqoran ta masu dauke da hauren maka, suka bayyana, wani sanyi naji araina ay aguje na esa gareta saboda tsabar mamaki kasa cemata komai nayi, sai kallon dana tsare ta dashi, ganin hakan da Aysha tayi ne yasa tace "kai wannan kallon fa SA'ADA,?

a hankali nace dole ne *mrs makama* nayi mamakin ganin ki ne shiyasa wallh.

rufe idona nayi nasake budewa kafin nace wllh tamkar amafarki nake ganin wannan rana, wllh Aysha ay fadar ma dadin wannan rana ko Wllh 6ata lokaci ne, kedai kawai mushiga ciki kihuta kisha ruwa idan kikayi sallah sai muje nakai ki gurin angon namu ki gansa, ba musu kuwa nayi gaba yayin da Aysha ke biyana abaya yayin da muke ciki da tsantsar farin ciki zuwan wannan rana...

wa kenan yaya, ko Mama za'a kaiwa? 

shiru yayi shi yamarasa me zaice haka dai ya daure yace baquwar gidan nan qawar sakna kakai mata kada kabawa kowa kuma kada kakuskura karanbani bakinka yasa naji kayi wata magana zaka sanine...

                       *(28)* 



Bata ankara ba taji andafa bayan ta saboda tariga tayi nisa cikin tinani, sakna ce koda ta juya a tsaye a bayan ta. 

Lpy kuwa tawajena naganki haka mekike tinani haka?

tinfa dazu nayi ta kiranki baki jiyo ba ganin da nayine kwata-kwata hankalinki baya gurin ne yasa naeso na dafaki Lpy kuwa? 

numfasawa safeena tayi kafin tace Lpy qlw ta hannu dama, kawai dai har yanzu jikin nake ji. 

tsareta da edo sakna tayi kamar mai kokwanto da maganr ta saidai kuma kawai tayi shiru ta qyaleta, tabbas ita tasan akwai abinda ke damun Safeena 6oye matane kawai tayi amma zata daure ta saka mata ido, lallai sai tagano abinda ke damun ta. 

shikenan sakna tace tare da nuna itama ba komai a ranta. 

tohm kizo muje muci abinci kada yahuce ko? 

ba musu kuwa safeena tabi bayanta har suka isa babba parlour.

gabanta ne yayi wani masifaffin faduwa saboda edonta da yasauka akan Saif yana duba wasu takardu da dukkan alama masu muhimmanci ne, rasa sukune tayi gaba daya, cikin sanyin murya ta gaida mama har qasa ta duqa ta kwashe gaisuwa..

da walwalar fuska mama ta amsa tare da cewa oh su safeena sarakan zaman daki ina fatar dai ba haka kikeyi a gida ba? 

kodai nanne kikeyin haka, tohm kuwa in hakane da banji dadi ba ay danan da can gidan naku dik daine ki dauka nanma gida kike dik abinda zakiyi a can gidanku nan ma kinada damar yinsa kinji? 

daga kanta tayi a hankali alamar taji, dan babu damar yin magana jinta take gadai tanan.

kitashi ga sakna can tana jiraki a dinning kuci abinci...

jiki ba qwari tatashi tamkar wacce kwai yafashewa ajiki, 

Hankalin ta mamã tamaida akan Saif dake aykin duba sauran takardun dake gabansa, tace "dikansu alhaji ne yabada yace kahada da sauran wa'anda ke gurinka ka ajiye kafin yasamu lokacin da zaku tattauna.. 

Cikin taushin murya yace "shikenan mama, daga haka naji kawai bakinsa yafurta kafin yacigaba da abinda yake



Da misalin qarfe 8:22 pm, gaban safeena ne keta aykin faduwa ajima kadan dá taje cikin toilet taduba wayarta da tasanya a qasan rigar ta koda Saif yakira amma shiru kake ji, har yakai 10:36 pm, shiru bai kira ba..

Hmm nidaman nasan bazai kiraba toh koya kira mai zaiceman yana wuce yaci gaba daman kashedin sá da baya qarewa tamkar ubana, dik azuciyar safeena keta wannan zance..

Sakna ce tashigo cikin daki tana kumbure-kumburin fuska daga gani wani abu aka matá,

Cikin tsokana safeena tace kekuma keda bakida aykin saí yamutsar fuska kamar wata mage😜 toh baki ko kyau da hakan dama kidaina...


tsaki sakna tayi tare da cewa kyaji dashi, ni gaskiya antakurani wai kinji mama cikin baqin da tayi daya zata kwana anan, cikin mu wai daya yakoma dakin ta sukana tare da baffa...

Ay safeena najin haka tadiro akan gado tana cewa toh allah yabamu alkairi ke ki kwana keda baquwa tinda kunsan juna zakuji dadi fira akaina da takalle ni na kalleta.. 


kafin sakna tasame abincewa har safeena tafice daga dakin, a hanya ta hadu da baquwa zata hawo sama ita kuma tá sauka, bayan sun qara gaisawa tá nufe dakin mamã.

koda tashiga har mamã tayi dakin abba yayinda baffa ke kwance akan gado yana bacci.

gefinsa tasamu ta ajiye wayarta da tindazu take qunshe cikin rigarta.

tare da sanyawa qofa key daman tindazu ta watsa ruwa bayan da tayi sallah isha'e kwanciya kawai tayi gefen baffa dan daman tafara jin bacci, cire wayarta tayi daga silent ta ajeta kai gare ta. 


bacci tafara cikin jin dadi saí taji wayarta tafara ringing tamkar zuciyar ta zata fito tsabar tsoron da taji..

numfasawa sakna tayi kafin tace dole kam nakafeki da edo tawan wllh kin masifar yin kyau.... Humumm wllh ba abanza kika tafi da yaya Saif b.... 

saurin bige mata baki safeena tayi tare da cewa waike lpy kike kuwa? bazaki daina man wannan maganar ba ko sai wani yaji, nariqeki da kirufaman asiri agama bikinan lapiya nakoma gida qlw, 

wllh kina masifar sani cikin tashin hankali idan kina wannan maganar dan daraja ma'aiki kidaina dan allah👏🏻👏🏻

da yafi kam dan yanzu da aunty meena nanan fa idan taji tsautsayi yashiga,

nashiga ukku Uzaif wannan kiran fa dakake taman lpy?

mutuminki ne yakira yace nabaki wai yakira wayarki baki dauka ba. 

kar6a tayi aranta take cewa ashe ko yau nabani da wannan rigimamen yanzu zai fara tinda yarainani, 

sama ta haye tare da kara wayar akunne ta, yanaji tanata kiran Hello! Hello!! Hello!!! yayi banza da ita, sai da tace Saif ko bakaji ne?

sannan yace menene sunan abinda kikayi?

bangane ba mekake nufi?

hmmm....... haka kawai kin amshewa mutane waya kuma ke idan ankiraki ba'a dauka kin ajiye waya kinbarta tinda ke batada amfani agurinki, shiyasa nakira uzaif nace yaje har gidanki yakaimiki wayar yace kinanan gida, kenan dik yanda zanyi ko zanji wannan by shafeki ba ko aunty?

kinsan yanda nakeji kuwa? kitaimaka kibawa yarinyar nan wayar ta, koda yake kibarshi kawai. daga haka yakashe wayar sa yabar aunty meena sake da baki mamakin duniya yarufeta.


Jinta take yau kamar amafarki, So gaba daya yagama makantar da saif yamantar dashi ko mecece take agurinsa, kai ana batun sa amarya ka alalle amma kwata kwata idan ma akace baisan meke faruwa yau ba baka musu saboda wannan baya gaban sa, lallai akwai qura nan gaba, dan tanajin Saif yamasifar fadawa tarkon so wanda shikansa bai sani ba. 

jikin aunty meena gaba daya yagama yin sanyi, tabbas ita kanta tanason ya aure yarinyar nan sabida sosai ta kwanta mata arai. haka aunty sadiya tazo tatarar da ita saida tadafata sannan tadawo tinanin ta, lpy kuwa?  aunty sadiya ta tambaye ta 

ba komai aunty meena tace daga haka suka sauka atare qasa amma zuciyar aunty sadiya nabata tabbas da akwai wani abu amma asannu zata sani.

Waikai meke damunka tin safe ka insheni dá tsakin ka, kodai amarya kakaeson gani nasanr a gyra motoci kasan fa daman saikayi haquri har akawo tá, 

banza yayi dá sanam danshi kokadan baisanya aranshi shi yanada wata amarya ba..

numfsawa yayi a hankali yadago kansa yadube sanam kafin yace, inason kadaukar man alkawari kafin nasanar dakai matsalata...

feena kuwa aranta take cewa bako yaya Saif ba, koda tafi matá kyawu a duniyar nan idan yaya Saif baya raayinka aykin banza ne...

nadiya kuwa dik tausayin Haneefa yagama rufeta ita kanta tana matuqar jin zafin halin yayan nasu nako inkula da yayi dá amaryar sa, amma aunty haneefa taji tagani ashe ko wulaqanci bata gama shanshi ba,


hakadai akafara taron zuciyar amarya ba dadi har wani daci takeji aranta amma ay akwai gobe tasan kobaiso saí yaje kamu da dinner, wannan tinanin da yafado matane yasa taji sanyi aranta. tadan fara walwala akaci gaba da shagali.

zaune safeena take yayin da aka zuba mata qunshi hannu da qafa, mai lalle ta shahara dan tayi suna ko a manyan mutune ita ba kowa takewa lalle ba, dik wanda yaga qunshin safeena sai yayi da gaske kafin bakin sa yayi qarfin halin iya furta masha allah saboda tsabar tafiya da zaiyi da tinanin ka.

da kaifa nake magana sai wani shareni kake Saif ninafi qarfin ka wulaqantani wllh kuma daman dan nasan akan wannan shegiyar yarinyar ne dan namaka magna akanta yasa kake qyaleni toh kokaso ko karka so, bazaka aure ta ba saboda mamy nasan ko zaka mutu bazata yarda ka aure ta, abinda nake son nasanar dakai shine saqone mamy tabani akan nafada ma cewa idan har ka kuskura bakaje wannan kamu da ake yau ba bata yafe maka.

*🌈 K͙A͙I͙N͙U͙W͙A͙ W͙R͙I͙T͙E͙S͙ A͙S͙S͙O͙C͙I͙A͙T͙I͙O͙N͙*🤝🏻📚📚



                       *(36)*



Sanam ne yayi sallama yashigo cikin dakin yana kallon sa sama da qasan sa, kafin ya iya furta cewa "a gsky mutume na kayi masifar kyau wannan adon haka wllh kaman dai -dai dadina fa dakai kenan ba iya sanya kaya ba, komai kasa dai dai yake ajikin ka gsky yau Hall dinnan zakasha kallo dannasan wasu musamman sukazo domin suganka sadai nataya amarya yake dan kada amata kwace 

dik cikin tsokana Sanam keta wannan maganar dan kawai Saif ya tanka masa amma gogan sai yayi banza dashi.

saboda yanzu Saif haushin sanam yakeji sosai nafarko dai yamatsa yagaya masa matsalar sa saída yafada masa yadawo daga baya yana qoshe abinda yakeso agabansa harda mishi ikirarin kozai mutu bazai same abinda yake só ba. 


na biyu kuma yagane yanzu yazama munafukin sa shike zuwa yana sanar da mamy wani abu akansa take tirsasahi tana masa masifa akan yayi abinda bayason yinsa kokadan a rayuwar sa, toh lokaci yayi dazai nuna mishi aynihin kalarsa 

tagaban sanam yabi yawuce cikin ni,emtaccin qamshin sa fuskar nan kuwa ba annuri kokadan gareta.

yana fita kamar jira ake aka bude masa mota yashiga kafin securitys dinsa suma sushiga suja suyafi.

Sanam nacikin eda shirinsa yaji tashin motocin Saif koda yayi hanzarin fitowa harsun fice gaba daya, wani murmushi ne yasake afili yace "oho dai nasan dai bani dakuwa da tini kasa anyi hakan gareni kuma dole azauna da wacce mukeso ko bakawa Allah, dadin abindai nima inada mota da yau naji haushi, sai dai kuma agefen ransa qwarai yaso su isa tare su biyu gurin kamun nan dan yasan shi kansa zaisha kallo gurin babies tinda angasa tare da Saif..


Lahaula wala quwatii... Iatace kalmar da Sakna keta faman nanatawa aranta Ashe harda fili tafito bata sani ba saida taji andan bugar mata baki sannan ta farga.

Lafiyar ki kuwa tin shigowar ki daikin nan kin ishe da wannan kalmar lafiya kuwa? 

bansani ba, sakna tafada adan zafi kafin tace banason iskanci haka kawai zaki kwa6ar man baki ina ruwanki da abinda nake fada, naga abinda yafi qarfin ganin idona kuma shikenan bazance komai ba.

murmushi safeena tayi tana dafa sakna cikin sanyin muryar ta tace "ahhhh maida wuqar tawan yi haquri bazan qara ba, menene yafi qarfin idonki da kike magana? 

cikin 'yar harara ta wasa sakna tace toh bake bace kinwani masifar canjawa, ta hannu dama wllh da cikakin sani na miki ba bazan ta6a ganeki ba, kinyi kyau harma bansan yanda zan fada miki ba, ni gsky abinnan nabani tsoro wai dai ko kece amaryar ake 6oye man ba'ason nasani, dan wllh ko amaryar tayi kadan ta nuna miki wani abu idan ma baki fita ba,.

Kalle komai naki tin da kan sutura dake jikin ki, zuwa kan komai naki ga mai makeup nan tasan aykinta tayi zaune tazane fuskar nan taki gwanin sha'awa uwa uba wannan masifaffin qamshin dake dad'a fisgar tinani na a agaskiya yau saina tambaye aunty wai meye sirrin..... 

jin da sukayi anbude qofar dakin anshigo tare da yin sallama, aunty meena ce tayi ado harta gaji ga wata danqareriyar sarka zinare da tamamaye wuyanta daka ganta zakasan cewa ita babba ce, da fara'a a fuskar ta, tadube sakna tace menene zaki tambaye ni qanwata takaina? 

qafta ido sakna yafara yi kafin can dai tadaure tace ni aunty wannan qawar ta safeena ce keban tsoro dazu nakawo mata ankon kamu dan tasa nata kince a... a akwai tuafafin da zata saka sai gashi na ganta yanzu da sutura da koke aunty tafi taki, shine nace ko itace amaryar shine ake 6oyeman? 

dariya aunty tayi sosai kafin ta iya cewa "inabada abinki sakna taya safeena zata zama amarya kina matsayin babba qawar amarya sannan ace a6oye miki baza'a sanar dake ba akan wani dalili? 

kima daina wannan zancin nima wannan shirin da kikaga inema qwarki sakani akayi..

daga haka aunty tanufe hanyar fita tare da cewa idan kungama kintsawa kufito mutafi tasakai tafice.



Shiru sukayi dukkansu yayin da tinanin maganar aunty ta tsya musu arai, kalmar da tace itama sakata akayi maganr ta sakasu tinani, toh kenan waya sakata? 


Kallon safeena sakna tayi, itama safeena haka ganin da sukayi ba abinda zasu iya cewa Junansu yasa suka tashi suka fita, rigar safeena kuwa sai biyar qasa take sharara suka sauka qasa zuwa palo.

koda suka tadda aunty waya take jin sukayi tace ok har kun iso? tom gamunan fitowa miskili tafada tana 'yar dariya kafin ta ajiye waya.

kallon su tayi har zuciyar ta take jinjina kyawu erin na safeena tohh bare yau uwa uba.

mayafin ta tadauka tare da cewa kuzo mutafi, ba musu kuwa sukabi bayan ta, har zuwa farfajiayar gida, wasu danqara danqara motoci ne suka hango kuma gurinsu sukaga aunty tanufa.

tin fitowar su ganin motocin ya qara tabbatar da zargin da take dan tasan ba makawa Saif ne.

gefen safeena ta dan kallah a gefen idon ta, taga kanta na qasa, wani murmushi tasake a fuskarta aranta kuwa dadi takeji sosai na ganin Lallai yaya saif da gaske yake.

jin sukayi aunty nakiransu suka qaraso saida ma sakna tadan daukarwa safeena rigarta da takeja, dan tsonaka saida sakna tace a hankali yanda safeena zata jiyota,  fadar take qiri-qiri yaya Saif yamai dani tamkar wata 'yar aiki yau naqare a daukar bayan riga...😜

safeena najinta ta qyaleta dan tasan tafadi hakane dan ta tankata, da haka suka isa gurin mota,  gefe motar da aunty take a tsaye tindazu tana magana ita tanunawa safeena tace "zo kishiga a nan, ba musu safeena tashiga batare da tasan wanda ke cikin motar ba, bayan aunty tarufe qofar tacewa sakna su shiga cikin daya daga cikin sauran motocin, bayan sun shiga ne aka tada suka bar gidan, baba isah mai gadi namusu fatar dawowa lapiya.. 


tin bayan da aka rufe motar take jin wani qamshi na musamman yana shigar ta, gaban ta yatsanan ta faduwa,  jin ringing din waya tayi gefen ta, bazato ba tsammani taji sautin muryar sa mai matuqar kashe mata jiki da saukar mata da kasala, a hankali yake magana bayan yadaga waya taji yace " kiyi hqr mamy yanzu haka muna hanya zuwa gurin, acan daya 6angarin kuwa mamy ce tabuga daurin ture... sai masifa take tana cewa "bayan sanam yace tindazu kafita daga gida gidan ubanwa kaje? 

dafe kansa yayi saboda jin da yayi yafara masa ciyo cikin sanyin murya yace "nabiya ta gidan aunty meena mun dauko tane kinsan mijinta baya gari kuma driver ta bayajin dadi shine tace muzo mudauko ta. 

ba wani munafunci ne dai erin naka dan karkaje gurin da wuri kafison sai ka6atawa mutane rai da lokaci, shine dik yakai ka gidan, amma ameena kam bazata saka kayi abu ba, kai din? wai ma yaushe ne kafara zuwa gidan ta da har zaka fadi haka, wai kasan saif ina dad'a fadama akan ka kiyaye ni ko? 

shi dai haquri yaci gaba da bata har yasamu ta sauqa da qara tabbatar mata cewa sunma kusan isa gurin, da haka yasamu takashe wayar ta, sannan fa Saif yasamu lpy wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya ce yiyi, aran sa yake cewa shi duniya yarasa wannan masifa erin ta mamy, shi yanzu ma yalura gaba daya kamar qiris kawai take jira dashi bai san meya mata ba.

Dik wayar da yake tana jinsa yanda yake magana abin na matuqar burge ta shi komai nasa a hankali ne, jin da tayi yayi shiru ne alamar yagama wayar kenan yasa tabude bakin ta da qyar tace mishi ina wuni?

sarai yajita amma yayi kamar baiji taba jin yayi takoma gaidashi still baice komai ba, ita kuma daman abinda yasa ta gaidashi saboda tarar dashi da tayi ciki ne dan karya ce bata gaidashi ba, amma bai yuwa ace baiji taba,  ta dik san yaji qyaleta kawai yayi...

tindaga nesa safeena zuciyar ta tayi wani erin tsinkewa ganin wani hadaddin gurin da suka nufa ga mutane nan kowa ka kalla ba sauqi, ga motoci nan eri daban-daban da yake wanda ke cikin  motar yana kallon na waje wanda ke wajin daine baya ganin naciki.. 

direct ciki motocin suka kunna kai, haba.... nan danan kaga mutane natasowa wa'anda ke waje, naciki ma sun fara fitowa jin cewa Ango yaqaraso,  kowa so yake yaga wannan tsadaddin ango kuma wace erin shigace yayo, Sanam ma yaqaraso.

su aunty meena da sakna suma finto daga cikin motar da suka shiga amma shiru Saif bai bada damar da za'a bude yafito ba.


Allah sarki jikin Haneefa har rawa yake yau zataga Saif dinta akusa da ita, dan wasu qawayin ta da basusan ko waye ango ba cewa sukayi ina zataje tabari angon yazo da kansa sannan su shiga, edo tazaro tace bakuda hankali wannan ba erin saura bane shi daban ne bayayi sai dai amasa, dik da ko erin ajina da kuke gani kukaga na sauka tom wannan yafini, daga haka ta bude qofa tafita, suma fitar sukayi dan ganarwa kansu shiko wannan wanene haka?


saida ya kammala danne danne sa sannan yadago kansa gefenta ya kallah, cikin sa'a kuwa sukayi ido biyu da juna, wani erin shock sukaji dikansu, itace tafara dauke kansa amma shi har yanzu idansa na kanta, yaune karo na farko a rayuwar sa daya ta6a qarewa macce kallo tin lokacin da yafara sanin kansa. 

bata ankara ba taji saukar numshinsa na sauka ajikin ta, kanta tadago da sauri sai akan kyakyawar fuskar sa da tamata rumfa,  ko wanne su najiyo numfashin dan uwan sa, cikin wata murya mai matuqar kashe jiki yayi qarfin halin furta, kinyi kyau sosai..

Idonta tawani warewa da saida yaji hakan har cikin jikin sa, wayarsa yazaro tare da daura kansa a kafadar ta kara dan yayi musu 1 shot a dai dai kuma lokacin da Sanam yabude mota dan gajiya da yayi da iskanci Saif nabarin mutane atsaye suna jiransa ga amaryar sa tintini tafito..

yana budewa hasken camera na fitowa alamar daukar Photo...

Sanam baisan sanda yace kutumar ubah.....  Iya qarfin sa,  ba wanda idonsa baikai akan pic su Saif alokacin da zai daukar photo, aunty sadiya, sakna,  Haneefa, nafeesat, uzaif, Nadiya, qawayin amarya, da dana ango, da sauran mutane gurin saboda bude qofar da Sanam yayi gaba daya,  danshi driver yana parking yafita... 


dik cikin su ba wanda baiyi mutuwar tsaye ba saboda ganin wannan baqon al-amari Ango Saif kwance a kafadar Safeena yana daukar pic, bayan ga amarya sa, tashin hankali wanda ba'a samasa rana, ay basu ankara ba sukaga Haneefa ta yanke jiki tafadi qasa sumammiya, yayin da Sanam yashiga neman layin mamy rai a 6ace, dan yasanar mata halin da ake ciki, Aunty Sadiy kuwa tsabar baqin ciki gadan gadan gurin motar tayo......

                       *(37)*


Tamkar wata zakanya haka ta dunfaro motor, qarasowar ta keda wuya hannu ta, tasa tare da fizgo safeena da qarfin tsiya wacce take a takure tsabar tsoron da ya lullu6e ta sai da tasake wata 'yar qara saboda yanayin jin riqon da aunty sadiya tamata a hannu, anan gurin agaban mutane ta yadata tafadi cikin tsautsayi ta koma bige qafarta gashi daman takalman da tasaka suna da matuqar tsini.

haneefa kuwa tini aka samu da qyar ta farfado bayan anjuye mata gora biyu ta ruwa masu masifar sanyi... 

shishikar kuka safeena tafara tana qarfin halin miqewa a tsaye dik ta bigi ajikin ta, aunty meena qarasowa tayi zata tadata aunty sadiya tadaka mata wata erin tsawa..kasancewar itace gaba da aunty meena kuma gata masifaffa shiysa aunty meena ke shakkun ta, cigaba tayi da cewa munafuka ay dama duk wani muna fuci dake aka shirya shi kuma wllh sai nasa mamy tayi maganin ki, sakarya kawai wace batasan ciyon kanta ba,

niko nace dik wannan abun da aunty sadiya keye na haukanta me zai hana taje tayiwa Saif tinda shine yayi, kuma gashi amatsayin sa na qanin ta amma ko gurin bata kallah ba, me hakan ke nufi? Hmmmmm... Kenan ko gaba da gabanta.


a tsaye take gaba daya kwalliyar ta yafara gogewa saboda kukan da take na wannan tozarci da yau ta janyo ma kanta ake mata.

jiyo murya Haneefa tayi wacce daman tasaba jin amon sautin mai cimata mutunci, har gabanta tazo taja ta tsaya, idon haneefa kadai zaka kalla ka hango tsantsar tsana da qiyayyar safeena dake tattare acikin su. 

Kalmar ta dake safeena sosai najin sakamakon mugayin Kalaman da haneefa tafara jifanta dasu na 

"banza karuwa wulaqantata wacce bata ajiye kanta, a inda allah ya ajeye taba, wllh kinci amanata kirasa wanda zakiyi eskanciki dashi sai mijin da zan aura? akuma lokacin da ake tsaka da hidimar biki na? agaban jama'a da sukazo dumin na? shine zaki neme kitozartani? mekike son adinga kallon mijina ne? koma meye ni yadace kewa mezaisa zaki neme kimin wasa da rayuwa tane, inason kidibi girman allah kifadi ko nawa kika bawa bokanki da yamiki ayki akan natsatsin mijina da kowa yasanshi akan tsantsanin sa, nako inkula kwata-kwata mace bata gabansa amma ke har kika iya sa, ashawo miki kansa dan kawai ki cusaman baqin ciki? 

Na roqeki da kitaimaka man kisa a warware mugun sihirin da kikasa akayiwa mijina, ni kiman koma miyene na yarda kiman, e,hh ya tsaya a iya kaina amma plsss kibarman muradina...


Edanko ba hakaba wllh wllh sai nasa an wulaqanta ki daga ke har banzan iyayinki da suka tsaya miki kike karuwan......


maganar da tahanama Haneefa fita kenan sakamakon zafafan marin da safeena ta dauke ta dasu hago da dama, wacce kukanta yatsaya chak"tin sanda haneefa tafara surfa mata zagi da cin mutunci.

Kutt'melesy Sanam yafada da qarfi shima cikin zafin nama ya wanke safeena da lafiyayin marin da saida wuta ta dauke mata na tsawon wasu seconds, hannusa yakoma dagawa cikin hanzari zai koma marin safeena kamar daga sama suka jiyo muryar sa yana cewa "wllh idan har kakuskura kakoma marinta sainasa an daure ka har abada. 

ay tini kallo yakoma sama ganin wanda ke magana ya bayyana agurin wanda ba wanda yalura da esowar sa, 

daf da aunty sadiya yaja yatsaya edo cikin ido yake kallon ta kafin yace "kisani idan har nasa aka kaita aka duba ta aka tabbatar man da cewa kinjiwa 'yar mutane ciyo? toh narantse da allah koda zan rasa komai nawa sai natsayawa iyayin ta sunyi shari'a dake.

Kuma daga yanzu dik wanda yane me yace zarafin ta dan ganin ko bata da gata toh ya tabbatar wa kansa cewa yana cikin matsala dan bazan qyale shi ba, saboda tanada cikakkin gata tinda tanada ni kuma tanada allah...


wani wulaqantaccin kallo ne yake jifan Haneef dashi kafin yadauke kansa daga kanta, magana yake cikin wani salon murya yana cewa, "Haqiqa dik wanda baya son ta toh bazan ta6a sonsa ba har abada, haka kuma dik wanda yaneme yamatsa mata toh bazamu shirya dashi ba, dan wllh zan iyayin komai akanta.

daga haka yajuya har zasu tafi sai kuma ya dan tsaya, koma kallon haneefa yayi kafin yace "kuma kalmar karuwanci da kika fada akan ta, inason ki ajeye wananna aranki. 

Juyawa tare da riqe hannu safeena da kansa yasakata amota yarufe qofar sannan shikuma yakoma daya 6angarin securities dinsa suka bude masa yashiga. 

aunty meena da sakna wacce ta dade da yin suman tsaye saboda ganin abinda yafi qarfin ganin ta, saida aunty meena ta taimka tariqata suka shiga mota a 360 sukabar harabar gurin, yayin da akabar sanam da qunar zuciya, aunty sadiya kuwa tsantsar mamki yakasa barin tayi koda qwaqwara motsi, tohh bare haneefa da takoma yanke jiki tafadi a some tsabar buguwar zuciya, 

qarshe dai haka wannan kamu yatashi ba'a yishi ba mutanin da sukazo gurin  wasun su dama koda ba'ayi kamun ba sudai buqatar su tabiya tinda sunyi edo biyu da saif wanda ganinsa ke masifar musu tsada. 

haka aka dauke haneefa ba numafashi sai hospital, yau nadiya da nafeesat kuwa sunkasa yarda da cewa wannan yayan sune saif, uzaif kuwa kyawon yarinya shi dik yafi komai daukar hankali sa, dan shi kwata-kwata bai damu da dik wani abinda yafaru agurin ba.... 

taro dai ya watse kowa yakama gaban sa makida damawaka yatsansu abaki saboda haushi...



Cikin mota kuwa yarasa yanda zai ce mata tayi shiru gashi kukan ta na ta6a ransa, tabbas yasan shi yajamata dik wannan toh meyasa shi bazasu masa ba? 

shi kwata kwata basu kyauta masa ba musamman aunty sadiya da Sanam, yanazu gashi sunmashi asarar kwalliyar sa, haqiqa zai dauke mumuna mataki akan sanam da aunty sadiya idan suka neme masa ba dai dai ba.

Kusantuwar qamshin sa akusa da hancin tane shine yatabbatar da kusancin sa gareta, 

tamkar mai rada yake mata magana.

Kinsan karki kuskura ki qara sauqar da hawanyar ki koda daya ne a edon ki, ki manta da komai da abin da yafaru, ninan na isar miki tinda allah yabaki ni, inason kibani matsayin da baki ta6a bawa kowashi ba azuciyar ki domin ni maison kasancewa dakene na har abada komai rintsi komai wuya zankasance tare dake kinason haka?

kanta ta dago tare da sauke idanun ta akan nasa, dik jitake yakashe mata jiki da kalaman sa tare da sanyin muryar sa, tohh me yake nufine har yanzu bataji kalmar da take son ji daga gare sa ba, dik da itama kanta tagama yankewa kanta cewa Sayf sonta yake amma tana son ta tabbatar..

cikin murya ta data fara dishewa tace "ko saboda me? 

shiru yamata amma idansa na kanta yana kallon yanda dan qaramin bakin ta ke motsawa a hankali gwanin sha'awa, saida yaja numfashi can qasan maqoshin sa ta iya yana cewa. 

Saboda ina tausaya miki, kuma kinada daraja agurina wannan shine kawai  ba komai ba. 

lumshi idanta saboda haushin jin abinda yace wanda bashi taso taji ba, ita dama tasani dik wannan abun da yake mata yanayi ne kawai saboda yana tausayin ta kamar yanda yafada wasune ke ganin kamar sonta yake amma ita tasan ba macce aurinsa bace, toh wanda ma zaa daurawa aure da isashiyar mace, tohh ita wacece? itadai gaba daya zuciyar ta tagama cutar da ita nafadawa son wanda yafi qarfin qarfin ta.

ganin da yayi kamar tayi zurfi cikin tunani ne yasa ya koma gurinsa tare da kwantar da kansa, shima lumshin idanunsa yayi yana mai jin haushin kansa na meyesa bazai iya sanar da ita abinda ke ransa ba, inaaa bazai iya ba yana gudun girman sa yafadi a edon ta..

_tohhh muje dai mugani idan miskilancin ka zai fidadda ka.._

                       *(38)*


Cikin sanyin jiki ta sake qofar amma bata komama, mazaunin taba, bayanta yabi da kallo gurin da akayi yankar rigar sai akabar gurin abude, ganin yayi fatarta tana wani erin sheqi kamar ka qwanta jini yafito aransa yake cewa lallai wannan shine aynihin kalar jikin da akeso a gurin mace.

Kauda wannan tinani yayi tare da buda bakinshi kamar wanda akayiwa dole yace "nizaki juyawa baya? 

gaba daya yagama saukar mata da kasala tanajinshi amma bata jiyo ba, dan haushi miskilancin sa had'e da girman kansa sunfara esar ta, ga zuciyar ta dake mata wani masifaffin bugawa tasan matuqar tajiyo ba abinda zai hana yahango qwallah datake ta qoqarin 6oye masa saboda haushin amsar da yabata wacce ba ita take son ji daga garesa ba, gashi gaba daya yagama kasheta da qamshin sa ita gaskiya fita zatayi sai dai yaji haushin ta, tana gama wannan tinanin tabude mota tafita.

da gudun ta tayi cikin gida sam bata kula da aunty da take gurin motocin ba tare da securities. 

Dama jira aunty take dan taga fitowar safeena dan tanason suyi magana da Saif, qarasowa tayi har gurin motar sa da safeena tabari abude, shiga tayi tare da dafashi wanda sam baiji shigowar taba saboda tinanin da yafada yasan yanzu labari yajewa mamy yayi mamaki ma da bai ga kiranta ba, har yanzu.

cikin tausaya halin da yake ciki aunty tafara magana cikin tattausan lafazi tana cewa "Saif muna cikin tashin hankali wanda banma san yanda zan kwatan tashi ba, tabbas munyi kuskurin tafiya da safeena a gurinna dan da duk hakan bata faru ba. 


inason komai mamy zata cema kayi haquri daman can kai mai biyayyane gareta amma yau gaskiya sai kaji dacin mamy fiye da tsanmani, nima kuma nasan hakan take agurina. 


Har aunty tagama dik maganar da zata fada baice mata komai ba, har saida yaga dama dan kansa sannan yake cewa "aunty ni dik wannan wllh bai dameni ba, babu abinda yatsaya man arai yanzu erin edan natina marin da Sanam yayiwa yarinyar nan, kar kiji yanda naji wllh alokacin da naga hannusa yakai jikin ta, jinayi tamakar zuciyata zata buga tafaso qirjina tafito, haqiqa marin ya tsaya man arai sosai..... 

galala aunty ke kallon sa, saboda tsabar mamaki, kafin tace "Lallai saif yanzu ina gananma erin tashin hankalin da muke ciki amma kai gaba daya ma wannan bai shafeka ba, kai tinanin da kakeyi da ban kenan ko? 

Wai Saif wani erin sone kake yiwa wannan yarinyar ne? 

nima bansani ba, yabata amsa kai tsaye "wllh nima aunty bansani ba kawai nidai nasan itace farin cikina...

toh amma kuma saboda tsabar miskilancin ka shine ya hana ka kasanar da eta, karfa kayi gangaci tazo qilama tanada wanda take so kaga kuwa ba zancin fin qarfi. 

arazane yadago kansa wanda saida ita kanta aunty ta tsorata amma sai tadake bata nunaba,  cikin wata eriyar murya yace "please anty karki saki man wannan maganar kuma dan allah kisauka zamu wuce kada narasa samun jam'e. 

abu daya zakiman shine kikulaman da lafiyar ta edan har bataji ciyo ba, yana gama fadar haka ya dauke kansa..

etadai aunty meena qyalesa tayi tafita, dan ita yanzu Saif tsoro yake bata dan talura tabbas maganr sa ce da yake cewa zay iyayin komai saboda safeena..... 

babu 6ata lokaci sukabar gidan suka kama hanyar aynihin gidan su Saif dan yafison komai zai faru yafaru..


cikin takunsa yaqaraso har cikin babba palo, akwai qwarorin mutane ba sosai ba kwance cikin Palo wasuma har sunyi bacci, sama yanufa dakin mamy, murda qofar yayi yashiga tare da yin sallama wacce ta tsaya a iya fatar bakin sa kadai.

Mamy ce zaune palon ta aunty sadiya kuwa tana matsa mata qafafuwan ta, tana mai cigaba da bata labarin abinda yafaru sai ga Saif yashigo, shigowar sa baisa aunty Sadiya tadaina fadawa mamy qarya da gaskiya ba, harda cewa tayi itama yarinyar saura qiris yarage ta fafallamata mari dan kokadan daga ganin idanun ta bata ganin girman nagaba da ita. 

Hohoho.. Dik inda ran mamy yake idan yayi yawan qasar duniya tohh ya6ace, jitake tamakar ta rufe Saif da duka tsabar baqin ciki da take ciki, dan yau da baqaramin baqin ciki yacusa mata ba, tare da 'yar mutane wacce yayi sanadiyar kwancin ta yanzu haka a gadon asibity aranar kamunta, wani erin mugun hali ne saif yake shirin daukar wa kansa? 

guri yasamu ya zauna a qasa dai-dai inda take fuskantar sa, gaidata yayi amma mamy tayi banza dashi. 

sai da zuciyar ta tadan rage radadi sannan tasame damar bakin magana. 

Meya kawo ka gurina eyee? nace meyakawo ka gurina Marar mutunci. yaudai kanunaman ni banza ce agurinka, ban isa dakai ba.tinda har kake iyayin dik abinda kasan cewa zai 6ataman.. 

wace shegiyar yarinya ce kadauka kaje da ita gurin bikin ka dan kawai kaci zarafin matarka ko? 

ni zaka wulaqantawa Saif har kana ikirarin cewa dik wanda baya sonta baka sonsa kuma dik wanda yamatsa mata bazaku shirya dashi ba, tohh inason yau kaneme littafi ka cikashi tabbbb tare da rubutawa cewa ni maqiyayar kace, dan wllh bana qaunar ta kokadan araina dik da bansan ko wacece ita ba.

Dan narantse da allah matuqar ina numfashi aduniya bazaka ta6a aure ta ba, inhar ma kanada wannan kayi gaggawar ciresa dan ko zaka mutu bazaka samun wannan dama ba, sai dai kuwa idan abayan raina....

In Allah yaso mamy saif yayi saurin katseta, dan allah kidaina rantsewa akan hukuncin da allah yasaukar banason fushinki kokadan kinsani, amma mamy kiyi haquri ki amshe abinda zuciyata ke mutuwar so, idan ko ba haka ba, wllh saidai namutu banyi aure ba, dan bazan ta6a yarda adaura man aure da wannan ba, bayan da ba ason farin cik.....

Tassssss" mamy tadauke Saif da wani mahaukacin mari da saida yadauke jinsa da ganinsa, tsaye take tana faman huci a gabansa,kafin tace "wllh kayi kadan ba'ayi dan da zan haifa da cikina ba sannan yazo yabijire man ba, dole kayi abinda nakeso ko kanaso ko baka so, kai d'inme? kai har ka isa nace ga yanda za'ayi kamusa wllh qarya kake, kuma ba laifin ka bane laifi nane dana nuna maka so feye dana sauran 'ya'yana amma hakan bazai hana nacire hannuna akanka ba, idan yaso sai kaje kamaida wacce kaza6a tazamo uwar taka, 


Jinsa yake kamar bashi ba ne, duniyar keta faman juya masa gaba daya, har yanzu ganin yake mafarki yake bada gaske bane ba, wai ace yau mamy ce da kanta tad'aga hannuta tamaresa, kai bazaiyu ba tabbas wannan mafarki ne yake wanda yake fatar farkawar sa daga yinsa. 

sautin murya aunty Sadiya yajiyo tana cewa "banda ma yaraina mutane yanason mai damu qananin mutane yaushine har yarasa wacce zai kula sai 'yar matsiyata, wllh saif kabani mamaki kuma kabada maza, ace dik matsayin ka da qimar ka, da mutuncin ka game da sutura da allah yama har zakayi gigin kwanciya a kafadar wannan aljana kana daukar photo, ga mata wa'anda suka fita komai dai dai dakai amma ko kallo basu isheka ba, sai yanzu zaka mace akan wannan? 

Ninasan tabbas mamy ba abanza tabarshi ba, tinda har yake ikirarin yadaure Sanam dauri na har abada niko yace zai tsayawa iyayin yarinyar idan har nakuskura naji mata ciyo kozai rasa komai said yazama gatan su, sunyi shari'a dani, ace muna uwa days uba dai da kai kake wannan maganar, wllh sam baka kyautawa kanka ba... 

Idan anaman magana kidaina sakaman bakin ki kisan wannan, ni bankasance cikin dai daga cikin mutane masu ganin za'ayi zalunci nasaka ido ba, haka kuma abinda nafada gaskiya ne har cikin qarqashin zuciyata, idan kina tantama toh kiroqe allah yasa taji ciyo kiga edan da gaske nake ko ba hakan bane, wllh SAFEENA tawuce wulaqanci ga kowa, tin lokacin da Allah yasamar matani... 

Mamy baqaramin girgiza tayi da jin erin kalaman Saif ba, agaban ta, yake fadar haka? wai ma wacece wannan yarinyar ne tana son sanin ta, da uban da ya haifeta, da take neman bijire mata danta da take mutuwar so mai tsananin mata biyayy komai rintsi. 

Kinji ko mamy, kinji da kunneki, agabanki yake qara maimaitawa, kasan Saif karka kuskura kaman rashin kun....

wane kafirin tsaki yaja tare da miqewarsa atsaye yafita baiko waiwayo ba, jiyake tamkar zuciyar sa zata kama da wuta tsabar zafin da take masa. 


Wllh ba gudu baja da baya yananan kan bakansa banzai ta6a sake amasa wasa da rayuwar sa ba...



Zaune suke Palo sunacin abinci, yayin da aunty sadiya ke kallon tv rabi hankalin ta na kan tv rabi kuma yana kan su safeena dake cin abinci. 

Dikansu sunyi wanka sun canza kaya sunyi sallar magarib, sai da safeena tagasa jikinta sosai sannan taji damar sa, fira suke jefe-jefe eta da sakna har suka kammala cin abinci. 


Bayan sun kwashe komai ne sun tsabtace gurin da sukaci suka dawo palo tare da zama suma suna kallo kamar yanda auntyn su keyi. 

Candai safeena takira aunty meena bayan ta amsa ne take sanar mata akan cewa ta yanke shawarar gobe zataje gida, idan tayiwa Mama sallama jibi sai tawuce gaba daya dan bazata iya zama har a kammala bikin nan ba, gaba daya kwanciyar hankalin ta shine tabar garin nan ko ta samu ta tsira da mutuncin ta da lapiyar ta. 

etama kanta aunty haka tagani yafi shiyasa ma bata yunqurin hanata ba, iyaka kawai tayi mata fatan allah yakaisu gobe lpy, miqewa safeena tayi tamusu sai da safe akan zata shiga ciki tayi sallah esah, sannan ta kwanta saboda kanta da kemata ciyo, fatan alkhairi suka yiwa junansu ta haye sama tabar su nan suna cigaba da kallo. 


Sanam dik inda hankalin sa yake yakai qololowa gurin tashi, adik sanda yatina kalamin da yau Saif yayi garesa, sai sukuninsa yabar jikinsa, wai shi Saif ke cewa zai daure saboda kawai yamare banza yarinyar nan,  yana matsayin dan uwan sa kuma aminin sa tin suna qanane, amma har ya iya bude bakinsa yayi masa erin wannan maganar agaban mutane, kuma tabbas yasan har azuciyar sa hakan take tsaf yana iyawa. 

tohko tabbas kozai rasa komai nasa shiko bazai ta6a barin saif ya mallaki wannan tsinaniyar yarinyar da take neman hadasa dashi ba, shi yanzu kwata kwata Saif ya canza masa ko 'yar fuska da yake samu ada gurinsa yanzu babu ta, ganin ma yake kamar haushin sa Saif keji.

Daga gidansu Hotel dinsa yasa aka wuce dashi, dan komai zai iya faruwa idan yayi ido hudu da sanam, wanka yashiga yafito yasaka wata jallabiya, tare da saka wani sassanyar turare daman dakin sa daban yake acikin Hotel dinsa da yagina da kansa dake cikin garin bauci, ba abinda babu na buqata acikin dakin.
