3:33 pm, 

Driver su sakna yadauke safeena har zuwa kaduna bayan sunyi sallama da qawar ta, mama ta mata sha tara ta arziki, haka kuma dik wasu  da kaya da saif yabata tazo da abunta ko tsinke bata bari ba, hatta da wayarta aunty meena tabata abarta, koda tabata itama akashe take kuma har yanzu bata kunna ba, saboda mutun dayane ke kiranta kuma baisan anbata wayarta ba, sannan ita kuma bazata ta6a iya kiransa ba

Kallon garin take tana tina ranar da suka shigosa etada sakna cikin farin ciki gashi yanzu zata koma cikin rashin dadin rai, shikadai ne yamata tsaye arai tasan banzai ji dadi idan yaji tatafi bata masa sallama ba, amma yazatai tinda batada wata dama da zata sa takira sa... erin su Saif ba shige musu ake ba, waya ma ba kowace suka damu su dauka ba, dan haka bazata wahalar da kanta na gaya masa ba, saidai kuwa yaga laifin ta.


Ba laifi Haneefa ansamu ta farfado amma fa tinda tafarka take ta faman kuka, zuciyar ta cike da alwashi kala-kala akan safeena, a yanda take ji wllh ko ranta tana iya dauka saboda tayi mata shishigi kuma ta cutar da zuciyar ta. 

tasha kuka harma bata iya bude edanun ta, koda jiya mamy tazo har suka koma Faman aykin da tayi kenan na rarrashin Haneefa akan tadaina kuka, toh ba laifi tarage amma kam idanu sunyi suntum sunmata nauyi,  babu 6ata lokaci aka sallamesu momynta kuwa sai faman kira take dan taji yanda 'yarta take sai dai ace bacci Haneefa keyi. 

gajiya yayi da jin ringing din wayoyin sa yakashe daya dayar kuma etama yadauka zai kashe sai yaga aunty meena ce ke kiransa.

Dauka yayi batare da yace komai ba yakara wayar a kunnesa, shiru da kamar 2mnt naji yace "what.....?  tare da sakin Cup din hannusa yatarwatse a qasa har coffee din, zuface tafara samun damar keto masa dik da Ac dake kunne yana ayki amma tashin hankalin da ya tsince kansa aciki ko wuta ce idan da hali tana fitowa ajikin sa. 

gaba daya komai nasa tsayawa yayi dafe kansa yayi wannan karon ji yake tamkar yafashe da kuka tsabar baqin ciki da takaicin abinda safeena tamasa, aunty nata faman kiran hello, hello, amma ina sam baya ko jin ta, qarshema yakashe wayar sa. 


Lallai wannan karon zaibawa yarinyar nan mamaki, me dan iska ta maidashi ne ko me? ay koba komai yadace tasanar dashi tafiyar ta zaisan cewa yanada matsayi agurinta, tinda aunty ma tabata wayarta tace, ko bata kirasa ba yadace tamasa koda text ne amma ko daya bata masa ba, kenan yanda yadauke ta ba haka ta dauke sa ba, yau yatabbar da cewa safeena tafita daban acikin sauran mata ajinta yawuce dik yanda yake tinani. 

amma bata kyauta masa ba dan yanzu da tasanar dashi motar sa zai bada har gida akai ta, daga nan driver sa yasan gida koda yabuqace ganin ta hakan bazai masa wuya, amma yanzu yasan motar ma haya tahau qarshin ta ma tanacan kusa da wani a zaune, rintse edansa yayi da qafi sakamakon wani abu da yataso masa a hankali yasauka kan gado yafara tafiya hanyar da zata sadashi da toilet nan juwa ta kwashe sa, anan bakin qofar yayanki jiki yafadi sumamme.... 

      *********

Zo kaga murna da farin ciki gurin dija da umma harda su Salim da salima qanneta, haqiqa farin cikin da suke ciki baya misaltuwa, wanka tafarayi sanan tayi sallah aka kawo mata lafiyayyin abinci tamkar ansan zatazo akayi shinkafa da miya harda salat da nama, aka hadomata da zo6o mai matuqar sanyi, cikin natsuwa tafara cin abincin ta cikin farin ciki yau gata gida cikin 'yanuwanta tana kuma kwasar girkin umman ta wanda tayi masifar kewarsa.

Bayan ta kammala ne tashiga jero kayayyakin da tazo dasu tare da ciro kudin kashi-kashi ay fa nan edanun dija tamkar sufado, waresu ta dingayi kafin takira sunan umma, amsawa umma tayi dan tana jiran qarin bayani, kinga wannan dubu hamsin ne, aunty sakna tabani kasancewa tanada kudi sosai kuma munje munyi kwanaki gidanta shine da zan dawowa tabani cewa da aunty meena, wannan kuma dubu talatin din mamace tabani tace nakawo miki ko zaki dan ja jari dasu ba waya, wanna kuma Dubuque ashirin sakna ce tabani tace nayo muku tsara dashi, kunga kudi sunkama dubu dari kenan.

ina zuwa safeena tafada tare da miqewa atsaye tanufe dakin su, bincike tashiga yi har takai gurin ma'ajiyar ta tadauko wasu damin kudi, kudin da saif yabata ne tin sanda taje adamawa tadauko su ta fito


gurin su Mama tadawo ta ajiyesu, baqaramin razana mama tayi da ganin wa'anna kudin da safeena tafito dasu ba, dija ko ajikinta saboda tasan da zaman kudin tin lokacin da sukaje adamawa

Bayani tashiga yiwa umma tin farkon fadansu da Haneefa da kuma haduwar su da Saif gami da erin taimakon da yamata da kuma kayyakin da yasiya mata da yazo dubin ta, gidan baba mallam dika tasanar da ita, sannan ta daura da cewa ashe umma shine yayan sakna kuma wannan hanifa din itace zai aura, kwashe komai tayi tasanar dasu bata 6oye musu komai ba, tace "wllh shiyasa hankalina yatashi nace gwara nadawowa ta gida ko ba'a kammala ba. 

Gwanda da kika dawowar ki cewar umma, kada su illataman ke haka Kawai akan wani tunanin su na banza, inama ke ina wannan, ay koda maza sunqare a duniyar nan bazaki aure sa, dan bangaji da rayuwar ki ba. 

Arazane safeena tadago kanta tana kallon umma cikin tashin hankali da jin kalamin ta, dik da tasan bazata ta6a samun sa ba, amma ay tana sonsa bazataji dadin wannan furuci na umma ba. 

Qoqarin 6oye kwalla da take shirin zubo mata tayi kafin tatashi kamar zata dauko wani abu tayi cikin dakin su. 

Ruf da ciki tafadi kwance kan dan yalolon gadon su, yayin da hawaye masu matuqar qona suke fitowa daga idon ta jin take tamkar zuciyar ta tayi bindiga tafito, afili take cewa "wllh umma ina sonsa meyasa zaki fadi haka akansa koda shi bayajin komai akaina tohni inaji, ni qilama sonsa ne zai kasheni tafada tare da koma fashewa da wani erin kuka mai ta6a zuciya. 

tadade tsaye akanta batun yau tasan 'yar uwar ta tafada cikin tarkon so ba, son kuma wanda yafi qarfin ajinta da nasabar ta, dik da tana iyakar qoqarin ta na6oye abinda ke damunta amma shi so ba'amasa haka dan kokadan baya 6oyuwa dan koka 6oye sa toh tabbas zai bayyana a idanun ka. 

dafata tayi tare da zama kusa da ita, saida safeena ta tsorata dan tadauka umma ce ganin dija ce yasa ta saukar da wata erin ajiyar zuciya, tashi zaune tayi tana share hawayin dake fuskar ta, kafin ta dubi dija tace "sis ina cikin matsala inacikin tashin hankalin da yake neman barazana da rayuwata dan Allah kitaimaka man Wllh ina tsananin masifar sonsa tamkar na zauce plss... 

gaba daya safeena tagama fita daga cikin hayyacin ta, tabbas tazauce tinda har ta yarda tafitar da abinda ke cikin ta tasanarwa dija, yarinyar dake da masifar zurfin ciki amma yanzu babu shiri ta amayar da abinda ke cinta. 

itama dija hawaye ne kebin fusakar ta ganin yanda 'yar uwar ta keneman rasa tunanin ta, akan babba abinda ta daukowa kanta, shi wanda ma take dominsa baiko san tanayi ba, mai yuwa ma ita da koma ganin sa sai wata qaddara idan allah yakoma hadasu

daga haka aunty bata jira cewar safeena ba takashe wayar tare da komawa dakin da sauri, koda ta isa su mamy suna a waje yayin da likitocin da suke cikin dakin sunfi aqirga, kallo daya zaka yiwa mamy ka tabbatar da cewa bata cikin hayyacin ta, kallon tuhuma aunty sadiya ta dinga bin aunty meena dashi, wanda yake tattare da jin haushin ta a ranta.

         *4 days later*


Yanzu jiki kam alhamdulillah Saif yasamu sauqi dan yanzu komai yana iyayi da kansa gayun ma ko a asibityn ba daynasa akayi ba, bazakama yi zaton marar lafiyar bane sai wanda yasani.

Magana kam da kowa bayayin ta yanzu har mamy, ba umm ba umm-umm ko daddy baya samun damar yamasa magana, wani miskilanci ne yataso masa feye da wanda yake ciki ada, dan shi da yanada iko ma yanzu da koda da zuciyar sa bazaiyi magana ba.

Saif kenan daman Hausawa sukace hali zanin dutse mai hali baya fasa halinsa sai dai ko idan daman can aransa yayi ba nasa bane.

aunty meena ce tamatso kusa dashi tare da zama kusa dashi, dakin ta duba taga ba kowa aciki sai Nadiya dake bacci tin dazu, mamy kuwa taje gida wanka itada aunty sadiya, Haneefa kuwa kusan atare suka fita taje wani Mall ne siyayya saboda wani juice da shi kadai ne Saif kesha shine taje tasiyo masa dan wanda kenan yakusan qarewa, shine taje taqara siyo wani tare da wasu sauran kayan buqatu. 


dik da zaman da aunty meena tayi akusa da shi bai sashi yadago kansa daga kan kallon wayarsa da yake ba tin dazu, ashe No safeena ce yatsirawa edo yake ta kallo jiyake tamkar yayi dialling number ta amma kuma bayajin zai iya haka saboda sosai yake fushi da ita.

Aunty meena ce ta katse masa tinanin da yake da cewa "dik wannan zurfin cikin da kake da shanqamshin ka wllh dik banaka bane, kawai malam kafito kagayawa duniya abinda ke damunka idan ba haka ba, wllh ba baki namaka ba wannan ciyon da kayi toh nan gaba zakayi babban sa, inharma baka tsince kanka a qabari ba, dan doctor yace zuciyarka bamai jure erin damuwa da 6acin rai bace dan kana gab daka illata kanka da kanka. 

Nidai nabaka shawara amatsayina na 'yar uwarka kuma mai son ganin cigaban rayuwa ka, har ezuwa lokacin da allah ya dibar maka wa'adin ka. 

Ruwanka ne kayi amfani da shawarata, kuma ruwanka ne kayi akasin hakan, amma dai inason kasani cewa da nariga da nagayawa Safeena cewa karta kuskura taqara kiranka sannan kuma tamanta dakai gaba daya a rayuwar ta, shine shawarar da nabata da kuma naga shine yadace da nafada mata amatsayina na mai qaunar t....

What...? Yafada da qarfi wanda yasa saida Nadiya dake kwance ta zabura wacce daman ba bacci take ba, ashe tindazu ta tashi tsaf kuma dik taji  abinda aunty meena tafada wanda ko tantama batayi maganace suke akan shegiyar yarinyar nan.

cikin tashin hankali yake kallon aunty meena wanda kallo daya zaka masa kahango tsantsar 6acin rai a fuskar sa, bai ta6a sanin aunty meena bata qaunarsa ba sai yau, ashe daman dik qaryace ba qaunar sa take ba, ashe daman ita tahana yarinyar nan takoma kiransa wani tsakine yaja mai mugun fasa kannan kafin yakwashe wayoyinsa yafita daga dakin wanda kokadan bayako ganin gabansa. 

Wani qayataccin murmushi ne aunty meena tayi naganin taci nasara shawo kan saif da wuri da tayi, tasan yanzu dolen sa yakira ta. 

Daman tayi hakan ne saboda fushin da Saif keyi da safeena ta gusar dashi a zuciyar sa, shi yasa tace kar safeena ta qara kiransa saboda idan har taci gaba da kiransa yanda Saif yake da giraman kai ga tsantsar miskilancin sa, ba dauka zaiyi ba kuma hakan naqara sakasa acikin damuwa daga shi har ita safeena, amma sai tabiyo masa ta wannan gefin yanzu tasan tinda tafada masa haka sai yakira ta, dik da batada tabbacin haka.


Dafe yake da kansa cikin wani qayataccin gurin hutawa dake cikin asibity, bakajin motsen komai a gurin sai tarin kuka tsintsaye saboda tsaruwar asibityn komai atsare yake kasancewar ta, prvt hospital.

********

Yau kwana biyu kenan da sallamu safeena daga asibity dan kwana biyu kawai sukayi tamatsa akan sudawo gida. 

Alhamdulillah za'ace tadan ji sauqi sai dai ramar da tayi batacin komai sai ruwan kunu kawai koshi dan umma namatsa matane akan taqicin komai shi yasa take cewa abata su kadai, hatta da dija tadan fada saboda halin da 'yar uwar ta, take ciki.

Su biyu ne kadai adaki, safeena zaune take aqasa tare da jingina bayan ta, da gado gaba daya tagama zurfafa cikin tunani, yayin da Dija tagama sallah tana addu'a bayan ta kammala ne tajiyo ta dubi 'yar uwar ta, wacce Kwata-kwata tinanin ta baya nan yatafi can wani guri daban. 

Sundade a haka kowa da nasa tunanin da yake, dan umma tafita taje kar6awa safeena magani dan ita a ganin ta harda iska ke damun ta.

Zuciyar safeena say faman bugawa take batasan me yasa hakan ba, wanda tsaf Dija takula da hakan amma bata nuna mata ba, ita dai kawai tamatsu akan safeena tabude musu text dinnan suga, kuma batason nunawa safeena tanason ganinsa dan baqaramin aykin ta bane, taqi budeshi har sai tagama ja mata aji kafim tagwada mata.

daurewa safeena tayi tabude text din suka fara karantawa cikin natsuwa kamar haka...


_Ana gane kyawun shuka ne idan har an gama ta da taki. Tayaya za a gane sama idan har babu k'asa? Kamar haka ne, farin-cikina zai yi wuyar bayyanuwa matu’kar ba ma tare da juna. Na kan shiga damuwa a dukkan lokacin da na duba gefena ban gan ki ba. Yau rana ce ta farin-ciki gare ni AMANA TA._


Wayyo dadi safeena tafada cikin wata erin murya, kinga ko Dija wllh yana tare dani bai manta dani ba,

sai da yatura text yashiga sannan yake baqin cikin turashi da yayi, tabbas wannan ba ra'ayinsa kansa bane yasan aykin zuciyar sa ne, shi gaskiya bata kyauta masa ba, 

Yayi nisa cikin tunani can yaji wayarsa na ringing dubawa yayi yaga sunanta har a ransa yaji dadin kiran amma bai dauka ba,  yana kallo harta tsinke.

da kamar bayan 5mnt aka sake kira har saida takusan tsinkewa kafin ya miqa hannusa ya dauka tamkar bayaso picking yayi amma baice komai ba. 

6angarin safeena ma haka dan da takirashi ana farko bai dauka ba qin koma kiransa tayi dik da magiyar da Dija ke mata takoma kiransa, amma taqi sake kiran har saida ita da kanta Dija takoma Kira tana ganin yadauka ne yasa ta karawa safeena wayar a kunne. 

sujima a haka ba wanda yace komai sai da shi yagaji da kansa sannan cikin murya sa mai sanyi yace "wake maga ne?

itama daurewa tayi cikin dakiya tace Oh  am so sorry plss wrong number ne nayi, daga haka ta tsinke wayar...

A yaune aka daura Auren SAIFULLAH NAZIR 'DAN KWANGILA tare DA HANEEFA UMAR KABIR, akan sadaki qalilan wanda akaje har adamawa aka dauro sa, dibin jama'a sunshe da aure Saif da Haneefa wanda aka shirya shagali ba kadan ba, ba kalan mutanin da basu zoba, haka aka kammala aka dawo gida, wanda Ango ma shi ko ajinkinsa tamkar bashi aka daurawa aure ba, dan baya ko jin shi aure yayi.

Amarya tatare a danqarerin gidanta dake cikin Manya gidajin Saif dake garin Bauci, maikaratu bazan iya kwatanta muku erin kyawun gidan da tsaruwar sa ba, saboda sunyiwa alqalamina girma, sai dai zanbaku dama ku kwatan ta iya qarshin tunanin ku...

Safeena kam tamkar numfashin ta yafita alokacin da taji umma na tsintar shinkafa tana saurarin radio, yayin da ake ta zuzuta daurin auren da erin yanda ango yayi kyau hatta da zobin dan yatsansa saida aka fadi iya tsadar sa da girmansa, wani shahararrin maqaryaci aka samu yake fadar cewa " mai sauraro zanso kaga bakin ango wanda yakasa rufuwa saboda tsantsar farin cikin da yake ciki, bayan ko inda motocin Saif suke mutun yayi kadan ma da yaje gurin bare ma harya yayi tunanin ganinsa.. 

Lallashin duniyar nan Dija tayiwa safeena shi amma taqi yin shiru, Wani azababbin kishi ne ya kecin zuciyar ta tamkar zata mutu take ji.. 

Tsantsar tsanar Haneefa yataru yayiwa zuciyar ta, katutu, wanda ko sunanta ta tsane ma taji ankira, bata ta6a sanin tana kishin Saif ba sai yau, har dai idan ta tuna cewa fa, darin yau tasan shine darinsu na farko, zasu kasance cikin farin ciki.....


Ay wata qarace safeena tayi wanda umma ma batasan sanda tayi jifa da tiren tsintar shinkafar da take tsinta ba, tayo cikin d'akin, a 360 koda taqaraso tasamu Dija ce keta faman tausarta, kuka take sosai erin mai cin rannan tamkar ranta zai fita, gaba d'aya ta birkice musu yau, hatta da umma yau batasan sanda ta fashe da kuka ba, tsabar baqin cikin ganin halin da 'yarta, take ciki kuma gashi taqi fadin damuwar ta, juyin duniyar nan anyi da ita tafadi meke damun ta, amma taqi fada, Deja ce kawai tasan sirrin komai, wacce ko giyar wake tasha bazata fara fadin sirrin 'yar uwar ta ba, dan ta hana ta fad'a...

Yau kusan kwana biyu da tarewar Haneefa amma ko inda sahunsa yake bata gani ba, dik shige shigenta takasa gano ko inda 6angarin sa yake, saboda tasabar girman gidan, amma taqudurata aranta dik inda yake saita sani kuma wllh dolin sa yasaurare ta ko yanaso ko bayaso tinda yanzu tariga da tazama matarsa ba yanda zayayi sai haquri. 

Wani tsararrin gurine dake cinkin company sa yake zaune yana dannan laptop wasu kayane yakeson shigar dasu shi yasa ko takan wayoyinsa baybi takansu ba.

knocking din qofa akayi sannan aka shigo security sane tare da wani matashin mutun shiba zakace masa saurayi ba, haka kuma bazakace masa magidanci ba. 

har qasa yaduqa ya gaidashi amma baiko dago kansa ba bare ma yagansa, saida duk yakammala abinda yake sannan yadago kansa, kallo daya yayiwa mutumin yadauke kansa, 

can dai yabude bakinsa yafara magana cike da ezza, yake cewa "Ina saurarin ka? 

Ayko mutumin tamkar jira yake yafara magana, cike da girmamawa kansa na qasa tamkar wanda ke gaban Mahaifinsa. 

am.... Ranka yadade tuni munka tura mutanin mu yanxu ma haka suna cikin garin kaduna insha allah, nayi musu bayanin dik yanda kace kakuma tsara insha Allah baza'a samu matsala ko akasin hakan ba.. 

Gud yace wanda saida gabaya matashin mutumin yaji, kana iya tafiya yafada ataqaice.... 

Security da yashigo dashi daman yanan a tsaye, tare suka fita, shikuma yaci gaba da aykinsa yana maijin cewa yayi maganin nisancin ta, gare sa...


Dija ce keta kaiwa da komowa saboda aykin da yataru yamata yawa tinda atare sukeyi da safeena, toh safeena kuwa yanzu ko tsinke bazata iya dauka ba, zaune kawai take a tsakar gida tamkar mutun mutumi, sallar ma magarib da qyar tayi ta, saboda tsabar juwa da take gani.. 

umma batanan taje tafowa dasu salma da yau ake walima a makarantar su ta isilamiya, 

Ganin Dija tayi safeena ta miqe tsaye da qyar ta shiga daki tafito da mayafin ta, "ina zuwa adda yanzu keda bakiko da lapiya? 

cikin yamutsa fuska  erinna marassa lapiya tace "zanje ne name siyo magani dan matuqar nakwana haka wllh kaina zai iya fashewa, kinga gara na rarrafa naje nasiyo. 

Amma ko bake kadai ba wllh adda, taya zanbari kifita kekadai kina haka? Jifa yanda kike hada hanya, kawai bari na dauko hijab narakaki, daman nagama komai kwasar abinci ne kawai yarage, idan nadawo zan qarasa. 

safeena batayiwa Deja musu ba suka jawo gida kawai basu rufe ba, dan sunsan ko yaushe umma na iya dawowa, mai yuwa kafin sudawo sunrigasu, tinda tafiyar safeena sai ahankali.



Da kamar bayan minty goma haka, ganin sukayi layinsu yacika maqil ba masoka tsinke sai faman wuce su ake da gudu ana qara shiga layin gidan su, jin wasu sukayi suma sunwuce suna fadar  ayga erin tanan rashin kulawarsu da mutane yasa yanzu gidansu yaqone mai yuwa ma duk suna ciki.

Kutt"me zasu gani suna karyo kwana sukaga gidansu nata famanci da wuta, haba ay safeena tini ta girgije, a 360 suka isa gurin cikin tashin hankali da d'emuwa, ummansu suka hango tad'aura Hannu ga kai sai rusar kuka take gaba daya tafita daga cikin hayyacin ta, isa sukayi gurin ta da sauri tana ganinsu kayi kansu sai kuma taqara fashewa dawani erin kuka mai ta6a rannan, magana take sonyi amma kuka yaci qarfin ta.

Tambayar ta suka shigayi inasu salma? cikin kuka umma tace indai har su Salim basu taddaku ba, toh tabbas suna cikin gidan sun qone, nayi zaton kuna gidane shi yasa naturosu gida dannasan ba inda kuke zuwa, nikuma sai nawuce gurin kar6wa safeena sauran rubutun ta, koda nake isowa gidannan sai naci karo da yanaci da wuta dik kururuwar danake ba wanda ya kawoman agaji, sai daga baya wasu su biyu sukezo suka fara kawoman agaji da sukaji ina anbatun sunayin kune shi yasa suka fadaman cewa da wuya ma edan kuna cikin gidan dan sunga randa kuka fita, amma basu sani ba ko kun dawo dan basu lura ba, hakan yabani tabbacin cewa lallai dukan ku, kun qone, ashe su Hassan ne da usaina ne Allah yaqaddara kwansu yaqare, takai qarshe maganar ta, tare da koma rushewa da kuka, suma su safeena kukan suke,  lallai wannan itace qaddara bata wuce rana, allah su yayi ba dasu ciki ba sai su salma ne allah yasa ajalinsu yazo. 

Ikon Allah wannan wutar dan dai basu bar komai a kunne ba, da qyar dai aka samu wutar tamutu, wanda ko gawar su salma ba agani ba...

Saif saida yagama dik wani abinda yake cike da taqama sannan yakai dubansa gurin wayarsa dako yanzu kiran yaqara shigowa.

Picking yayi batare da yace komai ba alamar yana saurare kenan, dik wata 'yar rahamar dake fuskarsa daukewa tayi lokaci daya, da qyar yayi qarfin halin cewa "dik abinda yadace amusu amma banason sunana yafito aciki karma a kuskura susan wani abu yafito daga 6angare na.. 

Bansan me wancan yace ba, iyakadai naga yacinke wayarsa tare da jifa da ita kan gado, tsaye yatashi yana zagayar dakin yayin da tunani yamasa yawa, wani gefe kuma kokadan baiji dadin da har akace anrasa qanneta cikin wutar ba har kusan su biyu, wani 6angare kuma yana jinjina wannan lamari da komai yazo masa cikin sauqi yanzu zata dawo kusa dashi kamar yanda yatsara ammashi nashi tsarin daban yake.

ikon allah sai kuma ga wannan mumunar qaddara dataxo lokaci guda tafadawa bayin allah nan, cak"tinaninsa yatsaya da yatuna yanzu erin quncin da take ciki gana rashin qanneta sannan kuma gana qonewar muhallinsu, yanzu yasan dik inda take tana cikin wani mawacin hali..

Wayarsa yararmo da sauri zaisake kiran mutumin, kome kuma yatina? sai naga kuma yafasa kokadan bayason shi kansa yadinga nuna damuwar sa akanta qiri-qiri barema har wani yagane..

kasa zama yayi sai faman safada marwa yake dan kwata-kwata natsuwar sa da wani sukuni dik yanemesu yarasa, afili kuma yace "tayama za'ayi nasame wani kwanciyar hankali ina anan ita kuma tana acikin hali na rashin natsuwa da kwanciyar hankali, tabbas tariga da tazama kamar mafarki na, ta yanda komai rintsi mutun yaruntsa sai yayisa, haka take aransa ba yanda za'ayi yaji dadi bayan bata kasance cikin yanayin da yake ba ayanzu.

Kasa haqura yayi har saida yayi dialing No mutumin,  bayan andauka ne, cikin yamutsa fuska na karma mutumin yadubi wani abu yace "ka isane?

Amsar mutumin yabashi da cewa wllh ranka yadade da qyar suka yarda suka amince har suka biyoni mussaman mahaifiyar su, amma dai nakaisu gidan kuma nasa ankawo musu abinci da abinda duk yakamata wanda zasu buqata, amma fa yalla6ai kamar dayar najin jiki dan su biyone 'yan matan, batada lafiya sosai wllh. 

kamar bazaiyi magana ba sai kuma yace ita wacce batada lpiyar yaya take? 

ayfa daman kallamu akwai surutu nan yafara zuba masa, "ay yalla6ai yarinyar farace Marar jiki sosai amma kuma ba'a kirata siririya ba, tana da manyan idanu da dogon hanci, zubinta tamakar na mutanin nan na qasar.....

Zancin kallamu yakatse ne lokacin da yaji Saif ya katsa masa wata erin tsawa wacce tasashi kidemewa ba kadan ba, tamkar bashi ba yarufe kallamu da fad'a yana cewa "bakada hankali ne? daman ba aykin da nasanya ka kajeyi ba? Yaushe ka rainani da zaka dinga man surutu haka? sai kuma yayi shiru saboda tsabar yanda yakejin zuciyar sa namasa zafi, cikin dauriya da kakkausar murya yace "wllh karka kuskura kaqara takawa koda abakin layin gidan bare harka shiga kadinga qarewa mutane Kallo.

10:00 am, yamaka agarin bauci idan allah yakai mana rai da lpy idan ba haka ba, sai kawai yakatse wayarsa.

Ikon Allah kawai kallamu keta nanatawa aransa, yalla6ai fa shi yace namasa kwatacin yanda take, namasa kuma abun yazama laifi? 

tinda yake dashi bai ta6a jin maganar sa haka ba, bare har yadaga murya irin haka, lallai da akwai wani abu?

toh koma meye sudai idone nasu, amma yau gaskiya ubangidan nasa yabasa matuqar mamaki, daman yana magana haka?  Kasancewar bamai basa wanna amsa yaja motarsa yanufe masaukin sa, dan gobe dayayi sallah assuba zai kama hanya, idan dai ba yanason kwanakin kudin abincin sa, suqare ba. 

Fita yake da niyar yi dan yaje gare ta, sai kuma yatuna fa ba abauci suke ba, su suna kaduna, wani haushine yataso masa wanda yasa dik komai nasa yatsaya, yamasan yau ko kwanciya bazatayo garsa ba, bare ma har takai da yasamu runtsawa..


         *2 days later*


Dik yanda saif yaso yaje kaduna koda aqofar gidan da suke cikine yaje yagani yaji sanyi aransa, amma abin ya faskara, sakamakon tafiyar da tazo masa cikin gaggawa a qasar India, haka yatafi cikin rashin qwarin jiki tamkar wani marar lafiya, har d'an fad'awa yayi saboda rashin samun kwanciyar hankali. 

Sanam dai yasamu anyi aurin Saif amma yakasa shin wace hanyace zai samu damar nemanta yanke shawara yayi zaije yasame qanwar sa tafada masa Address din gidansu safeena idan ma takama tare zasu tafi, idan yaso yashawo kanta kawai sai yazo yagabatar da komai cikin gaggawa kafin shi Saif yadawo, dan yanzu ko waya sundaina dan daman Sanam ne mai kiran kuma yanzu yadaina saboda kishin Saif da yake, 

Saif ko, shi ko ajikinsa dan baiko kula da rashin kiran Sanam ba, yama mantashi kawai abinda ke gabansa ne cikin ransa. 

        ********

Cikin ikon allah jikin safeena yayi kyau ya warware sakamkon magunguna da likitan yadaura ta akansu, dan kudi sosai kallamu yabasu da zai tafi acewar zaiyi tafiya ne yadawo, shi yasa suka same qarfin gwaiwar kai safeena asibity kuma dik magunguna da aka rubata musu sune suka siyo, dik da ko sunada matuqar tsada, kuma qari abinda yataimaka shine rage damuwar da safeena tayi aranta, ba abinda take yanzu illa kullum tana roqon allah yacire mata son saif a zuciyar ta, ko zata samu ta huta. 

Amma inaaaa, yananan ba inda yaje sai dai quncin zuciya allah yarage matashi, dan yanzu harda ayki tana dan kamawa ayi tare da ita.. 


 Haneefa abin duniya ya eshe ta, sunan wai tana amarya amma tinda aka kawota ko motsin ango bata ta6a jiba sai dai  kawai taji kamar dirar motocin sa, amma bazata fita ba, dan yanzu ita kuma haneefa ganin take matarsa ce fa, dan haka yanzu bazata iya jure wulaqanci ba wanda ada ta dinga bibiyarsa..

Gaskiya yanzu bazai yu..... Ringing din wayar tane yakatse mata tunanin da take wanda har sai da ta tsorata dan baqaramin zurfi tayi ba. 

No Kamal tagani akan screen din wayarta, babu 6ata lokaci tadauka, cikin tashin hankali yake cewa Haneefa "ranki yadade akwai matsala... 

gaban haneefa ne yayi wani erin masifafin faduwa, allah dai yasa Saif baigano abinda ake ciki ba, nan take cikin ta yamurd'a, amma cikin dauriya bakinta har rawa yake tace "bangane akwai matsala bai, matsalar me kuma? 

"Hajiya aykin da kika samu sam baiyi ba, ansamu akasi wllh, yanzunnan nagama jin labarin komai dan wani dan ayki ne yakawo ni kaduna yanzu ashe da rabon injin wannan mumuna labarin, daman yarannan basa cikin gidan su biyu dagasu har mahaifiyar su, qannesu kawai su biyu akace sun rasu,  kuma antabbatar man da wani yataimaka musu har yabasu masauki yanzu aciki ne suke zaune. 

Zufane keta faman karyo wa Haneefa wani nabin wani, daman ashe tsinanun mutanin nan basu mutu ba?  wani 6acin raine yaketa faman barazana da zuciyar ta, kafin tace wllh qaryane taci gaba da zama, a zuciyar wanda nake masifafin so, ko bandashi ba, daman akwai azazza atsakanin su, dan haka wllh kozata rasa komai nata sai taga bayan Safeena bata buqatar numfashin ta adoron qasa, dan saboda barazanace arayuwar ta.

Cikin 6acin rai tace "Kamal inason karka kuskura kafito garinan har saika san inda tsinanun nan suka koma, wannan karon abinda nakeso dakai yanzu shine kasame qarti majiya qarfi kusan su biyar ko shida mar imani kokadan azuciyar su, kudirar musu acikin taltilin dare, inaso kuyaga musu dik wata daraja dake jikinsu har mahaifiyar su ku hada, kacakaca nakeson kumusu har sai sun daina numfashi aduniya... 

Wata dariya ce Kamal yafashe da ita mai qarfi saida yayi iya mai isarsa kafin yace, indan wannan ne karki damu ay yamafi komai sauqi, wannan ayki ga lada da kuma la'ada kwantar da hankalinki kawai, wllh ayau za'a gudanar da komai kinsani danaci akan abu har sai nacimma burina, abinda kawai nakeso dake kikwantar da hanakalinki kisa aranki komai ma yazo qarshe...


               ******

wata tasowace yayi da qarfi yana mai karanta addu'ar dik da tazo bakinsa, zuface dik tajiqa masa jiki mamaki yake Ac dake kunne amma yayi wannan zufa, koda yake dole ne dan mafarkin da yayi yamatuqar tadamai hankali, agogo yaduba yaga 3:33 am, cikin rashin qwarin jiki yatashi yanufe toilet dan dauro alwala, amma mafarkin na aransa, gawani bugu da zuciyar sa keye tamakar zata fito, faduwar gaba yamasa yawa aransa yake cewa tom wai meke faruwane? 

Yayo alwalar amma yakasa yin sallah saboda mutuwar jiki da kuma mumuna faduwar gaba datake ta taso masa, kansane yadafe dik yanda akayi *AMANA TA*, tana cikin wani hali, dan tabbas wannan mafarkin da kuma abinda nakeji ba abanza ba.

Ina ma yana qasar wllh da komai dare yanzunnan sai yaje kaduna amma yanzu tamasa nisa sosai..


Hasbunallah wani'emaal wakhil, wasu qartine marassa kawon gani ko wannesu fuska ba alamun rahama atattare da ita, gidan sam baimusu wuyar ganewa ba haka kuma bai musu wuyar shiga ba, saboda yanayin anguwar da take da qarancin mutane tin qarfi 9:00 pm, kowa ke shikewa.

Babu ta inda zasu haura a gidan dole ne sai taqofa, shiyasa suka fara Knocking idan har ba'a bude ba tohhh zasu 6alla qofar su shiga...

tamkar amafarki sukaji ana knocking ummace tafara fitowa daga cikin dakin ta, suma su safeena fitowar sukayi atsakar gida suke atsaye suna mamakin wake qwanqwasa musu gida yanzu cikin dare haka?

Mamakin su bai qaro ba har saida sukaji ana qoqarin 6allar da qofar gidan ashigo nanko suka tabbatar da cewa lallai 6arayi ne  zasu shigo musu,

Habaaaa kafin kace me fitsare ya kwancewa Dija batako sani ba safeena kuwa zuciyar ta keta faman bugawa da qarfi saboda tsoro, tohhh bare umma wacce dik tafisu shiga cikin tashi hankali... 

Komai nasu tsayawa yayi sakamakon 6alla qofar da sukaji anyi za'a shigo.....

                   *(46)*

Da mugun gudu sukayi cikin daki dukansu yayin da suka sanyawa qofar key harda sakata sai faman bugu zuciyar su keye.

kuka kawai su Dija keye amma bamai erin fitowar nan ba, umma kuwa duk tafisu shiga cikin tashin hankali marar misaltuwa.

Jin motsinsu sukayi a tsakiyar gida suna ta budar dakuna dake cikin gida sai suga wayam, wani daki ne suka gani a rufe ay kuwa da hanzarin su sukayi can, bugun qofa suka shigayi da qarfi amma shiru ba'a bude musu ba. 

Hakan ya tabbatar musu da babu makawa suna cikin wannan dakin, shiyasa suka fara qoqarin koma 6alla wannan qofar..

wani fitsari ne Dija ta koma saki wanda aqallah yanzu tayi tayinshi yakai sau biyar saboda tsoro.

Yanzu kam basuda wata dubara sai yanda Allah yayi dasu, atakure suke guri daya gwanin tausayi. 

Dukan su saida numfashin su yakusan daukewa da sukaji ko wannan qofar ma sun 6allata, daya bayan daya suka fara shigowa, wanda yasa hankalin su safeena yayi mumuna tashi dan tamkar wasu samudawa qattin nan suke babu alamar tausayi ko rahama kokadan a fukokinsu, gashi basuda kyakyawar kama, kuka suka farayi iya qarfinsu suna roqonsu gafara akan kada su cutar dasu.

tsawace kamal ya doka musu fusakrsa tamkar wanda aka aykowa da saqon mutuwa. Cikin kakausar murya yace idan kuka kuskura ku qara koda wani qwaqwaran motse wanda bamune muka baku umarnin yin hakan ba wllh sai munfasa kwanyar kan wannan baqar uwa taku mai doraku akan son abinda yafi qarfinku matsiyata kawai.. 

tuni sukayi tsit tamkar basu ba sai cicika suke ta kuka almar sunyisa har sungaji,

Ke.... Kamal yadakawa Dija wata tsawa da saida takusan fita da tunanin ta, "tashi kibi wancan yafada yana nuna wani sungumemen qato da duk yafisu jiki da rashin kawon gani, hannu suka daura akai suna roqon kamal akan yayi hanquri. 

Wata dariya ce yakece da ita saida yayi mai isarsa sannan yace "uwar najadu kinfison ayi komai agabanki ne? 

Tohhh kema kanki baki tsira ba, dik da bakamai ne zamu tsinta akanki ba amma yana da kyau atina maki da abinda kika manta..

Wani qololon takaici ne yatasowa Safeena batasan sanda tace "amma kai dai wllh anyi dan akuya agurin nan, inbanda dabbanci da shedabci erin naka wannan zaka duba ka dinga mata irin wannan maganganun saboda bakasan daraja iyaye ba ko, koda yake mai yuwa kasabayi da taka uwar shiyasa kake neman na wasu uwayin, toh idanko hakane kayi kuskure dan bako wace uwa bace irin taka, kuma sai allah ya isar mana da duk abinda kuke qoqarin mana da ikon allah bazaku same nasara akan mu b...

ay safeena bata idar da maganar ta ba taji saukar wasu zafafan maruka da sunka kusan dauke mata jinta da ganin ta.. batare da yace komai ba yadube qartinnan yace kukaman eta a wancan daki wllh yau sai na mugun wulaqanta rayuwarki, badai nikeke fadawa hakan ba zakiyi mamaki kuma zakisan ko waye ni... 

tamkar wata 'yar baby suka daga safeena cak" tana duka tana cizo amma ko ajikinsu har suka fita da ita daga cikin dakin wanda yasa daukewar numfashin umma su, Dija kuwa hankalinta yaqara tashi ummansu ta suma ga addan ta anfita da ita, ga kalamin kamal na qarshe da yake cewa kumasu duk abinda kukaga dama idan zasu maku taurin kai kudaure su, kunyi maganin 'yan iska.. 

yana gama fadar haka yafita daga cikin dakin yanufe dakin da aka kai safeena cike da mugunta kala-kala azuciyar sa... 

Basukai da aywatar da mumuna qudirinsu ba, sukaji dirar Manya Manya motoci, Kamal ne yasake feena wacce duk fusakarta tayi ja saboda marukan da Kamal keta bata. 

window dakin kamal yadan leqa, ido yazaro wanda nan take mararsa ta qulle saboda tsoro nan take jikinsa yadauke mazari, ganin motocin sojojine kusan mota takai goma suka kewaye gidan bai gama kallo ba yaji shigowarsu cikin gidan, roqon feena yaci gaba dayi da tarufa masa asiri kada ta gwade gunda yake, yana roqonta yana shigewa a qarqashin gado



Eta kanta saida ta tsorata data gansu dan tamafi jin tsoronsu akan su kamal, rarraba edo suka shigayi suna duba ko ina sai kawai taji murya mutumin daya taimake su yana cewa "nan ba kowane? 

ganin yayi duk safeena tawani chanza kamanni daman ga mutun fari bare yanzu taqara ja fuskarta duk ta kunbura.

Kanta yayo tare da tambayar ta badai abinda suka muku ko? 

cikin dasashiyar muryar ta tafara cewa a..a nasuman komai ba sai dai su ummana dake wancan dakin..

Karki damu suma ba abinda suka musu ay tun lokacin da suka shigo nagansu toh natsaya jiran taimakon gaggawa ne shiyasa harma suka same nasarar jimawa a wannan gidan amma kuyi haquri insha allah hakan bazai koma faruwa ba kinji? 

Kanta kawai ta ita dagawa alamar taji kafin tace "babban su ma gashi aqasan gado tafada tana nuna gurin..

hannu daya wani soja yasa kadai yatadda gadon baki daya dan daman gadon bawani girmane keda shi ba.

tinkafin sukai da samasa hannu Kamal yafaray fitsare qafafuwansa wani gigitaccin mari ne sojan yabashi saida yakife aqasa nan take jini yafito abakinsa tashin hankali, dukansa suke tako ina tamakar zasu rabasa da ransa, nan take kururuwar su takarad'e gidan wanda duk shirun unguwar saida mutane suka fito, haka sojojin nan suka titsa qiyar su Kamal suka tafi dasu wanda tun nan sun musu jinajina toh bare suntafi dasu can barik dinsu na sojoji..

Kai suka hada subiyu sunata kuka yayin da umma take rungume dasu bayan ansamu ta farfado haquri da lallashi kawai kallamu ke basu, umma ce tace "daman alhaji kananan bakayi tafiyar da kace ba? ashe rabun ka taimake mune.. a gaskiya ba abinda zan iya cewa agareka sai addu'a da fatar gamawar ka lafiya, mungode sosai allah kaima yamaka abunda kamana na alkhairi yatsare zuri'arka baki daya daga sharrin mutanin duniya..

Amsawa da amin kawai kallamu keyi yanajin dadin wannan addu'a ta wannan matar qwarai suke basa tausayi shi yasa koda saif yace lallai yakoma har yashirya sai kuma yaji baya iyawa ashe gara da bai tafi ba.. 

security's dake gadin gidan da yake a qasar India ba qaramin mamaki ne sukayi ba da suka gansa yafito sai faman zubawa kansa ruwa yake tamakar wanda yazare jinsa yake tamakar yarufe ido yagansa a Nigeria kuma a kaduna acikin gidan da suke.. 

tin sanda kallamu yakirasa yake sanar dashi abinda ke faruwa bai koma samun natsuwa ba, kai da kowowa yake ta famanyi aransa yake ta nanata wannan kalma cewa "indai allah yakaishi gobe da rai da lafiya dole ne yabar qasar koda baigama abinda ya kawosa ba, dan yafi sonta da komai nasa da abunda duk ya mallaka ayanzu dana kuma nan gaba, dan haka bazaiyi wasa da abinda yazamo komai nasa ba.. 

baisan iya hukunci da zaiyiwa wa'annan mutane ba, haha kuma baisan iya matakin da zai dauka ga duk wanda yasasu ba, sai yanzu wani tunani yazo ransa tabbas ko wutar da sukayi yana kokwanto akanta, toh me ake nufi akan su ne? 

Koma meye zai sani tinda su wa'annan suna hannu yanzu.. 


Lokaci yayi da zai maida safeena cikakkiyar mace mai 'yanci da itada ummanta da qanwar ta, yanzu ne yadace yajiyar dasu dadin rayuwa ya kawar da quncin dake cikin zukan su, zasu fito kamar kowa wanda duk wasu iyalan wani mai kudi suke fantamawa agare kafin ya nunawa duniya matsayin safeena agurinsa, insha allah komai yazo qarshe dan gaba daya yanzu zasu koma rayuwar su acikin dai daga cikin manyan gidajinsa dake cikin garin abuja, har sai jin dadi yagama bin dukkan jikinsu sannan zai fitar dasu asansu yanda Duniya tayi kadan ta rainasu.. da wannan quduri yaji yaqara matsuwa akan gobe tayi. dan nan da kwana biyu yakeson qudurinsa yafara ayki, qarfin gwaiwa yaji sannan fa ya koma part dinsa dan gabatar da sallah nafila dan nuna godiyar sa akan tsare masa rayuwar sa da yayi, 

Abinda zaiwa kallamu shi kansa bai sansa ba, amma zai faranta masa kamar yanda yafaranta masa...

                       *(47)*



             *WASHE GARI*


Tin misalin qarfe 10:11am, agogon qasar India wanda yayi dai-dai da biyar dinmu na assuba anan gida Nigeria, su Saif suka taso, wanda kejin tamkar ya kar6i jirgin ya tuqa da kansa dan ganin yake tamkar jirgin baya tafiya..


Su safeena kuwa saida aka bar musu kusan sojoji biyar wa'anda zasuyi tsaronsu kafin safiya ta waye, jin dadin su daya ne da Alhaji, cewa da kallamu dan haka suke kiransa, dazai tafi yabasu tabbacin da gobe idan zai tafi can gurin iyalanshi zaitafi dasu saboda yaga nan rayuwar su nacikin hatsari. 

ganin babu inda zasu dafa kuma sun tsorata matuqa musamman umma shi yasa basu wani jaba suka aminci akan zasu bishi dan sunga alamar da wuya Alhaji yacutar dasu.. 


_(kuskurin da mutane keye kenan saurin yarda da mutun saboda yanayinsa, sai dai kuma mugu baida kama a fuska azuciya ne kadai za'a iya ganesa, sai dai kuma zuciya ma6uyar sirri ce Allah kadai ne yasan abinda ke cikin ta sai kuma mamallakin ta, sai dai muce Allah kada ya hadamu da miyagun mutane ka tsiratar damu daga sharrine su, Allah kuma yasa mudace ameeeen)_


tinda sassafe suka gama shirunsu daman ba wani abune keda suba, shiyasa koda kallamu yazo basu wani 6ata masa lokaci ba suka hau hanyar tafiya dan ko brk basuyi ba sai acikin mota, saboda son cika umarnin mai gidansa da yakirasa yanzu akan ko yayane yanason ganin feena...


Hanyar da zasu dauke sutafi Abuja suka hadu da manyan motocin Saif sunyi parking a gefen wani gidan mai kamar za'a sanya musu mai, shima kallamu parking yayi tare da cewa su umma suyi haquri zai siya mai ne.. 

glass din dake gefen safeena ya saukar wanda yayi dai-dai da gurin da motor Saif take, tsaf yake qare mata kallo wanda duk yaga ta wani fada da alama sosai take jin jiki, yaso ya saukar da nasa glass din amma bayason tagansa dan kartayi zargin wani abu, sai da motocin Saif suka daga ne sannan kallamu shima yaja tasa motar wai har ansa masa mai, bayan ba inda yaje, tinanine days ya tsaya masa arai na lallai wa'annan bayin Allah ba qaramin abu suke da yalla6ai nasa ba, saboda duba da kulawar da yake basu da wacce kuma yake shirin basu yanzu, amma koma meye wataran zasuji..

sosai yagaji saboda tafiyar da yayi dan ta Abuja ya sauka ga zaman jirgi kuma ga zaman mota, shiyasa koda suka iso wanka kawai yayi yakwanta nan take bacci ya dauke sa.

bayan su safeena sun isa cikin garin abuja wanda sunsha kallo kam kafin su qaraso suna mamakin tsaruwar garin da ni'emar sa gwanin sha'awa, gida da kallamu ke sauka ne idan yazo garin anan ya sauke su tare da cemusu suyi wanka su kintsa yanzu zaije yakawo musu abinci, idan sunci sun huta zai kaisu masaukin su.

addu'a suka shiga jera masa da godiya dukansu, suna masa fatan alkhairi musamman ma umma dik tafisu masa godiya.. 

har ga Allah kallamu na matuqar jin dadin yanda su safeena kemasa adduo'e da fatan alkhairi bai ta6a jin dadin aykin da ogansa yake sasaba erin na wannan lokacin, shima har aransu yake tausaya musu

bayan sunci abinci mai rai da lafiya, kusan kaza daya Deja keneman cinyewa sai da safeena tazaro ido tare da cewa waiko sis lpy kike Kuwait?

Deja da takejin numfashin ta nafita sama+2 saboda qoshin da tayi tace "adda kenan banni naci iya yanda nakeso andade ba'a hadu ba, abincin gashi gwanin dadi tamakr na aljanna ay dole na wuce cikina, takai qarshin maganar ta tana kai qafar kaza baki, sosai tabasu dariya har umma din, basu koma cemata komai ba suka saka mata edo dan sunsan komai cinta da hadamar ta, bazata iya cinye wannan abincin ba... 

Suna gama cin abinci nan gurin suka 6ingire sai bacci bama kamar Dija wacce da qyar take numfashi..


Saif shi abai farka daga bacci da yake ba sai 2:03pm, sannan yayi wanka yayi harama zuwa masallacin dake cikin gidansa..

bayan yagamane yadawo ya kintsa tare da zama kan dinning yaci abinci kadan kawai yaci yatashi dan yamatsu yaje yaga mamyn sa, dik da fushi take dashi amma a hakan zai tafi..




Safeena ce a zaune tanata faman yiwa Deja dariya tanayi tana Kallon ta, erin yanda tayi wani kwanciya tana numfashi kuma a hakan wai bacci take, dariyar tace ta tadasu duka suka tashi itama umma ganin dariyar da safeena ne keyi sosai shine yabata dariya Deja kuwa ganin da ita ake yasa tasha mur, ta daure sosai daman gata da zuciya, abu kadan ya hasalata, jin tayi cikin ta yana wani erin juyawa alamar abinda ke ciki yanason fitowa, ay da gudu ta nufe hanyar toilet, safeena qara fashewa tayi da dariya harda hawaye Deja najita ba damar magana sai aykin gabata kawai takeyi, jin sukayi sallama kallamu wanda suke kira da Alhaji yazo tafiya dasu kenan..


Durqushe yake gwaiwa biyu agaban mamyn sa, yana gaida ita, ba yabo ba fallasa take amsa masa kafin tace "ay nadauka daka dawo bazaka zo ba, dan daman kasan ajiraye nake dakai.. 

Ina Haneefar take ne? 

Fuska yayi erin ta nuna rashin inda maganar ta yadosa yayi cikin taushin murya yace "mamy wacece hakan kuma? 

Kutt"mamy tazaro ido cikin daga murya tace "wai yaushe nafara wasa da kaine Saif da har raini keson shiga tsakanina dakai, ita matar yaka da kuke kwana atare kuke tashi atare zan tambaye ka zakace man wacece haka, saboda ka rainani ko? 

sai a yanzu ya canke inda zancin nata yadosa shi harga allah kwata-kwata ya manta da yanada mata a rayuwar sa, kuma shi sam baisan hakane sunan ta ba.

gudun kada mamy ta dagoshi ne yadan sosa kansa "yace kiyi haquri mamy kinsan sunan ba riqesa nake ba saboda bada sunan nake kiranta ba shi yasa amma kiyi haquri idan hakan ya6ata miki... 

Murmushi mamy tayi cikin jin dadi na maganar da Saif yayi waton ma har wani suna yawa haneefa na musamman wanda shike kiranta dashi, lallai ga dukkan alama Saif yafara son Haneefa kenan, daman ita tasan qarya namiji dik izzarsa da nuna qin aure da wacce za'a aura masa sai idan har ba'a kai masa ita gidan sa ba, tuni zai watsar da komai  tohh yanzu dai ga zahiri tagani..

Sallama sukaji anyi anshigo Palo Haneefa ce mai shigowa wacce tasha ado tamkar zataje gurin gasar kyau, da gudun ta tayo kan mamy tana jin dadin ganin ta... 

Jin da yayi mamy tace Haneefa daman ashe tare kuke yaceman shikadai yazo kenan gurin su Nadiya kika fara leqawa kafin kishigo nan..

Cikin rashin fahimta Haneefa take saurarin mamy kafin takai aya.

sosai Haneefa ta kafeshi da ido tamkar bata ta6a  ganinsa ba, yamasifar mata kyau fiye da qima inama Saif zai ajiye girman kansa yabata lokaci qalilan dan ta nuna masa erin tsantsar Son da take masa, amma ina tasan hakan bamai yuwa bane amma insha allah koda qarfin tsiya sai taciyo kansa ya saurare ta... 

Dik maganar da mamy kewa Haneefa hankalin ta na gurinsa, ita kanta mamy kunyar hakan taji wanda ma take kallo kansa na aqasa yana dannan wayarsa..

kamar antsikare shi yatashi yana fadin mamy bari naje na gaida daddy kafin na wuce.. 

ok"tom amma kaje yana falo qasa dan Sanam baijima da zuwa gurinsa ba, suna ma tare yanzu haka.. 

sunan yayi ta nanatawa na Sanam aransa tamkar lokacin yasan sunan.. 

tohh meya kawo sanam gurin daddy adai dai wannan lokacin kuwa?

                          *(48)*

A Bakin wani danqarerin gida ne su Safeena sukaga anyi parking ana jiran mai gadi yabude get basu wani jira ba da sauri aka bude musu gidan, saida numfashin su dukansu ya dauke, nan take tsoro da firgici ya ziyarce su, sai yanzu ko wani ke saqawa aransa allah dai kasa basun kawo kansu bane inda za'a saida su...


sam basuma san anyi parking din motar da suke ciki ba sai da dukaji anbude musu qofa ana cewa sufito ankawo, sannan fa suka fara fitowa duka ko wannesu jikinsa asanyaye..

Ajiyar xuciya suka sauke lokacin da sukaga wasu mata guda 3 suna nufosu da qarasowar su, suka rusana suna gaida su, mamaki ne kuma yanzu yakamasu, jin sukayi alhajin nacewa matan nan kunga su nan sunzo wa'anda aka muku bayani akansu, karku manta da gargadin yalla6ai da yake cewa koda kadan ne kuka 6ata musu toh abakin aykin ku, ina fatar baku manta ba? 

Atare sukace bamu manta ba ranka yadade kuma insha allah dik abinda suke so ko sukace shine za'ayi. 

Gud"kallamu yafada cikin gamsuwa da zancin nasu kafin yadube umman yace "Hajiya nasan zakuyi mamaki matuqa ganin yanda nake ta hidima daku bayan baku sanni ba, nima haka, tohhh azahirin gaskiya bani duk kemuku wannan hidimar ba, wannan aykin mai gidanane shine yasakani yimuku komai da duk abinda kuke gani, hakan halayyar tasa take bakuka dai ne yakewa hakan ba, shi mai taimako ne feye da abinda kukaga yana muku to yakeyi ga duk wanda ke neman taimako, dan haka nakeson kada koyi tunanin komai ko kuzarge wani abu. 

Sannan ya hana da kowa yasansa acewar sa duk abinda yake saboda allah yake yinsa, shidai kawai abinda yakeso bai wuce addu'a ba.. 

Wa'annan da kuke gani sune ma'aikatan nan gidan da zaku zauna, wannan yanuna wata da takusan yin sa'a da umma yace itace mai muku abinci wa'annan kuma wa'anda da kadan ne suka dara su safeena a shekaru, dayar mai gyara muku ko inace anan gidan dayar kuma mai wanke-wanke ce da sauran ayyukan gida..

Sukuma wa'annan yanuna wasu maza su ukku, wannan shine mai gadi sunansa idi, wannan kuma Sani shine driver ku duk inda kukeson zuwa ku kirasa wannan kuma Nazir shine mai wanke da guga, insha allah ba abinda zaku nema ko zakuyi anan gidan... 

sannan kuma akwai kudi gurin Sani driver idan allah yakaimu ko zuwa gobe ne saiku shirya zai kaiku kusiyo duk abinda kuke so, daga abinda yakai sutura kayan shafawa dadai sauransu duk saiku siyo, ni yanzu insha allah gobe zan koma saboda yalla6ai dake nemana, kuma za'a kawo muku masu tsaro na musamman da zasu dinga kula daku da kuma dik inda zakuje karkuji komai.... 


saboda dadi umma da Deja fashewa sukayi da kuka, umma harda su sujada da allah, wannan wani erin mai tausayi ne? Lokaci guda yamaidasu 'yan gata lalali wannan zaisha addu'a da yarda allah kamar yanda yakeso... 

Ciki suka shiga nanfa tunaninsu yafara tsinkewa ganin erin daular dake cikin gidan, Wa'anna masu aykin ne mata suka qara gabatar da kansu gurin su safeena tare da sunayin su. 

Uwani mai wanke-wanke sai tabawa mai yimusu abinci sai zali mai kula da tsaftar guri, duniya yanzu takomawa su safeena sabuwa Saif yacika burinsa na farko akanta, wannan Kenan....



Koda aka budewa Saif qofa ya shiga Sanam yana batun fitowa suna sallama da Daddy, wani murmushi ne Sanam ya sakarwa saif tare da cewa "a..a ango na amarya shikenan kuma sai ka6uya ko? 

aka daina ganin ka wai ina kashiga ne? 

Banza Saif yayi da Sanam haka kawai yanzu yakejin haushin sanam baisan abinda yasa ba, kodan yaqushe masa safeena ne ya nuna shima baya ra'ayin ta, toh shidai bai san abinda yasa ba.. 

Har qasa yaduqa yagaida daddy, dik da Daddy yasan halin Saif ne haka, amma sam baiji dadin abinda yafaru yanzu ba, na yanda Saif yayiwa sanam, amma bayason ya nuna dan kada yasa musu abinda bashi bane azukatan su, tinda bamai shiga tsakanin su, mai yuwa ma wata rigimar ce tahadasu ta daban wacce bata taka kara takarya ba, ko kuma yau miskilancin Saif ne ya motsa akan Sanam din.. 

Sai da Saif yakoma gaida daddy sannan ya amsah, Sanam kuwa fecewar sa, yayi tinda daman sungama maganar da Daddy kuma sunyi sallama, aransa yake yiwa Saif dariyar mugunta dan ganin yagama da duk wani abu nasa da yake taqama dashi, yanzu abu daya ne yarage shine zaije har can kaduna gidansu safeena dan yagaya mata abinda ke tafe dashi kamar yanda daddy ya tsara masa, kuma yasan insha allah bazata ma qisa ba, dan ko dama Sakna zaije ta yanda zataji nauyin qin amsar buqatar sa, dik dama yasan haka da wuya yaqi yuwa saboda uwa uba yanada kudi...


Bayan Haneefa tadawo gida ne duk abin duniya ya dameta, Son mijinta yaninku feye da takejinshi ada, ganin da tayi mishi yau yaqara sanya qaunar sa aranta, duniya ba abunda takeso ayanzu illah takasance da saif ko ta halin qaqa.

ga number Kamal kullum duk takira akase take, batasan me yasa ba, kardai yaci amanar ta yagudu, adai dai lokacin da take buqatar taimakon sa, so take kota yaya a hallakar da rayuwar safeena a doron qasa dama duk wacce take da kusanci atare da ita, Hawaye ne kebin fuskar Haneefa daya bayan daya, tarasa Duniya abinda ke mata dadi, allah yasani wllh tanason mijinta, meyasa saif yaqi kula tane bare har yasota me tanema tarasa dik abinda akeso ga mace allah yabata tohh me saif ne yagani yakeson waccan figagiyar yarinya, lallai dole ne tasake shiri sosai tinda taneme kamal tarasa...


                  *3weeks letar*


Abakin wani rantsatsin Mall ne akayi parking da wasu hadaddun motoci guda biyu da sauri securitys din cikin motar daya suka fitu su biyu, suka nufe mota daya tare da bude qofar motar, kafufuwan Safeena ne suka fara sauka a hankali kafin ita kanta ta bayyana da yawa mazan dake gurin wa'anda parking dinsu kenan da wa'anda zasu fito daga cikin Mall din dama wa'anda ke tsaye gurin, Wow! Wow!! kawai kakeji ana fada, wasu ko suce masha allah, hatta da matan da suke gurin saida safeena taburge su, domin tayi kyau harta gaji bawani dadewa tayi cikin hutu ba amma saboda daman kala safeena ta hutuce shiyasa nan take jin dadi yaqara fitowa da asalin kala ta, wanda baisan sirrin ba zaima dauka ba a Nigeria tayi rayuwa ba, wata lafiyayar lifaya ce mai matuqar kyau da tsadace tayi sari da ita, komai najikin ta color kayanta, wayyo mai karatu inama inada yanda zan dauko muku photo ta danayi hakan dan nahutar daku dogon nazari, saboda tinanin ku kokadan bazai iya baku yanda safeena ta chanja lokaci daya ba.. 

Deja ma babaya ba ta chanja tayi kyau cikin kyakyawar atanfar ta color red & yellow sai tasanya komai color kayanta, sun matuqar tafiya da tunanin mutane wanda har yasa suka shiga ciki wasu basuko ankara ba, 

_Niko nace inama Saif na gurin wannan kallon haka_

Acikin matan dake gurin wata farar mace wacce fitowar ta kenan daga cikin motar ta, idonta yasauka akan safeena, tinanin take inda ta ta6a ganin ta, Ohhhhhh matar tafada cikin wani murya, sai yanzu natina inda nasan wannan fuskar kamar itace yarinyar da sukaje kamun qawarta Haneefa har akasamu matsala akanta, amma bazata tabbatar ba harsai takira sunanta tagani matuqar ko ta amsa toh tabbas itace, kuma wllh idan hakane sai ta sanarwa qawar ta taga yarinyar da akayi fada domin ta gurin kamun ta, kuma kamar harda securities din Saif tagani atare dasu wanda su ba 6oyayyu bane ako ina... 

da hanzarin ta kuwa ta cusa kanta ciki dan qara tabbatar da zargin ta... 

Kaya suke dauka dai bayan daya Deja jinta take tamakar 'yar wani Shugaba qasa daman dija ba baya ba, akwai son qarya kuma akwai jin kai sai dai aji ne bata sosai wannan sai safeena... 

Safeena tasa hannuta kenan ta dauke wani kwalban mai taji ankira sunan ta, ta bayan ta, wanda hakan yabata mamaki dan tasan ba wanda ya sansu haka kuma suma ba wanda suka sani..

kallon rashin sani takewa matar amma bata mata magana ba, sai itace tace "kinyi mamaki dana san sunanki ko daman nima canka ne dan kinman kama da wacce nasani ashe ba ita bace, kenan kema hakane sunanki? 

Numfasawa kawai safeena tayi tawuce tabarta atsaye dan haka kawai taji matar bata kwant mata ba.. 


wani shu'umin murmushi ne matar tasaki kafin tace lallai wannan jarida mai zafi ce, dan haka Haneefa tashirya sayen ta da daraja, dan tinda taga securities din Saif atare dasu toh ko Haqiqa Saif bai rabuda yarinyar ba... 

Juyawa ma tayi tashiga motar ta tabar gurin Allah-Allah take ta isa gida....

                       *(49)*



K'arasowa Deja tayi gurin safeena tare da cemata "adda wannan kuwa wacece Halan?

Nima bansata ba haka kawai naji takira sunana amma tace bani bace wacce take nufi ba, safeena tabawa Deja amsa kai tsaye kafin tawuce ta taci gaba da duduba abunda take buqatar siya.

Da haka har suka gama kammala duk abinda suke buqatar siya suka fito, nan fa ko yanzu ido yakoma komawa kansu, su dija sarkin  jin kai harda qara chanja wata tafiya take tamkar wacce batason taka qasa.. 

Haneefa zaune take zata fara cin abinci wanda mai aykinta takawo mata shi yanzu, sai taji wayar ta tafara ringing alamar kirane yashigo, idonta takai kan wayar tare da dubawa, wani murmushi ne haneefa tasaki naganin mai kiranta. 

picking tayi tare da cewa "wllh Naja bakida kirki sai yanzu kikaga damar kiran nawa?

wacce aka kira da Naja dariya kawai tayi, kafin tace "dukanmu ay ba kirkin kedamu ba, kuma ayni daman bari nayi kigama cin amarcin ki tukunna sannan nakiraki.. 

Annurin dake dauke a fuska Haneefa daukawa yayi lokaci daya najin abinda naja tafada amma da yake batason asan sirrin ta tsakanin ta da mijin ta yasa batace komai ba, illa cewa da tayi lallai kam kin kuma kyauta hakan da kikayi, tohhh yanzu meye labari 2days? 

Anzo gurin Naja tafad'a aranta, tana mai qara gyra murya tace "niko nake da labari Haneefa, amma kuma fa sai kinyi hanquri da abinda zakiji.. 

cikin yanayi rashin gane inda zancin Naja yadosa Haneefa tace "kamar ya kenan?

numfasawa Naja tayi tana mai cewa "saboda nasan cewa maganar da zan fada miki sai kinyi haquri, maganar kuwa itace "kinko san cewa yau da marecin nan naga yarinyar nan wacce mijinki saif yaje da ita ranar kamu ku, anan cikin garin Abuja cikin wani qayataccin Mall, Nan dai Naja ta kwashe komai tafad'awa haneefa hatta da securities din saif da yanda kuma taga safeena taqara chanjawa kamar ba ita ba, da kuma kiran da tawa safeena dan qara tabbatar da itace ko ba ita bace duk takwashe tafadawa Haneefa.

wacce zufa da tsantsar tashin hankali suka ziyarce ta lokaci daya kayan abincin dake gabata ne dik takama watse dasu, wanda sai da mai aykinta tafito kiching afirgice hannuta a dafe da gaba..

kuka Haneefa take tayi tana fashe duk kayan da suke kusa da ita, tsabar baqin ciki ko abinda zata cewa naja rasa samu tayi, sai kawai takashe wayar tare da cigaba da watse da take da kaya.

wanda da qyar mai aykinta ta samu taciyo kanta, dik ta jiwa kanta ciyo, amma sam baqin cikin dake cin zuciyar ta, yafi komai yimata ciyo jin ta take tamkar ta bude idonta ta ganta a Abuja take ji, dan haka tayanke shawara zataje tagayawa mamy komai, sannan kuma tasa Naja tayo musu bincikin inda gidansu yake, suje dan har mamy tasan bazata yarda abarta ba, wllh sai sun musu wulaqanci naqarshe wanda sai sungwammace rashin rayuwar su da zaman su araye..

Sai da zuciyar Haneefa tadanyi sanyi ne sannan ta lalube No naja bugu daya biyu ta dauka tare da cewa "abunda kikeji ya sauka ne?

WASHE GARI 

Koda saif yajewa mahifinsa da maganar auren sa, yayi matuqar jin dadi koba komai yasan yarinyar da yakeso baqaramin nasara tasamu na shiga zuciyar dan nasa ba, dan ko lokacin da yake tausar saif akan yayi haquri yarungume aure da mahaifiyar sa keson yayi, danshi amatsayin sa na mahaifinsa bazaiso yashige masa gaba dan yaqi bin umarnin mahaifiyar sa ba, tinda ba'aga wata matsala da zaisa ace mahaifin haneefa ba za'a so hada jininka da nasa ba, wannan kam da ansamu kuskurin hakan tom bazai bare ayi auren ba..

Koda daddy ke tausar saif akan indai yayarda akayi wannan auren toh ko daga baya yaga wacce yakeso karya ji komai gurin zuwa fadar abinda ke ransa, da haka yasamu saif yadan sake ransa... 

toh ko yanzu daddy cewa yayi yaje gurin mahaifinsa, Alhaji baba yasanar dashi domin shine wanda yadace yafara tunkara da wannan maganar kafin shi.. 

cike da gamsuwa Saif yake saurarin mahaifin nasa, sai da duk suka kammala fadar abunda duk yakamata sannan saif yayi mishi sallam da cewa yanzu daganan can gidan Alhaji baba zai tafi...

Addu'ar sauka lapiya da samun nasara daddy yamasa, sannan yafita.

daga haka saif yasa kai yaficewar sa, sam bai damu ko kula da yanayin da yabar dan uwan sa kuma aminin sa aciki ba...

"Insha Allah, sukace kusan atare

Shima Alhaji baba bai wani ja ba yayi na'am da hakan tare da yiwa saif alqawarin shegewa gaba gurin ganin wannan auren yayuwo, Saif bai tashi gurin ba har sai da yakira wayar mallam yayan mahaifin safeena suka gaisa da Alhaji baba cikin mutunci da ganin girman kawunan su, nan dai  Alhaji baba yasanar da mallam cewa insha Allah cikin satin nan za'axo dumin nemawa saif aure, 

Ba qaramin dadi da qara ganin mutuncin saif malam yaqara yiba, gashi duk wani bincike yasa anyishi akan saif kasancewar ba 6oyayye bane amma sam baiji wani kalami na 6atanci yabiyu bayan sa ba, sai dai alkhairin sa da mutane ke yawan fada, dan haka shi ko bayan ransa yabawa Saif auren safeena, 

Haka sukayi sallama Alhaji baba shima yaji dadin haka kuma yaqara tabbatar da Saif ba 'yar qananin mutane bace yadauko musu dan haka yayarda da wannan aure 💯/💯

.
Koda suka fito saif da sanam ba wata firace mai tsaye ta shiga tsakanin su ba, illah sunyi sallama akan sanam sai yazo dan suqara gudanar da tsare tsarin su na bikin, da gangan saif yaqi sanarwa sanam wacce zai aura dan tsaf zasu iya samun matsala matuqar yaji ko wacece zai iya qusheta da auren ma baki daya shiyasa tambayar duniya yayi masa akan wannan wace mai sa'a ce tasameshi haka, har yaga yanzu yana rawar jiki da wannan aure?

              *2weeks later*


Tohhhfa Komai ya kankama dumin har kudin auren safeena ankai komai dai anyishi gwanin sha'awa amma tun lokacin da saif yabar Abuja bai koma mata ba har yau sai dai suna waya sosai da umma dan ko bata kirasa ba, shi zai kirata, sannan da zaran yaji zata dauko mishi maganar safeena sai yakawo mata wani uzuri yakashe waya. 

Ita kuma umma ganin take kamar kunyar ta yake ji ne shiyasa yake haka, toh dan haka sai tadaina mishi maganar safeena baki daya..



Aunty rabi tamkar an gafarta mata takeji lokacin da aunty meena takira ta akan saif yabata kwangilar hado masa lefe naji da fadi, addu'a kam sun shata ba iyaka wannan karon ma tana india, amma tace dagan zata wuce Dubai, godiya take ta yiwa aunty meena har aunty meena tagaji ta tsinke wayar ta...

Mamy kuwa ba abunda yadameta da wannan auren domin aunty sadiya tabata shawara akan tabar nuna komai idone Kawai nata tinda har ita haneefa ta buqace hakan, sosai haneefa kebawa aunty sadiya haushi akan abunda tayi, baqin cikin hakan ma yasa zata wuce umara dan ko labarin bikin bataso dan haka tasan kafin tadawo angama dan bazata yarda taje wannan qaddararin auren na matsaiyata...

Nafeesat kuwa komai da ita ake tama koma gurin aunty meena gaba daya, 

Sakna na bata nan tana makaranta shiyasa batada labarin komai, tana dai mamaki duk takira number safeena bata shiga har tagaji tadaina kira, amma taqudira aranta idan tadawo Nigeria zataje har can kaduna tadibo ko lafiya? 


A safiyar yau su Zalifa suka yiwa Abuja dira ta musamman farin cikin da ake ciki yau kam babu misali, umma tin kafin aje ko ina bakinta yafara ciyo gaba daya gidan sungama tadashi, tohko safeena ba baya ba, dan kawai batada wanda zatayi dashi ne, sai dija, ita kuma ganin take rainata zatayi shiyasa bawai erin sake mata take ba. tohhh yau ga zalifa ga jidda, najwa da ikram kuma qanneta ne sa'anni dija ne shiyasa bata wani biye musu sai ko idan suna tsokanar ta sannan zata fara musu kashedin sufa ba tsarar ta bane... 


  Yau saura Sati biyu asa safeena a lalle, su aunty meena sunzo har Abuja itada qawayin ta da nafeesat gurin kawo kaya, kayanda bazan iya lissafa muku adadin su da tsadarsu had'e da haduwarsu ba, wannan nabar muku gwalagwalai kam abin sai kallo tare da Kujerar umara biyu, ta hajji biyu, umma sai da abin yaso bata tsoro matuqa amma su zaleefa da yake sunsan komai abin baiwani ta6asu ba, kai kayankan sunyi kyau sai san barka... 


Su aunty meena sunci sunsha sunyi qat bayan sun futa sukayi sallolinsu suka fara shirin tafiya dan jirgin qarfe 6:00pm zasu hau sukoma gida.... 


Yanzu damuwar safeena dayace, Saif tana matuqar son ganinsa da sunjin muryar sa, amma bazata iya kiransa ba, duk da ay dukansu sunada laifi dan yimata yayi ita kuma tarama, amma da zaiyo zata iya bashi haquri koba komai yatallafe rayuwarsu asanda suke neman taimakon gaggawa lokacin da kowa yaguje su, tom amma tana son kiransa amma tana gudun wulaqancin sa, Kawai zata daure....

Zaleefa riqi baki tayi kafin tace "lallai ma wllh kince wani abu, meye tom abun munafunci anan?

Jidda ce tace "dan allah qyaleta sis anyi munafurcin din ay abun munafurci akayi shiyasa har mukayi din, anaso ana kaiwa kasuwa dan munafunci aykece dashi... 

sororo safeena ke kallon su dan ita tana mamakin wannan abun adakeka a hanamaka kuka, zatayi maganin su ne jin tayi bata iya saurara su tatashi tafitar musu daga dakin baki daya, amma sai da suka biyota har parlo sukaci gaba da mata tsiya.. 


Oyoyo Oyoyo Haneefa ce kewa wata matashiyar budurwa oyoyo wacce kallo daya zakayiwa fuskarta kagane cewa fitinanniyar yarinya ce ta bugawa ajarida, daka ganta zakaga ta maka Kama da Haneefa sai dai haskin da Haneefa ta fita da kuma jiki 

Nasiba kenan qanwace ga haneefa wacce take bimata fitinanniyar yarinya ce kuma masifaffiya, bata daukar raini kokadan ko fada takeyi da mutun bata jiran komai take gaggawar daukeshi da mari sai dai duk abinda zai faru yafaru bata shakkar kowa haka bata gudun komai.

Koda haneefa ke mata oyoyo fuskarta atamke take saboda haushin abinda haneefa tayi dan wllh da tananan da hakan bazai ta6a yuwa ba ayanda takejin zuciyar ta tinanin take tamafi Haneefa baqin ciki da kishin wannan auren da za'ayi shiyasa koda Haneefa ta roqa tazo bata wanijaba dik da tarin abubuwan dake gabanta ta ajiyesu gefe tazo tarewa gidan haneefa dan ganin wace erin marar kunyace zatayi gigin kishi da yayar ta? 


Umma ce zaune kan sallaya yayin da safeena take jera mata gugar tufafin ta, da aka kawo mata daga gurin wanki.

sunan ta umma takira, a hankali safeena tadago kanta tare da amsawa takashe kunne tana saurarin abunda umman ta zata fada mata.. 

"kinji saqon yaron nan dan albarka?

Yace asanar dani baya buqatar komai daga garemu yace ko tsinke bayaso dan ko menene yariga da yasa anyo order dinsu aqasar waje dan haka yafutar damu.

sai kuma magana ta biyu data tsayaman arai wacce da kansa yakirani yaroqe ni alfarma akan nayarda dija takoma gidanku da zama Saboda girman gidan zaki dinga jinsa tsit tindashi ba zama yake ba, sannan kuma yasan koda masu ayki aka samomiki hazakiji dadin zama dasu ba erin 'yar uwar ki, tohm ni bakomai nakewa ba illah halin dija nake tsoro kinsan halinta da shiga abinda ko bai shafeta ba, nina haife dija amma ina tsoron rigima irin tata, banaso taje can gaki da abokiyar zama adinga samun matsala sam bazanso hakan ba, kuma narantse aduniyar nan babu abinda yaron nan zainema da bazan kasa masa Shiba, tohh shine abun ke damuna saboda bana iya cemasa a..a dama bashi bane da bazan wani 6ata lokaci ba zance bazaiyu ba.. 

Numfasawa safeena tayi yayin da cikin ranta fari qal saboda daman tana jinjina ranar da zata tafi tabar 'yar uwar ta, ga kuma fargama zaman kadaicin da take tunanin zatayi, amma inta gefen haneefa ne matarsa bata da Matsala da ita dan bata tsoronta kokadan tinda daman sun saba... 

cikin lalashi da nuna wannan ba komai bane da bawa umma tabbacin ba abinda zai faru safeena tasamu ta ciyo kan umma har ta amince, da haka suka dinga fira sama-sama har safeena ta kammala aykin da take tatashi tafita


Yau takama saura sati daya asa amarya a lalle tin 'yan uwan umma suka fara zuwa saboda yanzu anga tayi gaba sai rawar jiki ake yanzu 'yan uwan ta nayita tinda Allah ya daukaka ta shi yasa koda tasamu bata qisu ba ta dinga janyosu ajikin ta.

dik wanda yazo Abuja gidan umma sai jikinsa yamutu dan komai tunanin da kake bazaka ta6a daukakar da allah yamata yakai hakan ba..

Har masu ayki saif yasa aka qaro mata dan hidimar tayiwa masu aykin gidan yawa itadai umma kullum cikin addu'a take yiwa saif saboda yagamama ta komai arayuwa bata buqatar komai sai fatan gamawa da Duniya lpy, sai dai wani lokacin edan ta tuna yaranta salim da salima takanyi kuka sosai tana tsananin kewarsu gashi ko tokarsu ba agani ba, shike qara tadawa umma hankali, amma ya ta iya da qaddarowar ubangiji sai haquri..

                      ********

A hankali yake takowa har cikin palon ta, yayin da yake bin ko ina da kallo, watace ya hango akan kujira kwance tana kallo kuma wayarta tana hannun ta alamar chrt take kenan, gyaran murya yayi lokacin da ya iso tsakiyar palo atunanin sa ko haneefa ce tinda daman ba wani cikakin sani yamata ba.. 

Jin hakan ya ankarar da naseeba wacce ke kwance dago kanta tayi idon ta yasauka akans, da sauri tatashi zaune tare da masa sannu da zuwa tagaidashi amma gogan ko kallon ta baiyi ba bare ya amsa. 

Jin motse sukayi ana saukowa a hankali gurin nasiba takai kallon ta, aunty ta ce haneefa ke saukowa wacce kokadan bata lura da wanda ke cikin palo ba, har sai da taqaraso alamun mamaki da al'ajabi suka bayyana qarara a fuskarta, wani murmushi ne tasake nagefin bakin nan aranta take cewa nasan arina.. 

Kamar ba komai tagaidashi cikin girmamawa tare da mishi sannu da zuwa..

Sai ayanzu yadago da kansa daga duban wayarsa da yake, haneefa ya kallah sai kuma yamai da kallon sa gurin naseeba.. 

Kamar daga sama sukaji yace "wacece matar gidan? 

Ba haneefa ba hatta da naseeba sai da tambayar tadake ta da nuna tsantsar mamaki wanda yakasa 6oyuwa afuskar ta..

Haneefa tamkar zuciyar ta tayi bindiga taji tsabar baqin ciki, gashi shi ba erin mutanin nan bane da zasu gayama magana kasamu damar ramawa ko sanyi kaji ba, sam babu wannan damar dan qwarjinin sa yayi yawa babu wasa ko wani alama dazai baka damar yimasa ko wani kallon raine bare har kagaya masa magana..

qololon baqin ciki haneefa ta shanye da qyar kafin tayi qarfin halin cewa "nice.

banza yayi da ita kamar baiji ta ba, sai da yaga dama dan kansa yafara magana kamar haka batare da yadago ba, "kiyi list din duk abinda kikeso za'a kawo ko kuma idan kudi kikesu saiki rubuta iya adadin da kikeso wannan shine abinda yakawo ni, daga haka yasakai yafice ko waiwaye bare har yatsaya yasaurare amsa daga gare ta, yana fita wani dayan security dinshi yaturo waton shi yaturo amadadin sa dan yasaurare amsar haneefa.. 

Baqin ciki yasa takore security tare da gargadin sa karya qara kuskura yashigo mata part dinta ko waye ma ya aykosa.

Kan kujera haneefa tafada tare da dafe kanta, cikin qonar rai take magana, "narantse da Allah bancin da son da nakewa bawan Allah nan dana haqura dashi ayau dinnan, saboda duk wulaqanci da yake man banta6a jin zafinsa kamar na yau ba, saif baya sona baya qaunata, kokadan bayako son ganina bansan me yasa ba, kwata kwata bai damu dani ba, hakan yau da yaqara man ya tabbatar man da kokadan baya kishina saif baiko sanni ba wanda hakan yakasa banbance tsakanina  da qanwata wacece matar gidan? 

tinda mommy ta haifeni banta6a ganin rayuwar aure mai cike da wulaqanci da tozarci had'e da banzartarsuwa erin auren dake tsakanina da Saif bah...

Wllh duk zuciyata ce tacutar dani duk itace tasanya ni natsince kaina a halin danake ciki ayanzu, ninasan dani da mai gadin gidan nan kusan daya muke agurin Saif, dan ko securities dinsa sunfini matsayi agunsa tinda su a kullum suna ganin sa... 

waine dan Allah ya zanyi da rayuwata ne? takai qarshin maganar ta tare da fashiewa da wani erin kuka mai ciyo.. 

Wanda yaqara tunzura zuciyar naseeba, haqiqa ko ita da ba saif bane wllh ko wanene yayi kadan da ya wulaqanta mata 'yar uwa ta, agaban ta sannan kuma har ace taqyalesa.

tabbbbdi lallai wannan guy yanaji da wani abu, amma bancin da haka ace ko matarka wai baka sani ba? tohh inama gani qanwar matar gidan.. 🤨

Lallai dole ne sutashi tsaye edan ba haka ba zasu fusakanci wulaqanci da yafi wannan idan har aka kawo wannan shegiyar amarya, tinda yayarta tazama kaska...


Shiry-Shiryen biki kam sun kanka ma gefen amarya, amma gefen saif kuwa iyayinshi maza ne kawai ke sha'aninsu saiko aunty meena da feesah sai Maman su sanam, saiko angon da sauran abokansa, amma saboda sananne ne yasa duniya nan take tadauka da maganar auren sa wanda wasu sunyi ta surutu akai saboda ganin baijima da yin aure ba,

tinda suka shiga gidan suka rasa dik wani tunanin su sakamakon tsinke musu da yayi, gida ne bani da gida ba, wanda basai dai kaganshi anan ba sai erin qasashin qetare wanda ko tanbaba ba'ayi mutanin can ne suka tsara gidan..
